Woman At Table:  "Nani? Watashinokao ni nanika ga arimasu ka?"

*She says as she moves her hand through her hair*

Woman At Table:  "In Japan, you have to ask a girl permission to stare at them."

*To my surprise she then picks up a straw and pokes me in the forehead with it.*
Woman At Table:  "Don't be so gullible."
You:  "Huh?"
*Oh, she was joking. I guess?*
Woman At Table:  "So, Mr...judging by your accent i'm going to say American. What are you doing here?"
You:  "Oh, yeah. I'm American. You can tell just by my accent?"
*The woman smiles smugly.*
Woman At Table:  "What can I say? I'm just that amazing."
*She pauses for a second.*
Woman At Table:  "Well, you don't seem like you're here for fun..."
*She says, eyeing over my business suit.*
Woman At Table:  "So i'm going to say you're here for a job. English teacher?"
*Once again, i'm shocked.*
You:  "Y..yeah you're right."
*She leans her hand on her chin.*
Woman At Table:  "Of course I am."
You:  "How did you ever guess that?"
*She turns her eyes back to mine and gives me a condescending look.*
Woman At Table:  "Well, if you were a big business guy, you'd be in Tokyo. For a town like this, i'd say Teacher sounds about right."
Woman At Table:  "Besides..."
*She sits up, crossing her arms under her chest as she gives me a grin, her snaggletooth resting over her lip.*
Woman At Table:  "Your lack of Japanese gives it away."
*I make an irritated noise which she catches*
*and then proceeds to raise her hand to her face and laugh behind her hand softly.*
*Is she seriously trying to piss me off? Because if she is, she's definitely getting there. Still, it's my first day. Better play it cool.*
*I thought maybe i'd try something else.*
You:  "Oh, by the way, my name is <USER>. What's yours?"
*She waves her hands dismissively.*
Woman At Table:  "I don't believe I asked for your name. So many men give me their name, I couldn't possibly give it out to all of them."
You:  "Oh, does that mean you get with a lot of men?"
*The moment I say this, I realize how badly it sounds.*
*Immediately her face turns bright red as her entire composure changes.*
*She stands up suddenly.*
Woman At Table:  "W...WHAT ARE YOU ASKING??"
*She asks this louder than she probably should as a few people turn to hear the noise.*
Woman At Table:  "Are you insinuating that i'm some kind of-"
*She leans on the table, her face now only about a foot away from mine.*
*I put up my hands defensively.*
You:  "I'M SORRY! I didn't mean it like that!"
*She must see how pale my face is and my sincerity, as after a moment, her face begins to return to normal.*
*The woman then proceeds to sit down. She crosses her arms under her chest.*
Woman At Table:  "Hmph."
*An awkward moment passes.*
*Great. First person you talk to in Japan in a real conversation and you basically ask her if she sleeps around. Great job <USER>*
*You're just making all kinds of friends.*
You:  "Look, i'm really, really sorry. I just got flustered and-"
*Her head still turned sideways to me, she turns her eye back and sees me flailing.*
*She lets out a sigh.*
Woman At Table:  "You know, normally that kind of talk would get you a drink poured on your head."
You:  "Yep, pretty much the same in America too."
*I answer, defeated.*
Woman At Table:  "I'll let it slide, just this once. If anything, I assume you meant to give me a compliment."
*Full of yourself much?*
You:  "Of course I did."
*I answer reflexively.*
*A moment of silence goes by before I feel a poke on my leg*
*I immediately turn back to the woman, who seems to have gone back to her menu.*
*I feel it again*
*Is she poking me in the leg?*
*I look at her hands, which are both preoccupied with the menu.*
*I feel like asking her 'Are you touching my leg?' But considering the fiasco that just took place,*
*I have a feeling the night might end with her ACTUALLY pouring something on my head.*
*I'll just ignore it.*
*Ignore it.*
*Come on <USER>, ignore it.*
*That's it! I can't take it!*
*I duck my head under the table.*
*What I see nearly causes me to jump up.*
*Poking me in the leg is the most adorable rabbit I've ever seen in my life.*
*As I turn my head down to it, it merely tilts its own head directly at me, with what I can only describe as a large smile on its face*
*It pokes me in the leg again, this time making a soft 'chuu!' noise*
*I try to resist the lure of the adorable bunny, but i fail miserably.*
*Without even thinking much about it, I reach down and pick up the rabbit.*
You:  "Oh my goodness! Where did you come from? You are so cute!"
*I know it's not the manliest thing ever, but I can't help it. I love rabbits.*
*I always have ever since I was little when I used to have one named Ms. Stuffykins.*
*However, the moment that I lift the rabbit up, the woman's face goes pale.*
*She looks over at something on her seat and then immediately turns back to me.*
Woman At Table:  "Hey, HEY Give me her!"
*As she says this, her tone changes drastically.*
*It goes from an in control, more adult voice to almost that of a child whose toy was taken away.*
*She frantically looks side to side to make sure no one is watching.*
*I look from her to the rabbit*
Woman At Table:  "Yes, yes! The rabbit, please give her back to me!"
*The request is even more frantic now.*
You:  "Oh, is she yours? She's adorable."
*As if the rabbit can hear me say this, it smiles at me and, I must have been imagining things, but I swear it slightly blushed.*
*The girl, now beet red, is now frantically reaching across the table to grab the rabbit from my hands.*
You:  "Oh, right. Sorry."
*I say this almost as if I were in a trance, before slowly handing the rabbit over to the woman.*
*The second I get within range she snatches forward and grabs the rabbit.*
*As soon as the rabbit is in her hands, she seems to calm down slightly.*
*She then turns to the rabbit.*
Woman At Table:  "Uso! What have I told you about staying in the bag?"
*She asks in a chastising tone.*
*I can't help but snicker at the scene.*
*The woman turns her head back to me.*
*Oh crap, I think she heard me.*
Woman At Table:  "It's not funny!"
You:  "Right! Right, sorry."
*I say holding my hands up defensively.*
*We must have caused quite a commotion as a few moments later the waiter from earlier walks by.*
*Unfortunately, the woman doesn't see him and is still holding the rabbit.*
*The waiter, looking irritated, begins talking to her sternly.*
*I can't understand the words they're saying, but it seems like he's not very happy with her bringing in the rabbit.*
*I let the sweet irony of her being in trouble soak in for a moment.*
*I'm really sorely tempted to leave her like this.*
*But stupid me, I can't say no to a person in need.*
*Quickly I pull out my Japanese phrasebook and look for the phrase i'm looking for.*
*After about ten seconds I find it, while they're still arguing.*
You:  "Uh, sumimasen!"
*I say loudly, moreso than was probably necessary.*
*This causes both of them to stop as they turn to me.*
*Embarrassed, I repeat the phrase I just looked up.*
You:  "Kore wa......watashi...no...usagi desu. gomen. (This is my rabbit. Sorry)"
*The two of them blink in unison as the waiter then turns back to the woman.*
*He says something to her in Japanese.*
*She turns her eyes back to me and then back to him before nodding once.*
Woman At Table:  "Mm."
*She says simply.*
*The waiter looks to me, lets out a sigh, and then turns back to her, saying something else.*
*Now, once again blushing, she turns back to me.*
Woman At Table:  "The waiter says, I know you probably don't know Japanese customs, but please don't bring animals into the restaurant."
You:  "Can you tell him that i'm sorry? I just came from America and she was scared from the trip and I didn't want to leave her alone."
*Tentatively, she turns back to the waiter and translates what I just said.*
*The waiter looks back at me, and nods. He says one more thing to the woman.*
Woman At Table:  "He says....it's okay, just don't do it next time."
You:  "Oh, of course! I didn't know."
*Look at me, lying my butt off. Mom would have been so proud.*
*With a look of exasperation, the man takes our orders.*
*The woman places an order in Japanese. I struggle just pointing to the item I want.*
*Upon taking our orders, the waiter leaves.*
*What follows is a long silence.*
*The woman places the rabbit back in the bag as she continues to do her best to look away from me.*
*Still looking at me sideways, she focuses one of her pupils on me.*
Woman At Table:  "I didn't ask you to do that."
*She mumbles*
*I pause for a second.*
You:  "I'm sorry?"
Woman At Table:  "I said, I didn't ask you to do that."
*She said again, this time with less mumbling.*
You:  "Um, you're welcome."
*I say incredulously.*
*She makes a 'tch' sound as she turns her head away from me again, this time, a tinge of pink on her cheeks.*
*We sit in awkward silence for a few minutes before the waiter comes back with our selection.*
*I notice however, that the woman's is wrapped up in a bag.*
Woman At Table:  "Sorry, i'm taking mine to go."
*She says in her best attempt at keeping her composure.*
*She stands up from the table.*
*Before she leaves, she hands the waiter some money and says something to him in Japanese.*
*The waiter nods and then, taking the money, walks away.*
*The woman hitches the bag containing the rabbit on her shoulder.*
*Well that's a fine way to thank someone, give them the cold shoulder and then just leave.*
*She walks past me, only to stop a few steps away, as if something inside her made her stop.*
*Still standing there, the woman says a single word.*
Woman At Table:  "Kotone."
You:  "Huh?"
*I ask, incredibly confused.*
*She sighs lightly.*
Woman At Table:  "You asked my name stupid. It's... It's Kotone."
*And, before I can even say anything else, the woman who just labeled herself as 'Kotone' exits the cafe for real.*
*As soon as this sinks in, I can't help but smirk as I pick up my cup of coffee and take a small sip.*
*After about twenty minutes I finish my light snack and coffee.*
*Once i'm done I try to get the waiter's attention.*
*I silently raise my hand at first, but that doesn't seem to work.*
*What am I doing wrong?*
*Looking around the room I see several other people doing the same, but also adding a loud 'Sumimasen!'*
*Well, when in Rome, er....Japan.*
*I raise my hand tentatively*
You:  "Sumimasen!"
*I say loudly.*
*A few moments later the waiter returns.*
Waiter:  "Owarimasuka?"
You:  "um....hai?"
*I say extremely unsurely.*
*The waiter nods and then proceeds to take my plate.*
*Slowly I reach into my pocket for my wallet. Before I can however, he puts his free hand out towards me.*
Waiter:  "Daijoubu desu, Kanojo wa shiharaimashita"
*I once again try to reach for my wallet, confused.*
*He puts his hand out again. He then gestures to the door.*
Waiter:  "On'na."
*He says again.*
*I tilt my head in confusion as I quickly pull out my japanese phrasebook.*
*I flip to the word 'On'na.'*
*Apparently it translates to 'woman.'*
*Suddenly it clicks.*
*Oh my god. He's saying that she paid for me.*
*I can't help but feel a bit of warmth go through me.*
*Huh...maybe she isn't all bad after all.*

You:  "Ah...W..wakarimasu."
*Hearing this, the waiter smiles and bows to me.*
*I give a slight bow back.*
*Gathering my things I stand up and walk towards the front door.*
*I exhale as I step out into the street.*
*I try to get my bearings and remember the way I came.*
*And it works out exactly as well as I thought it would.*
*Luckily I'm able to hail a taxi and show him the address I copied down.*
*Within about a minute i'm back to my hotel*
*After paying the driver I take the walk of shame to my hotel room.*
*As soon as I get back to the hotel room, even though it's still bright out, I start to feel a bit tired.*
*Not as exhausted as I probably should be, but enough I can head for bed.*
*Welp, tomorrow's my first day at training. Hopefully it won't be a crash and burn experience.*
*Hopefully.*
*Gaikokujin no Sensei*
*I take a deep sigh as I stare at the looming school in front of me.*
You:  "I can’t believe it’s my first day of teaching already."
*I say out loud even though there’s nobody around.*
*That week of training went by so fast that I blinked and I missed it.*
*I thought I’d get more of a chance to practice teaching but…. Well, no such luck. It definitely seems to be more of a ‘throw you in and hope you swim’ kind of job.*
*Hitching up my messenger bag I make my way towards the front door.*
*As I head towards the door, I see a myriad of students making their way to the entrance of the school as well.*
*A few are on their cell phones, but most seem to be talking to each other.*
*And it isn’t lost on me that a large chunk of them are glancing towards me and then looking back and giggling or laughing.*

*She spots me nearly immediately. She turns to me and smiles.*
Secretary:  "Ah! <USER>-san! Ohayo Gozaimasu!"

*I repeat the phrase back to her with a forced smile.*
You:  "O…ohayo gozaimasu."
*She nods and puts up her hand.*
Secretary:  "Chotto matte kudosai."
*I nod and pretend to understand what she just said.*
*She picks up the phone and says something in Japanese.*

*I notice there to be a lot of shoe lockers where students are currently changing their shoes from their outside shoes to their school ones.*
*When I see this, the realization hits me.*
*Oh crap, I should be doing the same thing.*
*I glance back to the woman who’s still on the phone.*
*Taking a few steps back, I look for an empty shoe locker and take off my dress shoes.*
*Reaching into my bag, I pull out a pair of sneakers I had kept in there.*
*I slide on my sneakers and put the dress shoes in the cabinet.*
*After doing so I make my way over towards the receptionist.*
You:  "This is so weird"
*I mutter to myself as I look at my fully suited up self, the ensemble now complete with bright sneakers.*

Vice-Principal:  "Ah! Ohayo Gozaimasu <USER>-sensei. Ogenki desuka?"
*Once again, I’m so very thankful for my training class for at least teaching me those two phrases.*
You:  "Ah. Ohayo! Genki desu. (Ah, Morning. I'm fine.)"
*I think for a moment.*
You:  "Anata wa? (and you?)"
*I ask, completely unsure of myself.*

*He then motions for me to enter.*

*Once I step inside, I notice a large collection of teachers and staff, all either walking around, looking incredibly busy with papers in their hands, or sitting at their desk, frantically writing.*
*I’m motioned by the principal to an empty desk which has my name on it.*

Everyone:  "OHAYO GOZAIMASU!"
*They all say, basically in unison*
*I don’t even attempt to say it though, because by the point I would say anything, it would be far too late.*
*Embarrassed, I sit down as everyone else does the same.*
*The man at the front of the room continues to talk loudly, throwing out all sorts of Japanese that all basically sounds like gibberish to me.*
*It gets to the point where I start to zone out. However, this proves to be a bad idea as the man then turns to me.*
Vice-Principal:  "<USER> Sensei! jikoshoukai onegaishimasu!"
*Hearing my name, I jerk my head up and turn towards the front.*
*Some color drains from my face as I realize everyone in the room is now looking at me.*
*True, I was warned during training they would want me to do an introduction, but still, until you do it, you never realize just how never wracking it is.*
*As if my feet were made of lead, I make my way towards the front of the room.*
*I try to remember the words I had practiced so many times the night before.*
*I feel as if i'm marching to my death, and there's so many faces staring at me.*
*After the eternity that is the walk, I eventually make it to the front.*
*I take one quick look around the room. It seems that every pair of eyes is staring directly at me.*
*A sharp inhale later, I begin.*
You:  "Ohayo Gozaimasu! Watashi wa.... <USER> desu."

You:  "Amerika-jin...desu (I am American). Watashi no...nihongo ha warui desu (My Japanese is bad), demo.. (but)"

You:  "Yatte imasu (I am trying). Yoroshiku Onegaishimasu. (Please treat me well)"

*I hope to god that wasn't too horrible.*

*I dare to look back up to see them smiling at me.*
*My heart is beating a thousand beats a second as I slowly stand back up.*
*Daring myself to let out a small smile I make my way back to my seat.*
*My cheeks are still burning hot as I finally make it.*
*I'm not sure why, but that was the most embarrasing thing i've ever done in my life.*
*The day's only just begun and I feel like I need a nap.*
*Luckily for me, now that I have finished my terrible introduction, it seems that everyone's attention is now back to the front of the room.*
*The Vice principal talks for a while and I, of course, have absolutely no idea what he's saying.*
*While he's talking, I pick up my daily schedule and take a look.*
*It seems that I don't have any classes today. All that's really scheduled seems to be the opening ceremony assembly.*
*As the Vice principal finishes, everyone seems to be gathering themselves up and heading towards the door.*
*Oh shoot! We're already having the assembly?*
*Quickly I stand back up and follow everyone to the auditorium.*

*Of course the entire thing is in Japanese so I understand approximately zero percent of what he just said.*
*I have the strong urge to start dozing off, but realize just how bad that would look on my first day.*
*So I do my best to pretend i'm understanding what he's saying all while standing in the back of the auditorium with the other teachers.*
*Mercifully, the assembly ends with everyone applauding and the students filing out of the auditorium.*
*Quickly, before the students can turn around and see me, I head back towards the teacher's room.*
*The last thing I want is to stand there awkwardly and have every single student in the school staring at me as they walk by.*
*Luckily, I make it to the office ahead of the crowd.*
*I exhale as I sit down.*

*My schedule says that I don't leave until 4:30. Oh great. That means I have six more hours until I can go home.*
*What the hell am I supposed to do for SIX HOURS?*
*I think this to myself as the rest of the teachers file in. After a few minutes, nearly all of them gather some items and head back out.*
*Luckily for me there seems to be a computer at my desk. It is an old junky laptop, but hey, at least I could maybe look up some lesson ideas or something.*
*Booting up the computer I start to move around on the internet, looking at ideas for upcoming lessons but also tips and tricks for teaching kids.*
*There seem to be a few sites that are really helpful. I mark some ideas down in my notebook. Pretty soon, I've filled up two pages.*
*Soon enough though, my eyes are starting to get tired.*
*I rub my eyes and look at the clock.*
*11:30.*
*Are you KIDDING ME? It's only been an hour?*
*Oh my god, this first day is going to kill me. I just know it.*
*There has to be something else to do.*
*I close the laptop down and look around.*
*Huh... I wonder, would it be all right to just...take a look around the school?*
*I mean, it couldn't hurt to just walk around, right?*
*It's not like i'm going to go into the classrooms or anything.*
*Yeah, that should kill some time at least.*
*With new determination, I stand up and take a few steps out into the hallway.*

*As everyone seems to be in class.*

*I pass a few different rooms as I make my way through the hall. Several of them have students in them,*
*Talking to each other or listening to a teacher.*

*Of course. it's a school, what did I expect?*
*I continue my impromptu tour of the school before I eventually reach a door at the end of a hallway.*

*Oh. Neat.*
*I peer inside further. There doesn't seem to be anyone inside.*

Voice:  "Ah! KI O TSUKETE!! ABUNAI!"
*Hearing a strange voice yelling causes me to whip my head towards the direction it came from.*

Voice:  "AA! GOMENNE! DAIJYOUBU?"
*The voice is frantically asking as I hear it moving closer to me.*

Voice:  "Gomen! Gomen! Hontoni gomen nasai!"
*The voice is saying to me frantically.*
You:  "..."

*Through my fingers I can get a better look at the woman.*

*The teacher seems to be throwing out a string of apologetic Japanese that I don't understand even slightly.*

Gym Teacher:  "<USER>....sensei?"
*She says slowly as she finally realizes who she just beened in the face.*
*I glance to my side to see that a basketball was indeed the weapon that so callously careened into my face.*
Gym Teacher:  "I am so, so sorry!"
*The woman suddenly says quickly.*
You:  "No, no it's fine."
*I say without thinking as I wave my hand up and down.*
You:  "I should have been..."
*I stop myself for a moment.*
You:  "Wait...did you just speak English?"
*The woman, still sounding nervous puts a hand to her chest.*
Gym Teacher:  "That's not important. Are you okay?"
*She asks, touchingly concerned.*

*A bit of color returns to her cheeks as she exhales.*

*Whoa! Hey, she's pretty strong.*
You:  "Thanks"
*I say, wiping the dirt off my back.*
*She nods slightly as she doesn't respond.*
You:  "So, um, you know my name, but i can't say I know yours."
*She turns back to me confused.*
Gym Teacher:  "Oh, right, well, it's hard to miss you."
*She seems to think that sounds awkward as she speaks again, flailing her hands in front of her face.*
Gym Teacher:  "Oh! I didn't mean that in a bad way! You know, like a racist way! I just mean... oh no..."
*She says as she puts her hands on her face, which is now beet red.*
*I can't help but laugh slightly.*
You:  "It's okay, it's okay. What's your name?"
*This question seems to break her out of her stupor.*
Gym Teacher:  "Huh? Oh! Oh my gosh! I'm sorry! My name is Makiko Yuzue. But please call me Makiko!"
Makiko:  "In Japan you would call me 'Yuzue-san' but last names I always felt are way too formal."
You:  "Oh...sure...Makiko."
*As I call her by her first name, she blushes slightly.*
*The hell? She was the one to ask me to call her that.*
*Rather than keep the awkward going, I decide to speak again.*
You:  "So, your English is really good."
*She turns back to me with a surprised look on her face.*
Makiko:  "Oh! You think so?"
*I nod*
You:  "Well yeah, from what I can tell, it's pretty flawless."
*She puts her finger to her chin.*
Makiko:  "Well, I did study English pretty intensely in college."
You:  "Oh really? Why?"
*She looks back at me.*
Makiko:  "Hm? Why?"

Makiko:  "So! how are you liking your first day here?"
*Wow. Expert dodge there.*
*Oh well, it's obviously a sensitive topic. I shouldn't push.*
You:  "Well, I mean, it's a little overwhelming. There's um..hah. There's a lot of Japanese."
Makiko:  "Oh! That's right! You said your Japanese wasn't very good in your introduction, right?"
*I nod.*
Makiko:  "Well I thought it sounded all right!"
*She slaps me on the back.*
*I cringe as it nearly knocks the wind out of me.*
*I cough*
Makiko:  "Oh my goodness! I'm so sorry! I was just excited and...oh my goodness i'm sorry!"
*She frantically flails her hands again.*
*I don't know what it is about this situation, but I start to laugh.*
*Makiko looks at me confused.*
You:  "I'm sorry, that looked really funny."
*She puffs her cheeks as she furrows her brow.*
*She then exhales and puts on a smile.*
Makiko:  "I guess I deserved that."
*She then starts to giggle.*
*As the both of us are laughing, I hear a door open behind us.*
Voice:  "Ah Yuzue-sensei, Doko ni oite hoshide-"
*The voice stops halfway through.*
*I turn my head back to see a pair of middle school girls.*
Female Student:  "O! Eigo Sensei! Gomen!"
*The two girls smirk and then turn back to Makiko*
Female Student 2:  "Yuzue-sensei gomen nasai. atarashii eigo no sensei to futarikiri ni naritakattan desu ne."
*For some reason, Makiko's face turns bright red upon hearing this.*
*She clenches her fists tightly and raises her voice.*
Makiko:  "SONNA KOTO JA NAKUTE! KORE WA TADA-"
*At the rate she's screaming and flailing i'm worried she might overheat herself and pass out.*
*The two girls look from her to me, looking horribly confused, and merely giggle.*
*Finally, it seems that Makiko has had enough and walks over towards the two girls and begins to push them out the door.*
Makiko:  "Hai, hai. Omoshirokatta desu ne. Jaa detekinasai."
*The girls continue to giggle as she does so, but they are eventually lead out of the room.*
*Once she's satisfied that they're gone, she turns back to me.*
Makiko:  "Sorry about that."
You:  "Is everything all right?"
*She blushes again as she waves her hand up and down.*
Makiko:  "Oh yeah! Everything's fine! Everything's fine!"
You:  "O...okay."
You:  "Oh!"
*Something comes to mind.*
*I say the 'oh' so suddenly though that it catches her off guard and she shoots up surprised.*
You:  "Oh. Sorry. I just had a question."
*I reach into my pocket and pull out a piece of paper.*
You:  "I'm supposed to go to this building to get my paperwork done for moving in."
You:  "But I have no idea how to get there. Supposedly I can take a bus. Do you know how to get there?"
*Seemingly glad that the subject was changed, she walks over to me to take the paper from my hand.*
Makiko:  "Mm! Oh! Of course!"
*She says happily as she takes out a pen from a clipboard she had resting on the floor.*
*As she begins to write, she sticks her tongue out as if she were concentrating very hard.*
Makiko:  "And.....there we go!"
*She says, handing me back the paper, now with notes on it.*
*I give her a smile back.*
You:  "Oh! Thanks a lot!"
*I say as I give what she wrote a once-over.*
Makiko:  "Don't mention it!"
*She says, putting her hands on her hips in pride.*
*There's a short silence before Makiko speaks again.*
Makiko:  "Right! Well, I have to get back to setting up the gym. I was merely taking a bit of a break when you came in."
*I put my hand out.*
You:  "Right, gotcha, say no more. I'll let you get back to work."
You:  "I'll see you around okay?"
*Makiko smiles warmly.*
Makiko:  "Yeah! I'd like that!"
*Giving her a smile back I wave and take my leave.*
You:  "Well, that worked out at least. I mean, I got beaned in the face with a ball, but at least I got directions."
You:  "At least I have a plan. All right. Tonight, i'm heading out to get this stupid town paperwork done."
*Gaikokujin no Sensei*
*Well, I'm standing at the bus stop. At least I was able to find THAT without getting lost.*
*The bus pulls up to my stop, and I look upwards at the bus' number on the sign.*
*I look at the paper one more time and then to the bus. Yeah, this seems to be the right one, at least according to Makiko.*
*As the door opens, I inhale sharply as I step in, dropping the necessary yen into the box.*
*Looking around the seats, I notice very quickly that the bus seems to have hardly anybody on it.*
*Can’t exactly say that’s a surprise. It is past sunset and I’m sure not many people are headed to town hall or whatever they call it at this time of day.*
*I’m surprised at just now nice the seats are when I sit down. Nothing like the harder, metal seats on the buses in America.*
*And to my surprise, there’s no signs of tears, graffiti, or chewing gum. Man, they keep their buses nice here.*
*As the drive begins, I try to turn my head out the window to see the scenery. Unfortunately, as the sun has nearly completely gone for the day at this point, I give up on that idea and focus on counting the stops.*
*I check the paper. Makiko wrote down that it was 12 stops exactly. I certainly hope she’s right.*
*After a few stops, I notice the remaining people start to file out of the bus, until eventually it’s only me and a single old lady.*
*I count the stops as I go. 7.*
*8.*
*9.*
*I get more anxious with each one, hoping I don’t miss it. If I did, I would honestly have no idea what to do.*
*Finally, I count twelve stops. I press the button to get off.*
*The driver slows down and pulls over.*
*Grabbing my messenger bag, I thank the bus driver with a badly pronounced ‘Arigatou’ and step off the bus.*
*The moment I step off the bus, I feel my stomach sink.*
*The stop seems to be a little bus stop on the side of the road, and, in every other direction, nothing but forest.*
*I take a moment to get my bearings, but, before I can even turn around to get back on the bus, I hear the sound of the driver pulling away.*
*Well, crap.*
*This was definitely NOT the right stop.*
*Did Makiko give me the wrong directions?*
*No, I’m sure I did something wrong. That makes more sense.*
*I take a quick look around. Luckily for me, the moon seems to be out so it’s not COMPLETELY dark.*
*With the aid of the light of the moon I can see the bus stop on the opposite side of the street.*
*I jaunt across the small road and take a look at the time table posted on the sign.*
*I can feel the color drain from my face as I do.*
*Two hours. The next bus doesn’t arrive for TWO HOURS.*
*I can’t help but start to laugh at myself.*
*It’s either that or freak out, and apparently, my body decided laughter was the best option.*
*A few moments later I manage to compose myself as I try to think of what to do next.*
You:  "okay…okay. So let’s see…. The bus was a pretty straight line… So if I walk back… I should be able to catch another one faster."
*I mumble this to myself.*
*I let out a long drawn out sigh.*
*Okay. Here we go.*
*Hitching up my bag, I turn around and begin to walk back the way I came.*
*And here’s where I started. Thinking about all the things that lead me to this moment have at least passed some of the time.*
*Unfortunately, it’s been doing nothing to help my exhaustion from this long walk.*
You:  "Huff. Huff."
*Okay, this was a VERY bad idea.*
*i’ve been walking for about thirty five minutes and at this point my legs are starting to feel a bit wobbily.*
You:  "Holy crap I have to start working out."
*Every additional step I take feels like I’m wearing lead shoes.*
You:  "Come on feet…keep…moving."
*They’re doing their very best to ignore my commands.*
*Struggling, I lift another foot.*
*I bring it down with purpose.*
*I find myself smirk slightly.*
You:  "All right! Fight through the pain. You can do this. Just-"
*And that’s when my foot misses the pavement.*
*Stepping on the hilly side, I completely lose my footing and go tumbling down the side of the hill.*
*I try to reach out onto something and catch myself, but it’s to no avail as I go careening, head over heels down the hill.*
*Finally, I stop. Or should I say, something stops me.*
*I feel a pain in my spine as my ears ring from a loud CRASH!*
*I hear a snap as well, and hope to god it wasn’t one of my bones.*
*Now that I’ve stopped, I tentatively try to move different part of my body. Arms check, legs, check.*
*I move different parts of my body.*
*I feel relieved that nothing seems to be broken.*
*Thank heaven for small miracles*
*I rub my head gingerly as I sit up.*
*Still feeling a bit woozy, I reach my hand out and grab the object nearest to me.*
*I’m a bit surprised to feel the object supporting me feels rather smooth.*
*Upon pulling myself up I take a look at the object.*
*At first, it looks like some kind of ornate birdhouse*
*It’s completely covered in vines and looks like it hasn’t been touched in years.*
You:  "Tch."
*I make a noise with my mouth.*
You:  "It’s sad seeing people let things like this go into disrepair."
*Seeing as there’s nobody around, I decide to help out this poor birdhouse like…thing.*
*Reaching over I begin to pull off the vines from it.*
*Quickly I soon realize that this thing isn’t a birdhouse.*
*Oh right. I think I saw one of these in a travel book on Japan. I think they call these things…shrines or something*
*I almost have all of the vines peeled off. There only seems to be one left that’s located on what appears to be the doors of the tiny shrine.*
*I pull.*
*Nothing happens.*
You:  "Oh, come on."
*I yank harder*
*Nothing happens.*
You:  "Oh no you don’t. This might be one of the worst nights of my life, but I’m at LEAST not going to be defeated by a stupid vine!"
*I angrily pull with all of my might.*
*Then, as if someone cut a rope, the vine snaps, and I go flying backwards, the remnants of the vine still in my hand*
*My back crashes against a tree with a loud THWACK!*
You:  "Ow…man…I’m gonna need a chiropractor after this."
*Slowly, I open my eyes as I rub my now very sore back*
*As I do, I notice the doors on the small shrine are now completely vine free.*
You:  "Hm. Well that looks better."
*Before I can even take a moment to feel proud of my work, the small doors shoot open on their own.*
*I stand up quickly in shock*
*Timidly, I turn my attention to the doors.*
You:  "The hell?"
*Before I can say anything else, a blinding light emits from the tiny doors, directly into my eyes.*
*Instinctively, I cover my eyes and duck behind the tree I’m standing in front of.*
*After a few moments, the light dissipates, leaving the scene once again only illuminated by the moonlight.*
*Convinced the light show is over, I rub my eyes gingerly*
You:  "Ugh…the crap was that?"
*I ask out loud, waiting for the spots in my eyes to disappear.*
Strange Voice:  "Doumo, shisubekimono. Kyou wa anata wa gyoukou de arimasu. Kamisama ga anata ni hohoendekuremashita. Soreyu, rikai sae dekinai kyoui wo keiken shimasu."
Strange Voice:  "Waga gouryuu no hanashi wa anata no kuni de suenagaku tsudzukimasu."
*A voice, seemingly out of nowhere begins to speak a long string of Japanese, all of which I have no idea what she’s saying.*
*Wait. Is someone else here in the woods with me? Why would someone be here too?*
*Mustering up my courage, I peek my head around the corner of the tree.*
*As I do so, I’m greeted by the sight of a woman. She seems to be sitting on top of the small shrine that I had just ripped the vines off of.*
*Was that shrine big enough to support the weight of a human?*
*Wait. That’s not the question I should be asking myself.*
*The woman appears to be wearing a long Japanese dress. I think they’re called ‘kimonos'.*
*Her features appear to be more mature in nature. The top part of her outfit leaves little to the imagination as she definitely seems to be more…endowed in some places than I’ve seen since coming to this country.*
*The thing that by far confuses me the most though, are the fox ears sticking out her head, and the long bushy tail coming out of her back.*
*I rub my eyes once again to make sure that light didn’t start causing me to hallucinate.*
*I look back. Nope, she’s still there.*
*That's...a weird costume. Maybe there's a festival going on around here or something?*
*All the while this is going on, the woman is still going on with her monologue, looking in every direction except for mine.*
*Finally, I gulp and tentatively take a few steps forward.*
*As I gingerly take a few steps forward, I notice that the woman is still talking to seemingly no one in particular, as if she’s performing a theatrical monologue she had been practicing for weeks.*
Strange Woman:  "Watashitachi no unmei wa tsunagatteimasu. Erabareta anata to seirei no watashi. Deai ga eien ni wasurerarenai fuun no koibito no monoga-"
*Finally, after what seems like an eternity of incomprehensible dialogue, her golden eyes turn to me.*
*And as they do, her mouth freezes in mid-word.*
*Gingerly, I wave my hand towards her.*
You:  "Um...Hi."
*I say so quietly, I’m not even sure that she hears me.*
Strange Woman:  "Gai...kokujin?"
*She replies, nearly as quietly.*
*Color quickly drains from her face.*
*She shakily raises a finger towards me.*
Strange Woman:  "Anata wa…Gaikokujin desuka?"
*Confused, I scratch my head.*
*Oh! Right! My book!*
*I quickly think to myself as I reach into my pocket.*
*Pulling out the book I flip through the pages.*
*Gaikokujin….Gaikokujin…*
*Ah! Here it is… oh! Foreigner!*
*I turn back to her.*
You:  "Hai."
*I reply simply.*
*As the word exits my mouth, I see her shoulders slump as it seems that all the air deflates out of her.*
*I am so confused right now.*
*As I look back towards her, I reach my arm out.*
You:  "Hey, uh, are you okay?"
*Before my hand can reach her though, she immediately drops down behind the shrine.*
*I can still see her form behind the shrine as she’s crouched down holding her head.*
Strange Woman:  "Iya! Fukanou desu! Erabareta hito ga kare tte koto! Kitto kamisama ga machigatteimasu. Hai, sou desu ne. Kamisama ni ukagattara kitto kono gokai wo setsumei shimasu!"
You:  "Right um…I’m just going to go…that way…"
*Even though she’s not looking at me, I say this out loud regardless, pointing my thumb back towards the street.*
*Leaving her behind to do…whatever, I make my way back towards the street.*
*I don’t know whether I was still in shock or whatever, but random girls in fox costumes showing up out of nowhere should be throwing me for more of a loop than I am, but maybe my head is still rattling from the fall.*
*After about five seconds of walking, suddenly, from behind me, I hear a loud electrical ZAP!*
*Quickly, I whip back around to see a light coming from behind the shrine.*
*Accompanying the light, which is far more frightening, is a scream.*
*Did..did that girl just get struck by lightning?*
*I quickly turn my eyes to the sky.*
*No, not a single cloud.*
*What in the hell is going on?*
*Against all better judgment, I rush back towards the woman.*
You:  "Hey! Hey! Are you okay?"
*I ask concerned and loudly.*
*Upon reaching the shrine, I whip myself around it to see the woman. And, other than now having a slightly singed kimono and some blemishes on her face, appears to be all right.*
*I repeat myself.*
You:  "Hey, are you okay?"
*This time, her eyelids start to flutter as slowly, they open.*
Strange Woman:  "Dou shita no?"
*As she looks up at me, I notice a twinge of red start to appear on her cheeks.*
*Suddenly, as if breaking out of a trance, she bolts upright and looks at me, staring me straight in the eyes as she puffs out her cheeks.*
Strange Woman:  "Kora! Nande son'na ko to shita no! ? Sugoku shitsureidesu yo!"
*Seeing her poutily chastise me, I can’t help but laugh. Does she think I was the one who just shocked her?*
*As I laugh this apparently makes her more infuriated as she begins to shiver with irritation.*
*As quickly as she starts however, she stops.*
*Suddenly, her eyes go wide.*
Strange Woman:  "Demo… sore tte…"
*She then looks up at the sky.*
Strange Woman:  "Maa, kou shika ikenai nara…"
*Exhaling an audible sigh, she then turns back to me.*
*Nearly in an instant her entire disposition changes. Her gaze softens, her posture adjusts, and I can swear her smell changes slightly too.*
Strange Woman:  "Ima no wa gomen nasai. Tada… bikkuri shimashita. Anata ni eraremashita meiyo wa jinruihatsu desu. Kyou wa kamisama wa anata ni hohoendeimasu ne. Kyou wa watashi to hitotsu ni natte soukou to gouken no jidai wo umidashimasu."
*She’s getting closer and closer to me. Her kimono opens slightly at the top, revealing deep cleavage, but she doesn’t seem to mind.*
*I chuckle nervously, as that's the only thing I can think to do as i'm so confused.*
*Suddenly, I nearly drop the thing I was holding in my hand.*
You:  "Oh! right!"
*Quickly I put the book inbetween us, which seems to shock her.*
*I flip through the book until I find the page. I clear my throat.*
You:  "Nihongo ni hanashimasen. (I don't speak Japanese)"
Strange Woman:  "..Eh?"
*Suddenly she freezes, as if she were made of stone, and a cold wind blows through the trees almost to a comical effect.*
Strange Woman:  "Anata wa…nihongo ni hanashimasen…ka?"
*She asks, as if making sure she heard correctly.*
*Since she was using the words I just said, I assumed she was making sure she heard me correctly.*
*I nod once.*
*Her face becomes extremely flushed at this point as beads of sweat start falling down from the top of her face and her eyes start to almost swirl*
*As if she were clinging to a life preserver, she grabs onto my shoulders*
Strange Woman:  "Anata wa nihongo ga hanasenai to wa douiu imidesu ka????"
*I stare at her confused.*
*Seeing my abject confusion, she lets go of me and turns back around, holding her head.*
Strange Woman:  "Uso desu yo ne, Uso desu yo ne"
*She mumbles to herself as she holds her head*
You:  "Are you okay? Does your head hurt?"
*Trying to regain her composure, she turns back to me after taking a deep inhale.*
Strange Woman:  "Ecchi..shitai"
*Her voice once again changes as she asks me this. This time it’s far more sultry as she begins to pull the sides of her kimono down.*
*Is she like…trying to seduce me or something? No, this is way too weird.*
*I don’t know exactly what her game is, but I’m sure as hell not going to play it.*
*Seeing my look of astonishment, she places a smile back on her face.*
*Lowering her kimono even further to the point where I am starting to get more than just a little show, she then stops and begins to move behind the shrine, gesturing for me to follow her.*
*I’ve seen way too many horror movies to know where this is going.*
*As soon as she’s out of range, I turn around and make a sprint towards the street.*
*I don’t even bother to look behind me to see the look on her face, but I can only imagine it’s probably hilarious.*
*Within a few moments I hear another loud CRASH!*
*The light that emanated from it was so bright that I could see it in front of me despite it coming from behind.*
*I begin to slow my pace, until finally, I dare look behind me.*
*There, about 30 feet behind me, is the woman, lying face first on the ground, her arm outstretched to me, her clothes even more covered in scorch marks than before.*
*I look back and forth. No one else seems to be ready to jump at me from the shadows. At least, that I can tell.*
*I know it’s stupid, but maybe this time she’s really hurt? I can’t believe this girl got hit by lightning twice in a row. Isn’t that mathematically impossible?*
*I take a few tentative steps forward.*
*She doesn’t seem to be moving as I make my way closer.*
*Acting as if she were a trap about to snap, I slowly, very slowly edge towards the woman.*
*I slowly nudge her with my toe.*
*No response.*
*I inhale slightly and bend down.*
You:  "Hey um…you..ok-"
*Before I can even finish my question, the woman pops back up as if spring loaded, and grabs me by the collar.*
You:  "Whoa! Hey!"
*This time though, her face seems more determined, biting her lower lip in frustration.*
Strange Woman:  "Yaritai!"
*I merely stare at her in confusion.*
*I swear I can almost see a vein start to form on her forehead.*
*She releases me and puts her hand on her hips*
*She then points to me.*
Strange Woman:  "Anata"
*She points at herself.*
Strange Woman:  "Watashi"
*She then makes a circle with her left hand and sticks out her pointer finger with her right.*
*The woman proceeds to move the finger in and out of the circle.*
Strange Woman:  "Seiko"
*I hit my open palm with my hand in an 'aha!’ moment.*
*Oh…she wants to sleep with me!*
*Wait…SHE WANTS TO SLEEP WITH ME?!*
*I quickly pull out my Japanese phrasebook and flip through it like a madman.*
*I look from the book to her.*
You:  "Wakarimasu (I understand)"
*Hearing this word come out of my mouth, the woman sighs deeply in relief.*
*Before she can say anything else though, I speak first.*
You:  "Demo, iie. Kekko desu. (But, no thank you)"
*The woman's relieved smile is replaced with a look of confusion.*
Strange Woman:  "Eh?"
*She asks, this time looking at me, as if she didn't hear me correctly.*
*Huh. Maybe my accent was off.*
*I look at the book again.*
You:  "Iie. Kekko desu."
*I say, this time making sure I got the correct pronunciaction down.*
*The woman blinks a few more times.*
Strange Woman:  "Ee....iie? Kekkou desu?"
*She repeats my words as if she had never heard them before.*
*Was my Japanese REALLY that bad? I mean probably, but still.*
You:  "Right...well, I mean, you probably don't understand me, but um..I'm gonna get going. I have to get home."
*I gesture towards the street.*
You:  "Good luck with...well....whatever you're doing."
*Finishing this, I give her a wave and turn around and head back towards the street.*
*I barely make it a few steps before i hear a voice behind me.*
Strange Woman:  "Ee?! CHOTTO MATTE!"
*I look behind me, and this time, see the woman following me, her cheeks puffed in irritation.*
Strange Woman:  "'Iie' to wa doiu imidesuka?"
*I sigh with irritation and keep walking towards the street.*
*She tries to grab my arm, but this time, I don't even look back and shrug it off.*
*Thankfully, in a few moments, i'm able to make it back to the road.*
*As I look both ways, something glistens to my right down the road, catching my eye.*
*I feel like i'm going to shed a tear with happiness as I spy the reflective glass panel of a bus stop.*
You:  "Oh Thank god!"
*I say as I make a run towards the bus stop.*
*This seems to be very fortuitous timing as just as I make it to the bus stop, I can see the headlights of an approaching bus.*
*I look backwards frantically to see if the woman is still following me, but to my great relief, she no longer seems to be there.*
*I let out a deep sigh as the bus pulls up and stops.*
*I don't think i've ever been so happy to see a bus driver in my life.*
*I give him a wide smile as i drop the money into the slot.*
You:  "Arigatou!"
*I say, more than a little enthusiastically as I hop on and find a seat in the back of the bus.*
*Now safe inside the bus headed home, I pant heavily.*
*Man, i'm more exhausted from my little adventure than I realized.*
*I pull on my shirt collar, loosening my tie slightly.*
*I close my eyes and let the sound of the bus take me away.*
Strange Voice:  "Doko ni ikimasu?"
*A voice next to me says.*
*Thinking they have to be speaking to someone else, I ignore it.*
*Wait a second...I recognize that voice.*
*My eyes shoot open, as, sure enough, the woman is seated next to me, staring right at me.*
You:  "BWAH!!"
*I scream unintentionally as I fly three seats to the left.*
*This is so sudden it almost causes the bus driver to swerve.*
*Concerned sounding, the bus driver says something in Japanese.*
*Looking back to the bus driver, I quickly pull out my translation book.*
You:  "Aa. Gomen. Daijyoubu... (Sorry. I'm fine.)"
*Seemingly satisfied with this, the driver turns back.*
You:  "What are you doing here?!"
*I ask her in a loud whisper.*
*She stares at me blankly, obviously not understanding a word i'm saying.*
*I slap my hand to my forehead and slide it down along my face.*
*I pull up my book again.*
You:  "Naze anata wa koko ni imasuka? (why you here?)"
*I have a bit of trouble with this phrase, but i think she gets the overall point.*
*She looks at me with a soft smile and suddenly grabs onto my arm.*
Strange Woman:  "Doko ni mo tsuretekimasu! Ha-ni!"
*She adds what sounds an awful lot like 'honey' at the end.*
*I do my best to move my arm out of her grip, but there's not a lot of room to move.*
*Having completely given up at this point, I merely sit there and let her hold my arm for the duration of the trip.*
*After what seems like an eternity, we approach the station which I had originally left from.*
*Upon reaching the station, I begin to stand up.*
*Seeing this, the woman next to me begins to move as well.*
*As I walk forward towards the front of the bus, she releases my arm.*
*Stepping off, I look behind me, expecting to see her, but to my surprise, she is no longer there.*
*Feeling relieved, I take a few steps away from the bus.*
*However, my relief is short lived, as, once the bus pulls away, she once again appears to my left.*
*Tired and extremely defeated, I begin my walk home, trying my best to create some distance between me and her.*
*The entire walk home I keep looking over my shoulder to see if the woman is still there.*
*And every time I do, she is still behind me, looking at everything that we pass by in town with awe.*
*If I weren’t so worried at the moment, it might have been kind of cute.*
*When I’m about a few blocks from my apartment, I turn back to her.*
You:  "Look. I know you can’t understand me, but I’m ABSOLUTELY not going to sleep with you. So…you know… you can go now."
*I move my hand in a shooing motion.*
*Strangely enough, this seems to make her smile as she moves closer to me.*
*The hell?*
You:  "What the…NO! I SAID SHOO!"
*I make the motion again. Still doesn’t seem to be working.*
*I can feel a vein start to grow in my forehead.*
*The hell is this, opposite day?*
*With great irritation I trudge towards my apartment.*
*Upon reaching the door, I turn back to her.*
*I dig into my pocket and pull out my book.*
*Flipping through the pages I find what i need and read the passage quietly to myself.*
*I inhale as i put the book down to look at her.*
You:  "Watashi wa chu ni haitte imasu. Anata wa denakereba narani (I am going inside. You have to leave)"
*Even though my Japanese pronunciation and grammar is terrible, I’m pretty sure she got the message, as she puffed out her cheeks in irritation.*
*Turning back to the door, I reach into my pocket for my key.*
*As I do so, I look backwards one more time.*
*This time, I’m surprised to see that the woman is now gone.*
*My eyes grow larger as I look around*
*I move my head around the corner, looking everywhere a person could possibly hide. Nothing.*
*I let out an appreciative sigh.*
You:  "Thank god. She must have FINALLY gotten the message."
*I say to myself, allowing a small smile to cross my face.*
*Using the key, I put it in the lock and turn it.*
*As I begin to undo my tie, I open the door with my free hand.*
*As I do, I hear a voice.*
Strange Voice:  "Okaerinasai!"
*Hearing this voice, I freeze in mid pose.*
*Looking dead ahead, sitting on her legs is the woman, looking up at me with a smile.*
*I immediately look behind me into the open doorway and then back to her.*
You:  "What the-how did-"
*I can’t even formulate proper words at this point.*
Strange Woman:  "Okaerinasai, anata! Gohan wo taberu? Ofuro ni suru? Soretomo, wa. ta. shi?"
*As she says the three last syllables, she begins to pull down the front of her kimono seductively.*
*I can feel my eye begin to twitch in frustration.*
*That’s it. I’ve had enough. I seriously can’t deal at this point.*
*Slowly, I take a few steps over to her.*
*Placing down my bag, I reach down and pick up the woman bridle style.*
Strange Woman:  "Ara! Tsuyoi desu ne. Doko ni tsureteiku no deshou ka??"
*Even if I could understand what she was saying, I would ignore it. I can guess the general gist anyway.*
*As I pass through the doorway, I place her outside out of my apartment, and shut the door, leaving her outside.*
*Stepping back inside my apartment, I rub my hair with my hand.*
You:  "Jesus, what is wrong with some people?"
*Picking up my bag I take a few steps into my bedroom.*
Strange Voice:  "Beddo ni kite.... honey"
*There, laying on my bed, her kimono half off, is the woman.*
*I freeze where I stand, my mouth agape.*
You:  "How did..what did…."
*Is this lady a freaking ghost or something?*
*I quickly try to regain control of the situation.*
*I pick my book back out and look for a word. Finding the word I put the book back in my pocket.*
*I cough into my hand.*
*I point to the front door.*
You:  "Saru. (Leave)"
*Suddenly, and without warning, tears begin to fill the woman’s eyes.*
*She moves into a different pose, supporting her weight with one hand and lightly biting down on her finger with the other.*
*She then looks up to me with moist eyes.*
Strange Woman:  "Minikui tte omoimasu ka?"
*I raise my eyebrow.*
*Minikui?*
*I pick my book back out.*
*Oh. Ugly.*

You:  "So um, I’m going inside. You two might have a problem if I do so….just warning you."
*The two don’t seem to be paying attention to me as Fumo has managed to separate from Aina and is currently chastising her angrily in Japanese.*
*Seeing the two like this, I rub my hand through my hair in frustration.*
*This is going absolutely nowhere.*
*Reaching my hand up I place two fingers in my mouth and blow a large whistle*
*It echoes through the street and suddenly, both Aina and Fumo stop in mid-word.*
You:  "You two finished?"
*I ask, sounding slightly more irritated than I probably needed to.*
You:  "Or you want to embarrass yourselves some more?"
*It seems the two have finally realized that Makiko or Kotone are no longer there and the situation they were in finally hit them.*
*Both of them turn bright red at the same time.*
Fumo:  "S…sorry."
Aina:  "Gomenne…ha-…eto…<USER>-sama"
*The two of them seem to not even be able to look me in the eyes as they look away, completely embarrassed at their actions.*
*Still not looking at me, Fumo walks past me into the store.*
*I let out a sigh.*
You:  "Come on Aina."
You:  "We need to get Yukatas right?"
*I turn to Aina.*
You:  "Watashi tachi…ikimasuka, Aina. (Let's go, Aina)"
*Aina slowly turns her head up to me and places a small smile on it.*
Aina:  "Hai. To…."
*She stops to think for a moment.*
Aina:  "I..sorry."
*Her face drops.*
*I sigh again*
You:  "Ii desuyo."
*I reach out my hand towards her which she tentatively takes.*
*I do the same with Fumo who slowly takes my hand as well.*
*Hand in hand, I take both of them into the store.*
*I look around the store when I step in. Well, store is being a little generous. It seems to be only slightly larger than a walk in closet. In fact, it can barely fit the five of us.*
*An older lady is at the back of the room and starts to converse with Makiko in Japanese. I’m assuming it’s about what we’re going to need.*
*Upon finishing her conversation, Makiko turns to me.*
Makiko:  "Okay, so Kotone and I already have ours. I explained to her that you three need one."
*I look around at the different Yukatas.*
You:  "You sure they’re going to have one in my size?"
Makiko:  "She says she has a few that would fit you and she can always alter if she needs to. In fact…"
*Makiko moves behind me and starts to push.*
Makiko:  "She wants you to go first."
You:  "Wait. What?"
*Even if I tried to fight it there would be no hope against Makiko, so, I surrender myself to fate and allow myself to be pushed into a small measuring area.*
*Behind the curtain the woman takes a few of my measurements and then walks out*
*A few moments later she comes back with a dark blue yukata.*
*She gestures for me to try on the yukata.*
*I slide it on and look at myself in the mirror.*
*To my surprise it actually doesn’t look bad. It kind of looks like a fancy bathrobe. Only a lot more ornate.*
*After helping me buckle it correctly, she gestures to me to walk outside.*
*I can hear the girls talking to each other in Japanese as I step out. As I do though, all four of them go quiet as they look at me.*
You:  "So um..how do I look?"
*Silence*
You:  "Is it that weird?"
*Suddenly, stars seem to appear in Aina’s eyes.*
Aina:  "Aaahhh!!! Ha-ni wa totemmo handsemu!!"
*Fumo sticks her fingers inbetween Aina’s shirt and her neck holding her from doing something stupid.*
You:  "Well, I feel awkward."
Makiko:  "No no! You look good!"
You:  "Really? You think so? It doesn't...you know look weird?"
Kotone:  "Why would it look weird?"
You:  "Um, well...you know..."
Fumo:  "It looks good on you <USER>. Stop worrying."
Fumo:  "Isn't the fact that I'm holding back Aina saying anything?"
You:  "Not...really."
Fumo:  "Okay, that's true. But you know what I mean."
*I have to admit though, their warm words do make me feel a bit better. Still, probably better i break the mood.*
You:  "Well, anyway, thanks. Still, that’s enough of me being embarrassed all by myself now it’s someone else’s turn."
Kotone:  "Aw, come on, I want to embarrass you some more!"
You:  "Kotone, congratulations. You just volunteered for it to be your turn."
Kotone:  "What? No I didn’t!"
Fumo:  "Sounded like it to me."
Kotone:  "Ugh. Fine."
*Kotone says something to the woman and goes in to get changed.*
*Makiko then turns to me.*
Makiko:  "Okay, time for you to step outside."
You:  "Wait. Why? It’s not like you’re getting changed in here."
*Makiko blushes slightly.*
Makiko:  "No! It’s not because of that! It’s because you’ve never seen us in Yukatas before and It’ll leave more of an impression if you see us all at once!"
You:  "I mean…I don’t think it really matters."
Makiko:  "It totally does! You only get one first look! So come on! Get out, out out out."
*I throw up my hands in surrender.*
You:  "All right, fine, fine. I can tell when I’m not wanted."
Makiko:  "What? No! That’s not what I’m saying!"
You:  "Makiko, I’m joking. It’s all right."
Fumo:  "Makiko, you worry too much."
You:  "That’s what I keep telling her."
*That’s the last statement I get to make before I’m forcefully shoved out of the door.*
*Aina seems to want to follow me, however Fumo says something to her which causes her not only to stop coming after me, but perk up.*
*So now I’m standing outside of the shop…in a yukata…randomly outside as people begin to walk past.*
*I don’t normally feel shy, but jesus this is embarrassing. Random American standing outside in a Yukata looking completely lost.*
*I do my best not to look at anybody in the eyes to avoid any sort of awkwardness.*
*I attempt to reach into my pocket for my phone. My hand slides along my yukata as I do*
You:  "Oh damn. That’s right. Where are the pockets in this thing?"
*I move my hands against the yukata as I begin to fumble around*
*Finally, I manage to find the weirdly placed pocket which houses my phone.*
*As I attempt to bring up the internet, I accidentally click my finger on the photos button.*
*My eyes are drawn to the photos i've taken since i've arrived in Japan. Oddly enough, there aren't a lot.*
You:  "Huh…I hardly have any photos on this thing."
*I say out loud to myself.*
Makiko:  "Well then let’s take a picture!"
*A loud voice from behind me says excitedly.*
*I nearly drop the phone in surprise, as I play hot potato with it, before it launches up into the air.*
*I flip around in an attempt to catch it, but instead am greeted by the sight of all four women in their yukatas.*
*For an instant, no joke, I actually lose my breath.*
*For some reason, the four of them look extremely elegant. There’s something about the way that the yukata sits on them that looks stunning.*
Fumo:  "Got it."
*Fumo says, as, holding out her hands, the phone lands directly in them.*
*This action breaks me out of my stupor.*
You:  "Oh. Fumo, thank you."
Fumo:  "You’re welcome. Take better care of this though."
You:  "Right. Right. Sorry."
Kotone:  "I think we once again stunned Mr. American."
Aina:  "Ahhh Yukata wa iidesune…"
*Aina rubs up against her sleeve and giggles*
Makiko:  "So, how do we look?"
You:  "You all look great! I mean, I’ve never really seen Yukatas before, but they look really nice on you four."
Kotone:  "Not a bad answer. I give it a B+"
Fumo:  "I think it was fine."
*As i'm standing there, still somewhat entranced, I cough into my hand as I try to change the subject*
You:  "Right, so are we going to head to the festival or…."
Kotone:  "Ah, trying to get out of talking about how great we look, huh?"
You:  "That’s EXACTLY what I’m doing Kotone."
Kotone:  "Hmm… okay. We’ll let you off the hook. If you compliment at least one of us. And act as that person’s escort to the festival until we get there."
You:  "That kind of defeats the purpose. Besides, i'm not just going to randomly pick one of you"
Makiko:  "Ooh! I like this idea! Let’s make it random!"
*Except for the word 'random' she didn't hear a word I said, did she?*
*A confused Aina asks Makiko what’s going on who translates.*
Aina:  "Ahh! I like! Let us do!"
You:  "I have absolutely no say in this do i?"
Fumo:  "Doesn’t look like it."
You:  "Thank you for your honesty Fumo."
*I let out a sigh.*
You:  "Fine. I’m typing a number 1-4 on my phone"
*There’s a quick translation for Aina.*
Makiko:  "Three!"
Kotone:  "Four!"
Aina:  "Ichi! Eto… one!"
Fumo:  "I suppose that leaves me with two."
*All right. What number did I type on my phone?*
*1":
*2":
*3":
*4":
*I turn the phone around which reveals a number 2*
*Fumo sees this and her eyes go slightly wide.*
Fumo:  "Oh."
You:  "Well Fumo, look like I’m taking you."
*Aina looks visibily disappointed and she puffs her cheeks.*
Kotone:  "Ooh. Fumo eh? You treat her nice now Mr. American."
*I shoot Kotone an unamused glance.*
*Fumo seems as if she’s about to say something.*
You:  "Fumo please don’t say “sorry you have to walk with me’ or something like that. PLEASE."
*Fumo closes her mouth as I say this.*
You:  "Were you about to say that?"
Fumo:  "...Maybe."
You:  "Well let’s try it again."
*Fumo pauses for a moment.*
Fumo:  "Um..Let’s…have fun."
You:  "There we go."
*Satisfied, the four of us begin to walk towards what I assume to be the festival*
*I walk slightly closer to Fumo than the other three, but still keep a respectable distance.*
*I steal a glance over at Fumo who’s walking besides me.*
*Whenever I look at her, there’s always a kind of elegance about her. It’s hard to explain but it’s kind of beautiful in a way.*
*Fumo’s eyes move over to me without turning her head.*
Fumo:  "Something wrong?"
You:  "Huh? Oh. No, nothing."
*Suddenly I realize something.*
*Oh, the compliment. Right.*
You:  "Hey um…you uh..you look really nice in that Yukata Fumo"
*A shocked Fumo tilts her head to me, going red for a moment before turning it back as if realizing something.*
Fumo:  "Oh. The compliment. Right."
Fumo:  "Well, thank you."
*We continue walking towards the festival, as we do, I notice more and more people coming around, before eventually we started to get pressed together.*
*Fumo bumps into me.*
Fumo:  "Sorry."
*It’s then that Fumo seems to realize just how due to her height, she can’t see over the head of anyone else.*
Fumo:  "Can you see the others?"
*Standing several inches over most of the other festival attendees I nod.*
You:  "Yeah, They’re still around. But we better stick together or we’re going to get lost."
You:  "Also, we don’t want Aina getting too far away for obvious reasons."
*Fumo nods.*
Fumo:  "Mm. right."
You:  "Here, you better take my hand so you don’t get lost."
*I hold out my hand.*
Fumo:  "It’s all right I-"
*Before she can say anything else I take her hand in mine.*
*Once again I’m caught off guard at just how small and dainty her hand is. My hand easily encapsulates hers.*
You:  "Sorry."
*She shakes her head.*
Fumo:  "No, you're right. This will make it easier to stay together."
*There’s a short silence as we make our way towards the entrance.*
You:  "I meant it by the way."
Fumo:  "Hm?"
You:  "You really DO look nice in that."
Fumo:  "Huh? Oh…"
*Fumo turns her head away so as not to look at me.*
Fumo:  "You..you look good yourself."
You:  "You sure? I feel a bit weird."
*Fumo shakes her head.*
Fumo:  "No, you look fine. It IS a bit strange to see you in traditional Japanese clothes but… a good strange."
Fumo:  "In fact…I would say that describes you overall."
You:  "Well, that’s one of the nicest things anyone’s ever said to me. And hey, I could say the same about you."
*As Fumo’s about to say something, I get tackled on one side from Aina.*
Aina:  "Ahhh!! Ha-ni! Here!"
*Sure enough, I look around, and Kotone and Makiko are standing off the side, the latter of which is waving to us.*
*I turn the phone over to reveal a 3.*
Makiko:  "All right! I win! I should gamble more often."
You:  "No you shouldn’t Makiko, I know how bad your poker face is."
*Makiko’s face turns red with embarrassment.*
Makiko:  "I didn’t mean like with cards <USER>! I meant like…roulette or something."

*Seeing the results, Aina huffs in annoyance but heads off with the other three a few steps ahead of us.*
Makiko:  "So the deal was that I get to escort you right?"
You:  "What? No. it was the opposite."
Makiko:  "Ooh! So you get to be the knight? Protecting the princess from the dangers of the world?"
You:  "I mean, sure if you want to think of it like that."
Makiko:  "Hee hee. This’ll be fun. I thought maybe I’d have to be YOUR knight."
*She says as she raises up her arm as if to make a muscle*
You:  "Yeah, if an evil rabbit comes after us, perhaps you can hug it into submission."
*Makiko immediately blushes a deep crimson and goes to put a hand to my mouth.*
Makiko:  "Shhh!"
Makiko:  "Don’t go announcing that!"
You:  "Makiko, I’m speaking in English. Even if any of our students were here I don’t think any of them would speak good enough English to understand. "
You:  "And if they did, I’d be kind of proud."
Makiko:  "Well yeah, but you’re not going to be the one to get teased."
You:  "You know Makiko, last time I checked, you WERE a girl."
*Makiko blushes even more furiously.*
Makiko:  "We agreed not to talk about that!"
You:  "Huh?"
*I pause for a moment.*
You:  "Oh. OH! I wasn’t referring to that! I …sorry."
*Makiko takes a deep breath.*
Makiko:  "You need to think about what you’re saying sometimes <USER>"
You:  "Right. Sorry."
You:  "I uh…I like your yukata by the way."
Makiko:  "Ah. Trying to take away the awkwardness with a compliment are we?"
You:  "Maybe."
Makiko:  "Well lucky for you, it worked."
*She lightly punches me in the shoulder.*
*I smirk.*
*However, after a few moments, Makiko hasn’t moved and keeps looking at me.*
You:  "What?"
Makiko:  "You’re always leaving me hanging there."
You:  "Wait. What?"
Makiko:  "Well...you know, I always hit you on the shoulder, but you never hit me back."
You:  "You want me to hit you in the shoulder?"
Makiko:  "Um. Yeah."
*She responds as if I asked the most obvious question in the world.*
*Slowly I put my fist into a ball and bring it over to Makiko’s shoulder*
*Despite her asking for it, I do my best to bring it down gently. Unfortunately though, at the last moment, my arm jerks, and I end up putting a bit more oomphf into it than I mean.*
*My fist connects with her shoulder with an audible ‘boof!’*
*I feel her shoulder give way slightly.*
*To my surprise though, Makiko smiles at me.*
Makiko:  "Ooh wow! That was a good one!"
Makiko:  "I was afraid you were gonna go easy on me or something."
*I give Makiko a confused smile.*
You:  "You’re strange Makiko. One minute you’re talking about princesses and the next you want me to punch you in the shoulder."
*She looks back at me, a confused look now on her own face*
Makiko:  "Is that a bad thing?"
*I shrug.*
You:  "No, you’re just…unique. I don’t know, I think It’s pretty cool."
*A goofy smile forms on Makiko’s face before she tries to quickly suppress it.*
Makiko:  "Yeah well, you’re kind of cool too."
*She punches me in the shoulder again. This time, a bit harder.*
*The sounds of the crowd begin to shuffle in as we end up getting pushed closer together.*
*I can see the entrance to the festival just ahead.*
Makiko:  "Um…hey."
*I hear Makiko say to me quietly.*
You:  "Hm?"
*I turn around.*
Makiko:  "I was just wondering if uh-"
Aina:  "Oi!!! Ha-ni!"
*I feel Aina latch onto my arm.*
Aina:  "Hiii!!"
*I look over and sure enough, a few feet away, Fumo and Kotone are standing near the entryway, waiting for us.*
*I glance back to Makiko*
You:  "Sorry, what were you going to say?"
*Makiko lets out a nervous laugh.*
Makiko:  "Hahah, ah, it’s nothing. Nothing."
*She then walks ahead.*
Makiko:  "Come on! Let’s check out the festival!"
*I raise an eyebrow.*
You:  "...Right."
*I turn the phone around which had the number ‘4’ typed on the screen.*
Kotone:  "Ah, would you look at that? Kotone wins. Not that I’m surprised."
You:  "Ah yes, the pinnacle of modesty that is Kotone."
Kotone:  "Well, there’s really no REASON to be modest at the moment is there?"
You:  "Well, I mean.. I guess not."
Kotone:  "And that was the correct response. Come on Mr. American."
*She reaches forward and grabs my hand, pulling me ahead.*
*I can tell that Fumo and Aina are doing their best to keep within a decent range so as not to get zapped.*
Kotone:  "Soo...?"
You:  "Sooo what?"
Kotone:  "My compliment? Did you forget already?"
You:  "Ah, right of course."
*I look her over in her yukata. I don’t want to admit it, but Kotone actually looks really adorable in it.*
*The design and color look like they were tailor made for her. *
*I honestly don’t know what the definitive ‘japanese girl’ looks like, but I think Kotone would come pretty close in the looks department.*
*I cross my own arms and pretend to scrutinize her outfit.*
You:  "I think your quirky personality is very charming."
*This completely seems to catch Kotone off guard.*
Kotone:  "Kk-what? You..you were supposed to compliment my outfit!"
You:  "Oh, I was? I thought I was just supposed to compliment YOU."
Kotone:  "It was implied!"
You:  "Ah. Well, what can I say, I’m just dumb Mr. American."
Kotone:  "I didn't say you were dumb."
*She stops walking a moment as she says this to me.*
Kotone:  "I didn’t ever say you were dumb."
*She repeats this softer and more mumbles it than anything.*
You:  "Sorry."
Kotone:  "Yeah well…."
*She trails off.*
You:  "Um.. I DO like the yukata on you."
Kotone:  "Ha. That was pretty weak."

*This seems to put Kotone’s usual smirk back on her face.*
Kotone:  "Okay, that was pretty good. And true."
*We walk a bit in silence as we continue forward.*
*I look down and notice Kotone’s hand is still in mine.*
You:  "You realize you’re still holding my hand right?"
*Kotone glances down and then back at me.*
Kotone:  "Is that a problem?"
*I shake my head.*
You:  "No, not really. I just mentioned it.. I don't know, in case you forgot."
*She turns back to me, her eyes a mix of confusion and amusement.*
Kotone:  "In case i….."
Kotone:  "Haha. you really are weird."
*The two of us walk in silence, Kotone still holding my hand, but using it to pull me ahead through the now growing crowd.*
Kotone:  "And hey. You um..you don’t look too bad yourself."
You:  "Ha, really? I thought you’d be the first one to call me out about how strange I looked."
*Kotone stops*
Kotone:  "What do you mean by that?"
You:  "I don’t know. I thought you’d be like “haha, you look so weird in Japanese clothing Mr. American’ or something like that."
*Kotone gives me a small smile.*
Kotone:  "The last thing you need is a crush to your self esteem. Besides. Like I said, it looks good on you. If it didn’t, I’d tell you."
You:  "Well that’s true."
Kotone:  "And just so you know, I expect that same level of honesty from you."
*I can feel Kotone’s hand start to become slightly sweaty. She seems to feel it too as she releases my hand.*
*She looks at me, her face slightly burning.*
You:  "What? No, I told you, it looks good."
*Kotone looks me in the eyes for a good few seconds, as is staring directly into my soul, before suddenly turning away, her smirk back on her face.*
Kotone:  "I thought so. Just making sure."
Aina:  "Oi! Ha-ni!"
*I hear Aina call for me a few feet away over at the entryway where the booths begin*
*I turn away to look at her, and by the time I look back, Kotone has already walked away from me to rejoin the others.*
*Oh geez. It’s going to be one of THESE days, isn’t it?*
*I turn the phone around to reveal a ‘1’*
*Seeing this, Aina jumps up in the air.*
Aina:  "Yatta!"
*As soon as she lands, she immediately rushes over to me and latches onto my arm.*
Aina:  "Ikimashou! Ha-ni!"
*I let out an exasperated sigh as I look to the other girls.*
*Kotone claps her hands together in mock prayer.*
Kotone:  "I will say a prayer for you."
You:  "Gee. Thanks."
*The rest of the girls begin to walk ahead as Aina holds onto my arm tightly, pressing herself up against and nuzzling into me.*
You:  "Ah…that’s a little….tight."
*Aina looks over at me, slightly confused.*
Aina:  "Hm? Doshita no?"
*Using my free hand I fuss around until I get my phone free as I’m unable to reach my book with her holding onto my arm.*
*I type in ‘tight’ into the translation*
You:  "Totemmo…..kitsui"
You:  "Hazukashii desu."
*Seeming to understand me, Aina blushes and loosens up slightly.*
Aina:  "Ah..go…gommene."
*A few moments of awkward silence follows*
Aina:  "No like?"
*I turn my head back to her.*
You:  "No like...what?"
Aina:  "Aina....embarrass?"
*I try to think of a tactful way to say this.*
You:  "...Sukoshi (a little)"
Aina:  "Ah...Naru Hodou."
*She puts her free hand beneath her lip as if lost in thought.*
Aina:  "Ha-ni no like…"
*She looks back to me*
Aina:  "Aina…no embarrass"
*At that moment, Aina’s entire demeanor changes. Like flipping a switch, suddenly, her expression becomes much more neutral and her hold on my arm becomes much more tenuous.*
*It's not just her expression however. Everything from her posture to even her smell is altered.*
*To be frank, such a drastic change is a little unnerving.*
*We walk in silence for a bit. I keep glancing over to her and notice her unchanging expression. It’s hard to explain it, but it looks…different. Not exactly cold, but definitely more mature.*
*It’s strange, but after seeing the excitable Aina for so long, this feels really strange.*
*After a few more moments of silence, Aina speaks up.*
Aina:  "This..good?"
*Aina says to me, her voice matching more to the day we first met. The deep air of maturity eking into every syllable.*
*Something about it though. Something…seems off.*
*Suddenly, I stop walking and pull my arm away from Aina. A bit more forceful than I mean to actually.*
*Not expecting for this to happen, Aina’s eyes open wide in shock.*
Aina:  "Eh? Nani?"
*I turn my head back to her.*
You:  "Iie."
*She looks at me confused.*
Aina:  "W..wakarimasen. Do shimashitaka?"
*I do my best to think of some Japanese words.*
You:  "Aina…jyunai."
Aina:  "Eh? Doiu imi desuka?"
*Ugh. She’s probably confused. Funny enough, the feeling’s mutual.*
*I scratch my head as I try to think of any way to communicate this.*
*I look at my phone to find a word*
You:  "Judouku Aina….Hen’na. (Serious Aina...strange.)"
*She tilts her head diagonally. Trying to understand.*
Aina:  "Hen’…na?"
Aina:  "But…no embarrass."
*She looks up at me, as if unsure of what I want.*
*I shake my head.*
You:  "No. I’m sorry."
*She looks up to me, confusion mired in her eyes.*
Aina:  "What..<USER> want Aina?"
*I cross my arms as I think to myself. What exactly WAS I trying to say? *
*I mean, quiet and matronly Aina is really weird. That’s for sure, but it’s hard to just…explain that. especially to Aina.*
You:  "Aina be….Aina."
*Aina mouths the words to herself, taking them in.*
Aina:  "Aina..be…aina."
*Her eyes shift around, looking on the floor as if the answer to this will be found there.*
Aina:  "Aina..be..aina."
*She says again, mumbling.*
*The crowd murmur begins to overtake Aina’s out loud thinking as she still has her finger to her chin.*
*Finally, she looks back up at me.*
Aina:  "Aina..be…aina…okay? <USER>-sama….like?"
*I nod*
You:  "Aina is Aina."
*Hearing this, Aina smiles widely.*
Aina:  "Mm! Aina is Aina!"
*She then proceeds to grab my arm again, this time with more enthusiasm.*
Aina:  "Ah! Ikimashou! Ha-ni!"
*Aina then nuzzles into my arm, even more affectionately than she did before.*
*However, she then stops and looks up at me.*
Aina:  "Be..Aina..Okay?"
*I let out a sigh as I give her a small smirk.*
You:  "Ii desu."
*Aina lets out a giggle as she nuzzles back into my arm.*
*Oh geez, what was it that Fumo said about encouraging her?*
*I let out a sigh.*
*Oh well.*
*As I glance back at Aina a thought crosses my mind.*
*Why did I think it was so weird when Aina reverted back to her more mature self? Why did it bother me so much?*
*As I begin to mull this over, I hear the familiar call of Makiko.*
*There, a few feet ahead of us are the other three at the entrance of the festival.*
*Seeing that she has to let go of me, she lets out a huff and begrudgingly does so.*
*As I finally reach the entryway to the festival, I realize just how popular this event is.*
*Rows upon rows of stalls adorn the walkway as dozens of vendors call things out in Japanese hawking their food as well as advertising their games.*
Aina:  "Oooh! Sugoiyone!"
Fumo:  "I have to admit, I didn’t think it would be this… big."
Makiko:  "Heh, well it IS the biggest festival of the year for this town."
*I look over to Makiko.*
You:  "So does that mean that businesses bring their wares here? Or it is all just random people?"
Makiko:  "Oh no, a lot of businesses around here have booths set up."
You:  "So why don’t you Kotone?"
*I ask, glancing over to her.*
*A large, smug toothy grin appears on her face.*
Kotone:  "Tsk tsk tsk"
*She tsks with her finger.*
Kotone:  "And who says I DON’T have a booth here?"
You:  "Oh, you do?"

*There’s a short silence.*
Fumo:  "Were you checking them out?"

*Kotone smirks as she crosses her arms.*
Kotone:  "Well, just so you know Mr. American, Mei and Koharu turned eighteen a few months ago."

Makiko:  "What did I do?!"

Makiko:  "Awesome!"
Fumo:  "Wait. Why am I hated?"

*I glance over as Makiko is explaining to Aina why I’m so upset.*
*Aina then proceeds to put a finger to her lips as if thinking and then look up to me.*
Aina:  "Oh! <USER> young like?"
You:  "That’s it. I’m walking over there."
Makiko:  "Hey! Wait for me!"
*As i walk away, the rest of them follow suit, following behind me.*
*I mean, it’s not like I can REALLY leave Aina or Fumo behind. Well, TECHNICALLY I could, but then I would have to answer the ever present lightning question. *
*My eyes begin to wander as I look around at all the different vendors.*
*Man, I have no idea what i'm even looking at.*

*She then lets out a sigh.*

Fumo:  "Well, considering the last time I was here on earth, the amount of people who are here now would have been the entire village, yes."

*Fumo shrugs lightly.*
Fumo:  "I suppose I don’t think about it that often. But seeing everyone in these Yukata, it…jogs some memories."

You:  "So um… did you want to do something?"
*Fumo shakes her head slightly.*
Fumo:  "I’m fine just looking at people. You should go and have fun with the others though."

Fumo:  "Yes, well I’m sorry I-"
You:  "No no, you don’t understand. That question was rhetorical."
Fumo:  "I’m sorry. Rhetorical?"

*Fumo shoots me over a look with a raised eyebrow.*
You:  "If you don’t have a preference, I’ll find something, but either way, I'm not leaving you here alone."

You:  "You know how terrible I am at letting things go."

*Slowly she unfolds her arms and outstretches a hand towards me.*

You:  "Great! Let’s go then!"

Fumo:  "H-hey! <USER>! Slow down!"

Fumo:  "Fine. I’m coming."
*I feel the tension on Fumo’s arms loosen slightly as she picks up the pace before finally catching up to me.*
Fumo:  "Okay, I’m walking with you. You can slow down."
You:  "All right. Fair enough."
*I slow down my pace.*
Fumo:  "What was that all about?"

Fumo:  "Don’t ‘if you say so’ me, I wasn’t sulking!"

Fumo:  "No you don’t! I can hear it in your voice."

Fumo:  "Yes I –"
*Suddenly, as if a light switch went off, Fumo cuts herself off, realizing what she’s doing.*
*She shakes her head and puts a hand to her face.*

Fumo:  "I still don’t understand why that matters to you."

Fumo:  "I’m more likely to believe the first option."

Fumo:  "Because I don’t see what you get out of seeing me smile."
You:  "Ah yes. Because seeing you smile is such a terrible thing."
Fumo:  "Now you’re twisting my words."
You:  "Oh. I am?"
You:  "I don’t think I am. After all, you said you wanted to go to that stall over there."
*I point to a random stall.*
*Fumo scoffs and points at another stall*
Fumo:  "I never said that. Besides, I was hoping to check out that one over…"
*Her finger falters as a look of shock replaces the irritated one on her face.*
*Slowly her hand moves down as a small blush crosses her face.*
Fumo:  "I can’t believe I just fell for that."
*I’m glad I can’t see my own stupid grin at this moment.*
You:  "Yeah, well, you did."
*Without even giving me a verbal response, Fumo lets out a small sigh and walks over towards the stall she had motioned towards a moment before.*
*I quickly see that she has lead us over to what appears to be a ring toss game.*

Fumo:  "Ring..toss?"

*I give a slight shrug*
You:  "We have them in America too."
