*A man in charge of the booth smiles and walks over to the two of us.*
Stall Owner:  "Kimi wa kekkon shite imasu ka?"
*I’m not certain what the man says, but for some reason it causes Fumo to blush profusely.*
*Fumo begins to fumble with some words to the man.*
*I think he said.. kekkon. Huh…I wonder….*
*Quickly I take out my phone and type in the word into the translation search engine.*
*I nearly burst out laughing as I read the translation reads off as ‘married’*
*Ooh. This is too good of an opportunity to pass up.*
*I lookup the translation of the sentence before turning back to the man.*
You:  "Hai. Watashi tachi wa kappuru desu."
*It takes every ounce of restraint I have to not bend over laughing as I catch Fumo’s reaction out of the corner of my eye. Her eyes grow to the size of dinner plates.*
*Before she comes back to her senses, I pay the man and take a few rings which I place into Fumo’s hands.*
You:  "Here you go, ‘wife.’"
*I say, giving her a smirk*
Fumo:  "I…uh…"
*She looks back from the man and then to me.*
Fumo:  "We’re going to have a talk about this in a minute."
*She says, doing her best to sound somewhat intimidating, but the deep crimson blush is making it far less impressive.*
You:  "Right right. You should try throwing those rings now though….honey."
*I say this right as she’s about to throw a ring, causing it to go wild and nearly hit the poor ring stand owner.*
Fumo:  "What..what is wrong with you?"
You:  "Whoa. You should pay more attention Fumo."
Fumo:  "Y-you keep distracting me!"

Fumo:  "Be quiet for a second."

You:  "Oh geez. Sorry Fumo. That was-"

You:  "Fumo?"
*I quickly turn my head around in search of her. *
*Luckily, I find her a few feet away, steadily moving further away.*
You:  "Hey, fumo! Wait up!"
*She doesn’t seem to hear me though as she continues to walk at a brisk pace.*
*I manage to catch up with her before she gets too far.*
*I glance around to make sure i'm not getting too far away from Aina. Thankfully I can see her at a nearby stall.*
*Before I can say anything to Fumo, she flips back to look at me.*
Fumo:  "What was that?"

Fumo:  "Don’t act stupid. You know. That whole “we’re a couple’ thing"

*I taper off as I place my hand behind my head*

Fumo:  "So, you think I’m upset because.. you think i'm too good for you?"
*I use this opportunity to look back at her, also confused.*
You:  "...Is..is that not right?"
*At that moment, I swear I can see a ghost of a smile appear on her face as she turns away to let out a sigh before whispering something under her breath*
Fumo:  "Baka ningen"

Fumo:  "Fine. I…I’ll forgive you this time."
*I breathe a sigh of relief.*
*Before I can say anything else though, Fumo speaks up again*
Fumo:  "Um-"

Fumo:  "Actually it’s not-"

Fumo:  "Well..yeah. it was meant to be."
You:  "Well then, thank you Fumo."
*I say, as I once again lead her back towards the stalls.*
*For the next few minutes, I have to suppress laughter as Fumo is unable to land a single ring.*
*The normally expressionless Fumo is starting to show signs of frustration as she throws another ring.*
*Once again, it bounces off.*
Fumo:  "Well, it’s official. It’s impossible."

Fumo:  "Okay, fine. And if you miss…"

Fumo:  "You have to wear that Yukata into town on your next day off."

*She holds out her hand.*

*I turn back around and look towards the pegs.*
*Please don’t be rigged. Please don’t miss.*

Stall Owner:  "SHOSHA!"

*Suddenly, Fumo’s free hand moves to her mouth as if to cover it.*
*To my sheer surprise, a hear a noise coming from her, and it’s one I never thought I’d hear from a Fumo.*
*Not a snicker, not a chuckle, but an honest to god laugh is coming out of her mouth.*
*It’s seemingly taking everything Fumo has to cover her mouth so as not to reveal herself laughing.*

*I move my finger over the image and click 'Delete'*

*Fumo suddenly stops.*
Fumo:  "Oh."
You:  "Ah, you see what I did there."
*She lets out an exasperated sigh*
You:  "Yeah, I have that effect on people. And apparently on spirits too."

Fumo:  "Come on, let’s go back to the others."
You:  "Ah. Yeah, right."
*As Fumo begins to lead the way, I can't help but smile as I walk behind her.*
*Yeah, totally worth it.*
*The four of us break off from each other slightly as everyone begins to look at all the different options*
*I begin to turn my gaze to all of the options when a voice interrupts my thoughts.*
Makiko:  "Hey!"
*I turn back around to see Makiko standing right behind me.*

Makiko:  "You just looked a little lost."

*Makiko leads me towards a booth that thankfully isn’t too far away from Aina and Fumo.*

Female Stall Owner:  "Aa, totemo sutekina fuufuudesune..."
*For some reason, Makiko blushes at the comment.*
Makiko:  "Ah! Iie, iie! Tomadachi desu! Fuufuu jyunaiyo!"
Female Stall Owner:  "A. Futari tomo kawaii ne "

You:  "Hey uh, Makiko, what are you talking about?"
*Makiko flushes and turns back to me.*
Makiko:  "Huh? Oh. Nothing, nothing! She’s just saying how good you look in your yukata."
You:  "Oh. Really?"
*I take out my phone and type a sentence into it for translation*
You:  "Anata mo yukata wa…totemmo kirei desune oneesan."
*This causes the woman to put her hands to her face and smile goofily.*
Female Stall Owner:  "Hontoni? Ah totemmo yasashi"
*She then turns to Makiko.*
Female Stall Owner:  "Jya...Tabun watashi wa kare ni tazunemasu.."
*It may be my imagination, but I swear for a second, a look of irritation pass her face.*
You:  "Oh. Was my Japanese that bad? Did I offend her or something?"
*Makiko turns back to me*
Makiko:  "Huh?"
*She shakes her head a bit before letting out a small laugh, removing the expression she had earlier.*
Makiko:  "Ha, ah. No no, it was fine. She was just telling me you’re very nice."
You:  "Oh. Good. Is my pronunciation getting better?"
Makiko:  "Hm? Oh. Yeah. absolutely."
*A small silence passes between us.*
Makiko:  "Right! Well um, let me show you how to play!"
*Makiko pays the woman and takes two sticks, handing me one.*
*I bring the stick closer to my face as I begin to investigate it.*
*The stick appears to have a small circle at the top with some sort of paper covering it. It doesn’t look nearly strong enough to catch a fish.*
You:  "Um..Makiko, this isn’t a net."
Makiko:  "Mm. Right. It’s paper."
You:  "Makiko, why are we dipping a paper net into the water? Isn’t it going to…you know..break? Because it’s paper? In water?"
Makiko:  "That’s the whole point!"
You:  "The point is to…break the net?"
Makiko:  "Haha of course not. Using a net would be way too easy. The point is to try to catch the fish without getting it too wet."
You:  "Is that even possible?"
Makiko:  "Sure it is!"
Makiko:  "Here! I’ll show you!"
*Makiko proceeds to roll up her sleeves*
Makiko:  "All right! Let’s do this!"
*Before I even have time to blink, Makiko dunks her net into the water. She does it though with such effort that I end up getting slightly splashed.*
*A moment later she pulls the net out to reveal a giant hole in it.*
Makiko:  "Aw. It got away!"
You:  "D..didn’t you just say you had to be more gentle with it?"
Makiko:  "Huh? I was."
*THAT was gentle?*
You:  "Okay then, I guess it’s my turn!"
*I roll up my own sleeves and, unlike Makiko, try to be a little gentler. *
*However, my movements end up being much slower and, just like hers, the paper becomes ripped before it even comes close to catching a fish.*
You:  "Huh..well that didn’t work."
Makiko:  "Well of course it didn’t, you were way too slow."
You:  "Well your fast maneuver didn’t exactly work either."
Makiko:  "That….that was just a practice try! I’ll definitely get it this time."
*She hands the woman some more money and she is handed another hoop.*
*I do the same.*
*This time however, we both attempt at once, with our different speeds.*
*Once again, we come up empty.*
You:  "I think we’re doing this wrong."
*Makiko however doesn’t seem to even notice me as her eyes are now fully fixated on the fish.*
*She pays the woman again and takes another stick.*
*I do the same*
*We try again and fail.*
*Again.*
*Again..*
*Again...*
*After about fifteen sticks, I am panting, all of them having failed without a single fish being scooped.*
*Makiko on the other hand, while not looking as physically exhausted as me, looks me to be more than a little frustrated.*
You:  "Makiko, I don’t think this is working."
*She doesn’t seem to notice this as she buys another one. And fails again.*
You:  "Hey, Makiko."
*No answer.*
*As she goes to move her hand up, I reach my own hand out and grab hers.*
You:  "Hey. Makiko."
*My touch seems to bring her out of whatever zone she was in and she turns to me, shocked.*
Makiko:  "Huh?"
*Her sight slowly falls to my hand as her face begins to turn a deep red.*
*Realizing this, I move my hand quickly away*
You:  "Oh! Sorry!"
You:  "Sorry. I shouldn’t have –"
*Makiko shakes her head, embarrassed.*
Makiko:  "No, no! It’s…it’s all right."
*She takes her hand back and holds it in her free hand gently.*
Makiko:  "Sorry about that. I can get a little…too focused at times."
*I let out a soft chuckle.*
You:  "I noticed."
*I cross my arms as I look over at the fish.*
You:  "I don’t think this is working."
*Makiko crosses her own arms as a grimace crosses her face.*
Makiko:  "I think you’re right."
You:  "Any ideas?"
*Makiko unfurls her arms and looks at the hoop.*
*A short silence follows.*
Makiko:  "Well, I think you’re definitely going a bit too slow and gentle."

*Help The Child":
*Don't Help The Child":
*No, it’s not my responsibility. Besides, if the parent walks by and sees a large American talking to their small child, they might think I’m doing something weird. *
*I’m sure they’ll find their parent soon enough.*

You:  "You two are these important spirits for Japan right? I mean, the result of your little contest here is going to determine the fate of the whole country for the next 300 years. "
You:  "So um..who’s doing your jobs while you’re here?"
*Fumo raises an eyebrow as she looks over to me.*
Fumo:  "I don’t believe we ever said we WEREN’T doing our jobs."
You:  "Wait. You have been? How?"
Fumo:  "I can see why humans would be confused by this."
*She points to her head.*
Fumo:  "Unlike the human brain, we can process multiples things at once. For example, right now, I’m receiving about fifteen messages from humans."
Fumo:  "Judging on the situation, we can channel a response."
You:  "Wow that sounds…exhausting."
*Fumo shrugs*
Fumo:  "I’m used to it."
You:  "Okay, then give me an example."
*Fumo stops walking and lets out a sigh.*
Fumo:  "Fine."
Fumo:  "Let’s see. Okay, there’s a couple in Hokkaido who just prayed to me that they’re not pregnant."
You:  "Okay…and..how are you going to respond?"
Fumo:  "Well, I have to factor in several things such as probability, their own fertility and other variables."
You:  "Can’t you just….choose?"
Fumo:  "That wouldn’t exactly keep the balance if I decided on a whim, now would it?"
Fumo:  "Besides, there’s Aina’s side to think of too."
You:  "So does prayer to you affect anything?"
Fumo:  "Of course it does. Let’s say that the scales are balanced even at 50/50. They pray to me, I can choose to tilt it in my favor. They pray to her, the opposite."
You:  "So…where does this couple lay?"
Fumo:  "40/60 in favor of Aina."
You:  "Ouch. Well that seems unfair."
*Fumo shrugs*
You:  "Do you ever just ask Aina?"
Fumo:  "Why?"
You:  "I mean, to change it?"
Fumo:  "Why would I?"
*I guess that makes sense. She probably deals with thousands upon thousands of people per day. Why would a single person’s fate affect her?*
*Seeing my face, Fumo lets out a sigh and then says something in Japanese to Aina.*
Aina:  "Eh? Honto? Hai! Okay!"
*Fumo turns back to me and gives me a shrug*
Fumo:  "There you go. Now they won’t be."
You:  "Seriously? That’s it?"
Fumo:  "What? Were you expecting something big?"
You:  "I mean..I guess not."
*Fumo sighs*
Fumo:  "Come on, we need to keep walking."
*Geez. It never occurred to me that these two were doing their jobs all this time. *
*I guess after doing it for so many thousands of years it just becomes second nature. But still, it must be absolutely exhausting.*
*As Fumo continues up the hill, I move over to Aina.*
You:  "Hey uh, aina. Um..thanks."
*Hearing this, she gives me a broad smile.*
Aina:  "ii desu yo, Honey!"
*She floats around me in a circle a few times before floating on ahead.*
*it’s so strange. The more I talk to aina, it almost seems the more childish she gets. However, I have to say, this version of Aina is oddly charming in a way.*
*Finally, after what seems like several hours, when in actuality it’s only closer to one, we arrive at the shrine.*
*The shrine is at the top of a large hill, and to be honest, its seen better days. It looks a bit overrun by weeds which crunch under my feet, due to the cold.*
*As we reach the top, Fumo turns to me.*
Fumo:  "Okay, so from here only one of us can go in at a time. And, unfortunately, you can’t come in."
You:  "Oh. Well okay, I understand that. So I’ll just wait out here with whoever isn’t inside."
Fumo:  "That’s fine."
You:  "So…who’s going inside first?"
Fumo:  "Right. That’ll be…"
*Aina Goes First":
*Fumo Goes First":
Fumo:  "Aina. She needs the recharge more than I do."
You:  "Sounds about right."
You:  "So I’ll stay out here with you Fumo."
*Fumo nods.*
Fumo:  "That’s fine."
*Fumo points to the shrine.*
Fumo:  "Aina. Ite"
Aina:  "Hai hai! Ikimasu!"
*Aina smiles as she begins to float towards the shrine.*
*About halfway she stops and turns back to me*
Aina:  "Sugu modotte kimasu! Honey!"
*And with that, Aina turns around and disappears into the shrine.*
*As Aina disappears, that leaves just Fumo and I alone outside.*
You:  "So um..you’re not cold?"
*I ask Fumo, who’s wearing her usual kimono and nothing else.*
*Fumo shakes her head.*
Fumo:  "No, we don’t get cold in spirit form."
You:  "I figured that was the case, I just wanted to make sure."
Fumo:  "How about you? Are you cold?"
You:  "No, I’m all right."
*I say this as a particularly strong gust of wind comes and blows through me, causing me to shiver.*
*Fumo crosses her arms as she lightly shakes her head.*
Fumo:  "You don’t have to prove anything to me <USER>. I can see you’re cold. Come over here and sit down."
*She says, gesturing to a stone bench.*
*Seeing no real reason to argue, I make my way over to the bench and sit down.*
*As I take a seat, I look up at Fumo awkwardly standing near me.*
You:  "You going to have a seat too?"
Fumo:  "It’s all right. I can stand."
You:  "Yeah well it’s awkward for me. Could you please have a seat?"
*Fumo looks over towards me before giving a relenting sigh.*
Fumo:  "All right."
*Fumo slowly moves over towards the bench before sitting down a respectable distance from me.*
*The two of us sit there for a few moments, nothing being said between us as we stare off into the distance, taking in the mountain view.*
*As I look at the scenery, my eyes gradually pan over to Fumo. As she becomes fully in focus, I realize that she’s glancing over at me too.*
*As soon as we realize this, we look away from each other.*
You:  "So um..it’s pretty here."
*I say, trying to break the awkward tension.*
Fumo:  "Mm. Yes. It’s nice."
*I let out a mock gasp.*
You:  "You didn’t say ‘it’s not bad’. I don’t know if my heart can take it."
*Fumo shoots me a look.*
Fumo:  "Yes well, someone told me that I should express myself more."
You:  "Oh. Well they sound like a nice person."
Fumo:  "They’re all right."
*We fall into silence again as we look out over the scenery.*
*Once again though, I speak up.*
You:  "Back when I was at that summer retreat, you asked me if I missed America. But I never asked you. Do you miss the spirit world?"
*Fumo puts her hands on the stone bench and looks down.*
Fumo:  "Not particularly."
You:  "Well I guess being there for thousands of years could make it a bit boring."
*Fumo nods lightly.*
Fumo:  "It’s not just that though. Though, it’s difficult to explain."
You:  "I can imagine. Do you have any sort of family there? Do spirits even HAVE family?"
*Fumo shakes her head side to side softly.*
Fumo:  "No, well, I don’t at least. Unless you consider Aina to be that."
You:  "Do you at least talk with the other spirits?"
Fumo:  "At times."
*She looks over to me and raises an eyebrow.*
Fumo:  "You certainly are inquisitive today."
You:  "Is that a bad thing?"
Fumo:  "No."
*She leaves it on that simple word before turning back.*
You:  "So….are there a lot of people praying to you right now?"
*Fumo slightly nods.*
Fumo:  "A few."
You:  "What are they saying?"
Fumo:  "It varies. There’s a lot of yelling."
You:  "Yelling?"
Fumo:  "Mhm. Some are scared…others are mad at me."
You:  "How can they be mad at you if they don’t even know who you are?"
*Fumo pulls up her knees to her face and shifts her eyes to me.*
Fumo:  "Haven’t you ever cursed up to the heavens at god for ‘doing something to you’?"
*I make a face as she says this.*
*Yeah, there definitely have been ‘cursing god’ moments during my life. I just didn’t realize anybody actually heard those.*
You:  "Right. Well, I certainly won’t do THAT ever again."
*Fumo gives me a soft smile.*
Fumo:  "I’m sure whoever you’re yelling at would appreciate that."
You:  "If that’s the case, doesn’t it ever get overwhelming? So many people yelling at you?"
*Fumo shrugs, her knees still pulled up to her chest*
Fumo:  "You get used to it."
*An awkward silence falls between the two of us.*
You:  "Well if it helps, I wouldn’t yell at you."
Fumo:  "It doesn’t bother me much anymore. It’s almost like fuzz in the back of my head."
Fumo:  "But, thank you. I get your intent."
*As I watch Fumo curled up in a ball, I feel something deep inside me. Like..a desire to protect her. Before I even think about it, I remove my backpack and unzip it.*
*Fumo continues to look down, not even glancing over at me as I proceed to grab the item I’m looking for.*
*I quickly pull out an extra coat and lay it across Fumo.*
*This seems to shock her, but she doesn’t attempt to remove it.*
*She merely shifts her eyes over to look at me.*
Fumo:  "I told you that spirits don’t get cold."
You:  "Yep, you sure did."
Fumo:  "Then why did you just put a coat on me?"
You:  "I don’t know, I just felt like doing it."
*Fumo looks back forward*
Fumo:  "You really ARE strange."
*Another moment of silence passes*
Fumo:  "I’ve been around you for nine months now and I still don’t understand you."
You:  "I could say the same about you."
Fumo:  "Yes, well it’s not the same. You’re-"
*She stops herself.*
You:  "I’m human."
*She lowers her mouth back to her arms.*
You:  "It’s all right. I know compared to you guys I’m like a child or something. I understand. It’s not an insult."
*Her multicolored eyes move to me.*
Fumo:  "Because you don’t GET offended, right?"
You:  "You remembered."
Fumo:  "Of course I did."
You:  "For what it’s worth Fumo, I’m glad you’re here. I don’t know what I would have done without you"
Fumo:  "Because of Aina, right?"
You:  "That too."
*Fumo looks back out front. As she does, something small catches her attention.*
*A snowflake slowly falls and lands on her knee.*
*As if confused, she extends her finger out and picks up the snowflake, and begins to inspect it as if it were something of great import.*
*To my surprise, the snowflake doesn’t melt on her finger. Instead, it merely stays on her finger, perfectly balanced.*
*A thought then comes to my head.*
You:  "Fumo, is this your first time seeing snow?"
Fumo:  "Huh?"
*Fumo turns her head to me.*
You:  "Haven’t you seen snow before?"
*Fumo turns her attention upwards, noticing the slowly falling snowflakes, and then turns back to me.*
Fumo:  "I’ve seen snow before. Just…"
You:  "Not in the human world?"
*Fumo nods.*
*I let out a soft chuckle.*
Fumo:  "What’s so funny?"
You:  "It’s just…you’re not really experiencing snow like that."
Fumo:  "Like what?"
You:  "Well, for one, you shouldn’t be able to hold a snowflake like that."
*I gesture to her, the snowflake still balanced on her finger.*
Fumo:  "Does it really matter?"
You:  "Well, it’s a bit of a different experience."
Fumo:  "It is?"
You:  "If you don’t believe me, try it. Turn physical for a moment."
*Fumo gives one more look to me before shrugging.*
Fumo:  "All right."
*Fumo closes her eyes for a moment as a flash of black light covers her. A moment later the same person is sitting in the seat.*
*The snowflake quickly melts on her finger as suddenly, Fumo’s eyes open wide. She shivers as she pulls the coat close to her.*
Fumo:  "Oh my goodness. IT actually IS cold."
*I let out a small laugh.*
You:  "I knew the jacket was a good idea."
*Fumo looks over to me without turning her head.*
Fumo:  "Well if it weren’t for you, I wouldn’t have turned physical."
You:  "That’s true. So I guess it cancels it out."
Fumo:  "mm."
*Fumo gives me a noncommittal noise as her attention is already drawn to the sky.*
*The snow has begun to pick up as the sky is soon filled with white stars falling to the ground.*
*Snowflake after snowflake falls from the sky and lands on her face and ears, causing her ears to twitch.*
*Slowly, Fumo stands up and begins to walk through the falling snow*
*A sound beneath her feet causes her ears to twitch again. As she looks down she sees that the snow has begun to pile up and the crunch is the snow beneath her feet.*
*As the snow picks up, I notice more snow beginning to fall in the gap between her neck and the coat.*
You:  "Oh hang on a second…one more…thing."
*I say this as I rummage through my bag.*
*Within a few moments I pull out my prize, a scarf.*
*I take a few steps over towards Fumo and offer her the scarf.*
You:  "Here. This will help."
*Fumo looks back from the scarf and then over to me.*
Fumo:  "Oh. …thank you."
*She slowly takes the scarf and wraps it around her neck.*
Fumo:  "How…how does it look?"
*She asks me in a way that…well, almost sounded human.*
*Looking back at her, the only word I can use to describe the image is..well adorable.*
*Fumo’s red and silver eyes are staring back at me from the side, her cheeks red from the cold. Every few moments I see her foxlike ear twitch only adding to the cuteness.*
*For just a moment, I feel my heart speed up.*
You:  "It …it looks good."
*Fumo turns her head back.*
Fumo:  "Oh..that’s good."
*She looks back to me.*
Fumo:  "You think of everything don’t you."
*I snicker.*
You:  "Not really, I just try to be prepared when I can."
*Fumo pulls the scarf closer to her face.*
Fumo:  "This scarf smells like you, you know."
You:  "Oh. Sorry about that."
*Fumo shakes her head as I can swear she goes a bit redder.*
Fumo:  "It’s all right. I don’t mind."
You:  "So um…what do you think of the snow?"
*Fumo turns her head upwards to take a look at the snow once more as if to inspect it.*
Fumo:  "it’s cold…and yet…it’s beautiful in a way."
Fumo:  "You were right. It’s a completely different experience like this. In my spirit form it’s like I’m watching the snow through a window. But.."
*She reaches up and holds out her hand. A snowflake falls on it and disappears quickly.*
*She looks over to me*
Fumo:  "Thank you for showing me this."
*I give her a small smile back.*
You:  "You’re welcome."
*A few moments of congenial silence pass by as she continues to look at the snow.*
*Turning around I move back to the stone bench, wiping away the snow that has piled up on it.*
*After it has been sufficiently cleaned, I take a seat.*
*Following my lead, Fumo joins me on the bench.*
*I notice as she rubs her arms over her coat, her coldness evident.*
You:  "You can turn back to spirit form if you’d like."
*Fumo merely keeps looking at the snow, a small smile on her face.*
Fumo:  "In a few minutes."
You:  "Well, in THAT case…."
*I say as I reach back into my backpack.*
*A few moments later, I come back with my prize, a portable umbrella.*
*I open it up and place it over our heads.*
*Fumo at first looks at me in slight shock, before her look of shock is replaced with a soft smile.*
Fumo:  "What DON’T you have in that bag?"
*She asks, gently teasing.*
You:  "Is this okay?"
*Fumo nods.*
Fumo:  "Yes, thank you."
*For the next minute or so, the two of us sit there, merely staring at the falling snow, not a word between us.*
*At that moment, the world seems empty. As if we’re the only two people on the planet. There’s a certain serenity to it, and in a word, it’s beautiful.*
*The one to break the moment is actually Fumo, who lets out a slight yawn.*
Fumo:  "Oh. Sorry."
You:  "Right. You haven’t recharged yet. You must be exhausted having to keep this form."
*Fumo shakes her head sleepily.*
Fumo:  "I’m okay."
*I feel my heart race slightly as I look back at her.*
You:  "If you’d like, you can rest against me. I’ll let you know when Aina’s done."
Fumo:  "N-no. that’s all right. I-"
You:  "Fumo, I’m the one who made you waste even more energy turning physical. I’d feel much better if you took a bit of a rest."
*Fumo opens her mouth as if she’s going to say something before closing it and letting out a slight sigh.*
Fumo:  "All right. I’ll only need a moment."
*I shake my head.*
You:  "Take all the time you need."
*Slowly, tentatively, I feel Fumo’s head lean against me.*
*I feel my body run hot as she does. Even through the coats, my heartrate increases exponentially.*
*Her head against my shoulder, the two of us sit there in silence for another minute.*
*Finally, Fumo speaks up, so quietly I can barely hear her.*
Fumo:  "Hey, <USER>?"
You:  "Hm?"
Fumo:  "Thank you."
*The voice is so soft, so quiet, and yet, I can feel my heart breaking as she utters it. *
*It’s the most simple ‘thank you' I’ve ever heard. The way she says it, the sheer innocence of it, it takes everything I have to stop myself from crying.*
You:  "You’re-"
*My voice cracks.*
You:  "You’re welcome."
*I say quietly, this time more certain.*
*My answer is a steady breath close to my ear.*
*Without moving my shoulder, I turn my head slowly to take a look at Fumo.*
*What I see next takes my breath away.*
*There, lying against me, Fumo is completely asleep.*
*Her eyes are closed and a steady in and out breath is coming from her mouth.*
*She’s leaning most of her weight against me, but I can hardly even feel it.*
*I’ve noticed her weightlessness in the past, but here, laying against me, asleep and completely vulnerable, it’s a sight I don’t think I’ll ever be able to forget.*
*I feel Fumo’s ears twitch slightly as she lets out a soft moan.*
*Oh. She must be dreaming.*
*I raise my arm slowly, carefully, as I move it towards Fumo’s sleeping form. I open my hand and place it on her head, rubbing it gently.*
*As I do, the moaning stops, and a small, yet noticeable smile appears on her face.*
*I give a soft smile of my own as I turn back and look at the snow.*
*Staring at the beauty of the scene ahead of me, I feel content, but, at the same time, terrified.*
*I know that in a few months Fumo will be leaving me. This will be our first, and last new Year’s together.*
*As the weeks and months have progressed, the thought of the impending end of the school year has been filling me with dread. For a reason I couldn’t put my finger on.*
*It isn’t until this moment that I realize what that is. Despite all the calamity these two have put me through, I already consider them family.*
*But, not only that, there’s one more thing that’s causing a knot to grow in my stomach as I sit there in that snowy landscape.*
*A thought that I didn’t realize what is was until just this very moment.*
*And the fact that I can truly put a name to this frightens me more than anything in the world.*
*I don't want them to go.*
Fumo:  "Me. I need less energy than her, so I’ll go first."
You:  "Oh. Right. Okay. That makes sense."
*Fumo turns to Aina.*
Fumo:  "Aina. Stay."
*Fumo points her finger down.*
Fumo:  "I go."
*She gestures to herself and then to the shrine.*
*Aina nods emphatically and puts up a mock salute*
Aina:  "Ryokai! Fumo-chan!"
*With an eyeroll, Fumo heads off towards the shrine, leaving the two of us behind.*
*Huh. Well, this is awkward.*
*As soon as she’s sure that Fumo is out of range she turns to me.*
Aina:  "Hi honey! How you?"
*I put my finger to my temple as if doing this will help the Japanese come better.*
You:  "Genki..desu..demo..samui. (I’m good. But cold)."
Aina:  "Eh? Honey Cold? I warm…"
*She holds out her arms towards me, inviting me in.*
*I raise my eyebrow.*
*Aina puts down her arms and giggles.*
Aina:  "Joke. Is joke."
You:  "Right."
You:  "Aina not cold?"
*Aina shakes her head*
Aina:  "Aina no cold. Seishin no cold."
*Seishin?*
*I pull out my translation book.*
*Seishin..seishin…oh! Spirit*
You:  "Seishin samui…jyunai? (spirit cold..not?)"
*Aina giggles*
Aina:  "Hai. I understand. Japanese..no good. But I get"
*I laugh a bit in spite of myself.*
You:  "Nihongo wa…muzukashii desune.. (Japanese is difficult)"
Aina:  "Sou desu! ..English hard."
You:  "Sorry."
*Aina’s ears twitch as she tilts her head to me.*
Aina:  "Why <USER> sorry?"
You:  "Nihongo ha totemmo..warui desu. (My japanese is bad)"
*A warm smile appears on Aina’s face as she comes over and takes my hand in hers.*
Aina:  "Son'na koto yori, anata ga sutekinahitodearu koto ga daijidesu"
Aina:  "Mochiron, watashi ga nani o itte iru no ka wakaranai to omoimasuga, sorede ii nodesu "
*As she says this complete stretch of complicated Japanese I look at her confused.*
You:  "W..wakarimasen."
*Aina merely giggles.*
Aina:  "That okay."
*She then proceeds to let go of my hand.*
*Aina moves back and puts her hands behind her back as she smiles.*
*There’s still so much I don’t know about aina. I only just now found out that she’s even still doing her job.*
*I can’t imagine having to do an entire job in the back of my head 24/7 while living my life as well.*
*I suppose, now’s a good time to ask some questions.*
You:  "Hey Aina."
*She looks over to me*
*I pull out my book and look up some things.*
You:  "San..hyakku-nen..akachan. doko…akachan? (three hundred years..baby. where baby?)"
*I cringe at just how badly I’m mangling the Japanese language.*
*Mercifully, Aina seems to understand.*
*Although, upon hearing this, her large smile soon transforms into a much sadder one.*
Aina:  "Ah… eto…dead."
You:  "Oh. I..i’m sorry."
*Aina shakes her head.*
*She then holds out her hands as if holding a child.*
Aina:  "Aina…have..baby. Then…aina…not baby."
*She makes a poof gesture*
*I cross my arms in confusion.*
You:  "Wakarimasen."
*Aina puts her hands on her hips as if trying to think of something.*
*She eventually gestures to me.*
Aina:  "Book. Give book."
*I nod and hand her the translation book.*
*She skims through it and looks at it. Seemingly satisfied she nods.*
Aina:  "I baby have. Baby turn…..eto…energy."
*She says as she tries to think of the word.*
You:  "Oh."
*I think I understand. She has the baby, and it sounds like shortly afterwards, it turns into…some kind of energy? At least I think that’s the general gist.*
*Wow. That’s..i don’t really have anything I can say in response to that.*
You:  "I’m…I’m sorry."
*Aina smiles kindly at me.*
Aina:  "Not you. Honey no apologize."
You:  "Um…"
You:  "If baby not…turn energy… you want baby?"
*As soon as I ask this question, Aina stops for a moment. All sense of smile removed from her face.*
Aina:  "Aina..want?"
Aina:  "Aina want…no important."
*She crosses her arms and looks away from me, a sad look on her face.*
You:  "if it important?"
*For the first time in a long while, all sense of wonder and joy seem to leave Aina’s expression. Instead it’s replaced of one of exhaustion and melancholy.*
Aina:  "I tired."
You:  "Now?"
*I ask, slightly confused.*
*Aina shakes her head.*
Aina:  "No..no now."
You:  "Oh."
*I say in understanding.*
Aina:  "Everyone Aina! Baby! Baby important! Not Aina."
Aina:  "It so bad Aina important?"
*Her face seems to contort into a look of distraught as the words get louder and louder.*
*Before I realize what I’m doing I make my way over closer to Aina.*
Aina:  "I..i so tired.."
*It’s at this point I can see tears start to form in the corner of her eyes, it obvious she’s trying to hold them back.*
*Taking one more step forward, I open my arms and enfold Aina in them. My arms easily overtake her.*
*This catches Aina off guard completely as she stops talking.*
Aina:  "Eh?"
You:  "It okay Aina. Ii desuyo."
*I can hear a small sniff come from Aina, followed by a silence.*
Aina:  "Arigatou."
*After a few moments pass, I go to move my arms.*
Aina:  "Eto!"
*She says suddenly.*
Aina:  "Little more..okay?"
*She asks, so quietly I barely hear it.*
Aina:  "Just…little."
*With her voice sounding so frail I honestly can’t turn her down.*
You:  "Yeah, that’s okay."
*And for just a few moments, the two of us merely stand there, as I do my best to comfort Aina.*
Aina:  "Arigatou. Anata wa..tottemo yasashi."
*The moment seemingly lost in time finally ends and she releases me.*
*As she does, her normal chipper face is back on.*
Aina:  "Hai! Arigatou! Ima wa totemmo genki!"
*She then moves off of me and gives me a big smile.*
*As I return the smile, I feel something cold fall on my face.*
*Apparently Aina notices this too as she looks up.*
*Sparkles seem to appear in her eyes as she does.*
Aina:  "Ahh! Yuki!!! Sugoiyo!"
Aina:  "Look! Yuki!"
*She begins to laugh as she spins around as the snow begins to cascade down with all the free abandon of a child.*
*Looking at her, it reminds me of that time at the retreat when I saw her spinning around without a care in the world.*
*Even back then she had told me just how busy she always was, and at the time, I didn’t quite understand it. But…I think I do a little better now.*
*I suppose if I had thousands of people in my head all the time, I might be a little eccentric too. …well, a little MORE eccentric.*
*Having watched her dance for a few minutes, I notice that the snow has begun to pile up quickly, coming down much faster than I anticipated.*
*As Aina continues to twirl around, an idea pops into my head.*
*Bending down I pick up a scoop of snow and pack it into a ball.*
*Then, as Aina makes a twirl, I toss the ball, and it lands directly on her face.*
*A shocked Aina stops dancing and blinks in confusion.*
*I let out a laugh at her expression as I pack another ball.*
