*I hand her the bottle back.*
You:  "Thanks."
*However, Makiko doesn’t seem to be reacting as she stares at me, a blush appearing on face as she looks down at the bottle.*
Makiko:  "Kan..setsu.. ki-"
*Makiko begins to whisper.*
You:  "Sorry, you say something?"
Makiko:  "W-what? Oh. No, just mumbling to myself. Hahaha!"
*She adds what sounds like a nervous laugh at the end.*
Makiko:  "Well, it’s only fair that if you got to taste mine then I can have some of yours!"
*She says as she points to my drink.*
You:  "Yeah, that sounds fair. Here you go."
*I hand her the drink.*
Makiko:  "Right! Thanks!"
*She takes the bottle and slowly moves it to her lips.*
*However, she then stops.*
You:  "Something wrong?"
Makiko:  "What? No, not at all!"
*Then, as if preparing herself mentally, grips the bottle tighter and then slams back a swig.*
*I let out a snort-like laugh.*
You:  "It’s not tequila Makiko. It’s warm apple juice."
Makiko:  "Huh? Oh yeah… right. Right."
*She hands me back the bottle.*
*We spend the next two or so hours experiencing the different attractions and visiting the different stores.*
*Despite it being a holiday it’s not that packed so we manage to get everything we want to do done in a pretty short amount of time.*
*Both us agree that we have seen everything we wanted to and head towards the exit.*
*Arriving back at the train station, I turn to Makiko.*
You:  "Well, I guess that means it’s your turn. Where are we going?"
*Makiko smiles toothily at me.*
Makiko:  "Hee hee. Oh no. You wouldn’t tell me, that means that I’m not going to tell you."
*I give her a smirk in return.*
You:  "Okay, that’s fair. Lead on."
*Having left the park, Makiko seems to have regained her confidence back, especially that now that she’s going to be the one in charge.*
*Dutifully I follow behind her as we continue to walk.*
*After a few minutes, Makiko turns back to me.*
Makiko:  "The good news is, the place I wanted to take you is in Tokyo too so it’s not going to take a long time."
You:  "Oh, well that worked out well."
*Makiko doesn’t say much as the leads me through the busy streets of Tokyo to a subway. We only take a few stops before we get off.*
*After taking a few more steps, Makiko smiles and turns back to me.*
Makiko:  "Okay, here we are!"
*Glancing over behind her, I see what appears to be a large outdoor ice skating rink.*
*I look back at her with a smarmy look on my face as I cross my arms.*
You:  "So, ice skating, huh?"
*Makiko smiles back at me.*
You:  "I’m intrigued. What lead you to believe I liked ice skating?"
Makiko:  "Hee hee. You mentioned it at the festival."
*This actually catches me off guard.*
You:  "You remembered that? I mentioned it once like five months ago."
*Makiko places her hands on her hips and puffs out her chest with pride.*
Makiko:  "I have a pretty good memory."
You:  "I’d say."
*It’s then that Makiko grabs my hand suddenly.*
Makiko:  "Well come on, we can’t stand here all day!"
*With Makiko’s impressive strength, she pulls me along towards the popup area to rent ice skates.*
*At first I’m worried they won’t even have my shoe size, but luckily they seem to have predicted they would get a lot of foreigners and they give me the largest size they have, which snugly fit.*
*Tentatively, I step onto the ice.*
*I manage to not immediately fall on my butt, so that’s a plus.*
*It’s been at least a year since I went ice skating last, but thankfully, for the most part, it’s like riding a bike and soon, I’m at least somewhat comfortably moving around the park.*
*Makiko on the other hand..isn’t doing so well.*
*I nearly laugh out loud as I watch Makiko holding onto the side of the makeshift rink, barely keeping herself standing.*
You:  "Hey uh…Makiko, you doing all right over there?"
Makiko:  "What? Oh. Haha, yeah I’m fine, I’m fine!"
*She gives me an awkward smile and slips again, catching herself on the wall.*
*I cross my arms as I give her a knowing smirk.*
You:  "You’ve never ice skated before, have you?"
Makiko:  "What? Pff no. of course I have! I’ve-"
*She nearly slips again but catches herself.*
Makiko:  "I’ve skated-"
You:  "Makiko. You have one of the worst poker faces I’ve ever seen in my life. I know this is your first time ice skating. It’s so obvious it’s painful."
Makiko:  "No- i…."
*Makiko lets out a sigh.*
Makiko:  "Okay fine, yes it’s my first time."
You:  "Well there’s no shame in that. Why were you trying to lie? Terribly I might add."
Makiko:  "That’s..I…"
*She mumbles something which I can’t understand.*
*I let out a sigh and extend my hand.*
You:  "Here, let me help."
Makiko:  "What? Haha, no, no it’s fine. I can figure this out, just-"
*Realizing this is going nowhere I reach out and grab Makiko’s hand that’s attempting to shoo me away.*
You:  "Here. Give me your other hand."
*Without much resistance she slides her other hand into mine.*
You:  "All right, now lets get you your balance. Try standing up straight."
*I give Makiko a reassuring smile.*
You:  "Don’t worry, I won’t let you go."
*Makiko, who is now very bright red, nods quietly as she attempts to stand up.*
*After a few tries and me having to hoist her up, she seems to have gotten the hang of it.*
You:  "Okay, you think you've got it?"
Makiko:  "I..I think so."
*I slowly start to move my hands from hers, only for her to latch on tightly.*
Makiko:  "Wait! Nononono don’t let go!"
*Her strong reaction catches me by surprise.*
Makiko:  "S-sorry"
You:  "No, it’s fine. But it’s going to be a bit awkward to try to skate with me holding both your hands like this."
*I slowly begin to try to move one of my hands away.*
You:  "I’ll hold onto one of your hands okay? If you start to fall I’ll catch you."
Makiko:  "O-okay."
*Makiko responds in a very un-Makiko level of uncertainty.*
*Despite myself, I begin to laugh.*
Makiko:  "W-what? What’s so funny?"
You:  "Sorry you just sound really flustered"
Makiko:  "W-well I don’t want to fall!"
You:  "And yet you’re the one who wanted to go ice skating."
Makiko:  "I figured it would be a lot like roller skating!"
You:  "It’s all right. I’m just teasing you."
Makiko:  "Oh! Right..right."
You:  "I actually think it’s kind of cute."
Makiko:  "C-cute?"
Makiko:  "Oh! Oh right, you’re teasing me. Haha!"
*Well to be honest, I wasn’t, but me saying that seemed to make Makiko a bit uncomfortable, so I shouldn’t press the issue.*
You:  "I’m going to start moving. Okay Makiko?"
Makiko:  "Huh? Oh. Oh sure."
*Slowly, I begin to skate, gently pulling Makiko along.*
Makiko:  "Whoa!"
You:  "It’s all right, I’ve got you."
*I make sure to keep my tone reassuring and calm as I slowly pull her*
Makiko:  "You’re not going to let go are you?"
You:  "Of course not."
Makiko:  "Okay. B-because you better not do that thing when you say you’re not going to let me go and then do!"
You:  "I promise I won’t let you go Makiko. Now come on!"
*Makiko takes a deep breath*
Makiko:  "Eh. Ganbattayo."
*She mumbles under her breath.*
You:  "Hai. Ganbattane, Makiko."
*Makiko looks back to me in shock.*
*I return with a smirk.*
You:  "Don’t be too surprised. An old dog can learn a trick or two still."
Makiko:  "I-I wasn’t thinking that."
You:  "You’re still a terrible liar."
Makiko:  "And you’re not old."
You:  "Oh, well thank you."
You:  "I have to say, I’m a little impressed that you remembered that I liked ice skating."
Makiko:  "Well, it seemed important to you."
*I let out a soft chuckle.*
You:  "You just keep on surprising me Makiko."
Makiko:  "Hee. Well I hope in a good way."
You:  "Well yeah, of course."
*As we’re talking, Makiko seems to be noticing that she’s skating less and less until finally, she seems lost in the conversation with me.*
You:  "By the way,"
Makiko:  "Hm?"
You:  "You realize you’ve been skating, right?"
*Suddenly, it’s as if Makiko just realized she had been skating this whole time.*
Makiko:  "OH MY GOD! I’M SKATING!"
You:  "Why I do believe you are."
Makiko:  "Ah! This is so cool!"
You:  "And you haven’t fallen once, have you?"
Makiko:  "Well, not yet anyway."
You:  "So um, would you like me to let go of your hand now?"
*Realizing my hand is still firmly clasped into hers, Makiko blushes.*
Makiko:  "N-no."
You:  "Well I think you’ve gotten the hang of it. You should be able-"
*She looks up at me, her face flushed with furrowed eyebrows.*
Makiko:  "Hey, you promised you wouldn’t let go of my hand!"
You:  "Okay, you’re right, you’re right. I’ll keep holding onto you."
*Makiko looks down so as to avoid my eye contact.*
Makiko:  "G-good."
*A short moment of silence rolls by before suddenly, something cold falls on my nose.*
*Thinking it’s nothing, I ignore the sensation, only for it to happen again. This time, I turn my eyes upward to look for the source.*
*To my surprise, I notice pure white snowflakes beginning to descend from the sky.*
You:  "Oh. It’s snowing."
*Hearing me speak, Makiko turns her attention from me, and then to the sky as well.*
*Almost immediately, a large smile crosses her face.*
Makiko:  "Wowww. It IS snowing!"
*Makiko, still holding her hand in mine, glances around at the falling snow.*
*The snow seems to pick up from a few snowflakes into a gentle flurry in seemingly a matter of moments.*
*As she gazes at the snow, Makiko squeezes my hand harder unconsciously.*
*She immediately turns to me and loosens her grip*
Makiko:  "OH! Sorry."
*I shake my head and chuckle lightly.*
You:  "It’s fine."
*She then turns back to the sky.*
Makiko:  "It’s beautiful, isn’t it?"
*As she says this, I find myself glancing over towards her out of the corner of my eye.*
*Makiko is by far one of the strangest people I have ever met in my life. Her abundant energy is something I could only dream of.*
*To be honest, I’m actually quite envious of her zest for life. She’s a bit naïve to be sure, but at the same time, that is also very charming.*
*The strange thing is, when Makiko said ‘beautiful’, my mind didn’t go to the snow. It went to…something else.*
*I smile softly as I nod gently.*
You:  "Yeah..beautiful."
*I mumble.*
You:  "Hey Makiko."
*I say slightly louder.*
Makiko:  "Hm?"
*She turns her head to me.*
You:  "Happy new year."
*Out of my peripheral vision I can see a large smile cross her face.*
Makiko:  "Mm! Happy new year."
*And It might have been just my imagination, but I could have sworn I felt her hand squeeze mine a little harder one more time.*
*Having picked up the phone, I look at the two contacts in there, and slowly put the phone back down.*
You:  "I’m sure those two don’t want to be bothered today. From what I hear, today’s an important holiday in Japan. I’m sure they’re spending it with their families."
*I take a deep breath as I put the phone back in my pocket.*
*I look around the empty apartment.*
*I never realized just how lonely it can get in this place without those two around to bug me.*
*Shaking my head, I step towards the door.*
You:  "I need to take a walk."
*Grabbing my coat and scarf, I put on my boots and head out the door.*
*As soon as I step outside, i realize just how good of an idea it was to bring my coat and scarf. It’s easily below freezing.*
*Reaching inside of my coat pocket I pull out my gloves and slip them on.*
*Not even looking in a specific direction, I head off down the street, letting my mind wander as I do.*
*As I walk down the street, I watch people crossing my path. Some give me strange looks, others are too busy chatting with their friends or family to notice.*
You:  "What am I doing here?"
*Nearly a whole year here and my Japanese is still awful, I’ve made a total of two friends, and I still have no idea where my life is going from here. *
*Do I even WANT to stay another year here in Japan?*
*And…if what Fumo and Aina say is true, a few months from now, I won’t even have THEM to keep me company.*
*Sure I have Makiko and Kotone, but I feel almost as if I’m intruding in on their lives…like I’m some sort of charity case.*
*I’m so lost in thought that I don’t even notice the first few snowflakes falling on me.*
*It isn’t until one falls directly onto my nose that I finally bring myself back to my senses.*
*Sure enough, it has begun to snow. More than a flurry in fact, but still, a soft, gentle snow.*
*My mind returning to the landscape in front of me, I soon realize, I have absolutely no idea where I am.*
*I turn my head around, looking for some sort of landmark, but to no avail.*
You:  "Oh crap. Good job <USER>. You got yourself lost."
*I pull out my phone to pull up a map. As I do however, the snow begins to land on the phone. I attempt to wipe it off, but all I seem to do is smear the water.*
You:  "Ugh, this isn’t going to work, I’m going to have to find some sort of awning..or something."
*I turn my head, looking around for some sort of overhang or building I can go into.*
*Not seeing any, I continue walking in a random direction.*
You:  "Well, I’m going to have to hit something eventually."
*This leads me to five more minutes of walking aimlessly.*
*Finally, up on my left, I begin to approach what looks like some sort of park.*
You:  "Ah, maybe there’s a building there or something I can stop in."
*As I approach what I assume to be the park, I get a better view of it*
*I’m greeted with row upon row of gravestones.*
*Oh, this isn’t a park, it’s a cemetary.*
*I let out a sigh as I walk alongside of it.*
*As I walk on the sidewalk alongside of the graveyard, I find my eyes drawn to it. I’m surprised to see how clean and nice all the gravestones look. *
*In perfect rows, not a bit of space gone to waste. Well, it’s efficient at least.*
*Still though, it’s a bit eerie as from what I can tell, the place is deserted.*
*It’s then, that I notice a single, solitary figure standing in front of a gravestone.*
*I’m about to walk away thinking I shouldn’t bother them, when I catch a glimpse of their face.*
You:  "Yuriko?"
*Sure enough, it’s definitely Yuriko standing in front of the gravestone, an umbrella in one hand.*
*Her mouth seems to be moving, but I’m unable to make out what she’s saying. I assume she’s probably talking to the person who the grave belongs to.*
*I know I probably shouldn’t bother someone in this moment, but, as hopelessly lost as I am, I decide it’s probably the best option.*
*I take a few steps towards Yuriko and for the first time, I notice my feet starting to crunch through the snow, it having started to pile up.*
*It isn’t until I’m a few feet from her, that she seems to notice the sound, and turn towards me, slightly shocked.*
Yuriko:  "Oh! <USER>! You startled me."
You:  "Sorry! Didn’t mean to. I was just walking by and I saw you"
*Yuriko seems a bit confused as she looks around.*
Yuriko:  "It's all right, but, what are you doing here?"
*I find myself blushing slightly as I cough into my hand*
You:  "Um, I um….i MAY have gotten…a bit um…lost when walking."
*Yuriko’s eyes grow slightly larger before, as if it finally clicked, closes her eyes and giggles lightly.*
Yuriko:  "I suppose directions aren’t your strong suit."
You:  "Heh. Yeah…never have been."
*A small awkward silence passes.*
*I gesture to the tombstone.*
You:  "Um, was…that someone you were close to?"
*She gives me a small, yet sad smile before turning back to the gravestone in question.*
Yuriko:  "You..could say that."
Yuriko:  "My mother."
You:  "Oh. I’m sorry."
*Yuriko shakes her head simply.*
Yuriko:  "Don’t be. I never knew her. She died…when I was very young."
You:  "Oh."
You:  "I’m sorry, I didn’t mean to-"
*Yuriko shakes her head*
Yuriko:  "Don’t get me wrong, I wasn’t looking for sympathy. You merely seemed curious."
*Ouch, spot on. I would be lying if I said I hadn’t been curious.*
You:  "If it’s any consolation, I know how that is. My mother died too. When I was 10."
Yuriko:  "Oh. I’m sorry to hear that."
*I give her a small sidelong smile.*
You:  "That wasn’t meant for sympathy either. I just wanted us to be even. A fact for a fact."
*Yuriko has a slightly shocked reaction before it transforms into a small smile.*
Yuriko:  "That is definitely fair then."
*There’s another small silence.*
You:  "So..um…"
*She looks back to me.*
You:  "I uh…heard you talking to someone."
*This causes Yuriko to look away, slightly embarrassed.*
Yuriko:  "Oh. So you saw that."
Yuriko:  "Sometimes..when I visit, I like to talk to my mother. As if she were always a part of my life. Even though she never was. I know it’s silly."
You:  "It’s not silly. I talk to my mom sometimes too."
*Yuriko giggles slightly.*
Yuriko:  "That name Mr. Kind Heart really DOES suit you."
*Suddenly, I let out a sneeze.*
*As if for the first time, Yuriko realizes the snow piling up on me.*
Yuriko:  "Oh my goodness. You’re going to catch a cold. Please, come under the umbrella."
You:  "Oh, I couldn’t possibly."
Yuriko:  "No, I insist."
*It doesn’t look like I’m going to win this one.*
You:  "Okay okay, but you have to at least let me hold it."
*Yuriko smiles gently.*
Yuriko:  "All right. That’s fair."
*She hands me the umbrella and I move in so that both of us are under it.*
*Getting so close to her, it was completely different than the time at the pool park. *
*I can smell some sort of light perfume emanating off of her, and I notice the bright red of her lipstick on-*
Yuriko:  "So, you said you were lost?"
You:  "Huh?"
*My thought is cut off*
You:  "Oh. Oh right. Yes. I kind of just started walking in a random direction and I got lost in my thoughts and well….here I am."
*Yuriko giggles lightly.*
Yuriko:  "Somehow I can imagine you doing that."
You:  "Now that I have a place to look at my phone, I can check the map for directions home."
Yuriko:  "Ah, I imagine you have plans with your friends?"
You:  "Haha, no, none to speak of. I was probably going to just go home and…I don’t know, watch a movie on my computer or something."
You:  "I’m sure I’m keeping you from YOUR family or friends though. Sorry about that."
*Yuriko puts on a small smile as she shakes her head lightly.*
Yuriko:  "No, this was pretty much all I had planned for today. I’m probably going to go home and get a head start on some work."
You:  "Oh.. isn’t this an important holiday in Japan though?"
Yuriko:  "It is. To most people."
*There’s a short silence.*
*As I hear this response from her, I can’t help but feel a bit bad. Her response didn’t SOUND like it was too depressed, but still…*
You:  "Hey um.."
*She turns her head to me.*
You:  "Well, if you’re not too busy, did you maybe want to do something together?"
*This actually seems to slightly shock Yuriko, as her eyes open slightly wide.*
*Quickly she regains her normal composure, that same small smile on her face.*
Yuriko:  "Why Mr Kind Heart, are you asking me out on a date? If so, I don’t think that’s very appropriate."
*I immediately blush a deep crimson.*
You:  "W-what? No! Not a date. I was just thinking because both of us had nothing to do maybe we could be less bored…together."
*Yuriko lets out a giggle.*
Yuriko:  "I was merely teasing you. My goodness you blush easily."
Yuriko:  "But…sure. That sounds lovely. Where would you like to go?"
You:  "Well, Maybe somewhere for food would be nice?"
Yuriko:  "So a lunch…not-date?"
You:  "Yeah, just two people with nothing to do hanging out."
Yuriko:  "All right. I know the perfect place."
*Before I can even say anything, she begins to walk ahead of me. I quickly move forward, making sure to keep the umbrella over her head*
*We begin to walk in silence, the sound of the crunching snow beneath our feet the only thing I can hear.*
You:  "So um… is this... is this what you do every new years?"
*I can see Yuriko give me a soft smile from the side.*
Yuriko:  "Normally, yes."
You:  "Does that…ever get lonely?"
*Yuriko raises an eyebrow*
Yuriko:  "My my. What an oddly personal question."
You:  "Sorry, I was just…yeah I’m not good at small talk."
Yuriko:  "Hee hee. I’m sorry. It’s just quite a bit of fun to tease you."
You:  "I get that a lot."
Yuriko:  "To answer your question, it’s all right. I keep myself busy with work normally on holidays."
You:  "Sorry if I’m keeping you."
Yuriko:  "Ah. Once again you misunderstand me. You worry too much. I find talking to you to be very... pleasant."
*A short silence follows*
Yuriko:  "And you?"
You:  "Hm?"
Yuriko:  "What do YOU normally do on holidays? In America that is."
You:  "Well, last year after I broke up with my girlfriend I kind of… didn’t really do much."
Yuriko:  "Oh, so there HAS been romance in Mr. Kind heart’s life."
You:  "I guess you could say that."
Yuriko:  "Well… hopefully this doesn’t sound strange now, but I’m glad your path lead you here. From the reports I’ve received about you, you seem to be an excellent teacher."
You:  "Well now you’re just trying to inflate my ego."
*Yuriko giggles lightly.*
Yuriko:  "Ah, you caught me."
*We talk for a little while longer until we reach our destination.*
*We seem to have arrived in a part of town I didn’t even know of. It’s a lot more high end than the area with Kotone’s café or the school. Several expensive boutiques line the street.*
You:  "Huh…this is still our town?"
Yuriko:  "Oh, you haven’t been here before?"
You:  "No. Man, I really should go exploring more."
*Yuriko giggles again.*
Yuriko:  "Exploring cities in other countries CAN be intimidating. Especially by yourself."
*Yuriko finally stops in front of a building I wouldn’t even know was a restaurant. It looks more like a post modern office building made of glass.*
Yuriko:  "Here we are."
You:  "Looks…interesting."
Yuriko:  "I know it’s very…modern. But the food is quite good."
You:  "I trust you. Lead on boss."
*Yuriko sighs playfully.*
Yuriko:  "I’m not your boss today <USER>. I’m Yuriko. But…just for today."
*She winks*
*After stepping into the restaurant, we’re greeted by a host who speaks to Yuriko in Japanese. She says something in return and he bows, leading the two of us to a seat.*
*As we begin to walk to our seats however, I can hear some relatively loud chattering going on near us.*
*Glancing over, I notice there are a few women all gathered around another one, excitedly asking questions.*
*I of course have no idea what they’re saying, but it doesn’t take a degree in Japanese language to see that the one they’re all cooing around is pregnant.*
*Yuriko seems to follow my eye movement and notice them too. She immediately whips her head back.*
*I notice this quick reaction.*
You:  "You okay?"
Yuriko:  "Huh? Oh yes, yes I’m fine."
*Yuriko quickly picks up the menu and pretends to be engrossed in it.*
You:  "Do you know those women?"
*I ask confused*
Yuriko:  "Hm?"
*She looks up from her menu.*
Yuriko:  "You could say that."
You:  "So old friends?"
Yuriko:  "I..i wouldn’t go that far."
*I feel the table start to slightly shake. Confused, my eyes roam a bit until I eventually find the source of the motion.*
*Underneath the table, Yuriko’s knee is bouncing nervously, causing it to lightly hit the table several times.*
*As I peek out at her from behind the menu she seems to notice me looking at her and she blushes.*
Yuriko:  "S..sorry."
You:  "You know Yuriko, if there’s something going on with you and those women, we can leave and go somewhere else."
Yuriko:  "N..no, it’s fine. Really. I’m sorry."
*She says, sounding a little less confident than normal*
You:  "Ok…ay, if you say so."
*As I go back to looking at the menu, Yuriko is seemingly doing her best to cover her face with her menu.*
*A few moments of silence pass.*
You:  "So um…do you recommend anything?"
*As if my presence shocks her, she looks back at me from behind the menu.*
Yuriko:  "Huh? Oh, let me see…"
*She says, as if remembering she had the menu in her hands.*
*It's then that one of the women from the other table calls out to her.*
*Suddenly, Yuriko freezes up.*
*They call to her again.*
*I glance over and see one of the women waving over at us.*
You:  "Hey uh, I think they’re talking to you Yuriko."
*As I look over at Yuriko, I realize just how pale she’s gotten*
*Before I can say anything else, the trio of women have made their way over towards her.*
*Suddenly, they start speaking to her in a string of Japanese that I couldn’t even begin to comprehend.*
*It’s obvious something is off with Yuriko as the more they talk to her, the more uncomfortable she’s getting, but she’s doing her best to maintain her composure.*
*The pregnant one of the group seems to do the most talking as she keeps motioning to her stomach while talking.*
*Yuriko says something politely, putting up her hands, which I assume to be a decline of some kind.*
*It’s at this moment however that the woman grabs her hand and pulls it towards her, placing it on her stomach.*
*Whatever color remained on Yuriko’s face is now completely gone as her hand rests on the woman’s stomach.*
*The pregnant woman says a few things in Japanese as a now sweating Yuriko attempts to keep her composure.*
*After a few moments however, Yuriko quickly removes her hand from the stomach and begins to cradle that hand with the other one, as if it were injured.*
*She says a few more things to the women, a pained smile on her face.*
*Seemingly satisfied, the women wave to Yuriko and walk away.*
You:  "Well that was…interesting. You okay Yuriko?"
*I turn my head from the leaving women back to Yuriko.*
*However, Yuriko doesn’t seem to be responding.*
You:  "Yuriko?"
*I hear her breathing get slightly louder.*
*Suddenly, Yuriko looks up to me, a large forced smile on her face.*
Yuriko:  "I’m sorry, I have to step out for a minute. I’ll be right back."
*Before I can even say anything in response, Yuriko stands up and practically power walks out the door.*
*Well that was weird.*
*I awkwardly look around. The place is pretty busy but I manage to flag down a waiter*
You:  "Uh… Sumimasen!"
*I reach into my pocket and pull out my book*
You:  "Watashitachi wa sugu ni modotte kimasu"
*The waiter merely nods, understanding what I was trying to say despite my terrible pronunciation and grammar.*
*Quickly, I leave the building and look around. Where could she have gone?*
*it doesn’t take a long time of me searching before I eventually hear a heavy breathing coming from a nearby alleyway.*
*Without thinking, I turn down the alleyway*
*There, greeting me is a sight I’m not prepared for.*
*Leaning up against the wall, her hands over head, is Yuriko, hyperventilating.*
*I can see sweat dripping from her face as it lands in the snow beneath her.*
*Unsure of what to do, I move over to her and slowly place my hand on her back.*
You:  "Yuriko, are you-"
*Before i can finish my inquiry though, Yuriko quickly jerks away from me, as if being stabbed.*
Yuriko:  "Don’t!"
*Her voice is loud, almost at a scream, before, as if recollecting herself, lowers her volume.*
Yuriko:  "S-sorry. Please, just…not right now."
*I pull my hand away.*
You:  "I’m sorry. I didn’t-"
Yuriko:  "N-no. it’s all right. Just, can you please leave me alone for a moment?"
*I back up a few feet, the snow crunching beneath me.*
You:  "R-right. Sure"
*Respecting her wishes, I turn around and walk back out into the street, effectively making myself a shield so anyone walking by won’t be able to look in and see her in the alley.*
*After about a minute, I hear a voice behind me.*
Yuriko:  "I’m all right now."
*I turn around to see Yuriko, looking much better than she had a moment ago. She removes her glasses and wipes them before replacing them.*
Yuriko:  "I’m sorry but, I don’t really feel like going back in there."
You:  "That’s fine. I can just go back in and grab our stuff."
Yuriko:  "You don’t have to-"
You:  "No, it’s fine. I’ll be right back."
*Turning back, I walk into the restaurant and grab our items, and briefly explain to the waiter apologetically that we have to leave. Since we didn’t order anything there was really no issue.*
*I step back outside and hand Yuriko her coat.*
You:  "Here you go."
*She looks away from me as I hand it to her.*
Yuriko:  "Thank you."
You:  "So, where are we going instead?"
*There’s a short silence*
Yuriko:  "I think I’m going to head home actually."
You:  "oh, we’re going to your house? I thought you said this wasn’t a date."
*This actually causes Yuriko to stop and turn to me.*
Yuriko:  "What? No. I meant I was going home alone."
*I put my hands in my pocket.*
You:  "Oh, so you’re going to ditch me? Not very professional of you."
Yuriko:  "I..i don’t think I’d be good company right now."
You:  "well, I’m NEVER good company, so that evens it out."
*To my surprise, Yuriko’s face turns to a puzzled one, before eventually breaking out into a giggle.*
Yuriko:  "That nickname of yours really is appropriate."
Yuriko:  "I’m not that hungry right now, but.. you can walk with me if you’d like."
You:  "Sure, I’m not doing anything else."
Yuriko:  "Oh, glad to see I’m such a high priority"
You:  "You know what I mean."
Yuriko:  "And YOU blush too easily."
*I give her a smirk as the two of us continue to walk, the streets becoming less and less populated as we walk, until finally, it’s just the two of us on the road*
*After a few minutes of silence, Yuriko’s the one to break it.*
Yuriko:  "You really aren’t going to ask?"
*She says, making sure not to look at me while we’re talking.*
You:  "About what?"
Yuriko:  "You know exactly what."
*She says, still not looking at me.*
You:  "Well, it’s none of my business. Why would I pry?"
Yuriko:  "I bet the curiosity is eating away at you though."
*I shrug*
You:  "Not really. I have a good idea."
Yuriko:  "Oh really? Would you like to share with the rest of the class?"
*I stop as Yuriko continues to take a few steps forward.*
You:  "You have a pregnancy phobia, don’t you?"
*Suddenly, Yuriko stops.*
*She stops and turns back to me.*
*Yuriko looks at me with a shocked expression, with only silence following*
You:  "Am I right?"
*She looks away from me and upwards towards the falling snow.*
Yuriko:  "And?"
You:  "And what?"
Yuriko:  "Well, aren’t you going to say something about it?"
You:  "Like…what?"
Yuriko:  "About how stupid or silly it is?"
*I shrug*
You:  "No, not really. I totally understand."
Yuriko:  "You…understand?"
*She asks, as if these specifics words were ones she never saw coming in a million years.*
Yuriko:  "What do you mean by that?"
You:  "I mean, it totally makes sense. That kind of thing freaks me out too. If I were a woman I wouldn’t want any part of that."
*For a moment, Yuriko blinks at me before looking at the ground for a moment, and then back at me, her expression back to normal.*
Yuriko:  "Actually….I’m sorry but..I AM feeling a bit hungry. Would you mind if we stopped at another place to eat?"
*I shrug*
You:  "Like I said, I have no plans."
*I gesture with the umbrella.*
You:  "I’ll even keep holding the umbrella."
Yuriko:  "Why thank you."
*Yuriko and I continue to walk in silence. As she’s far more familiar with the area than me, I’m just following her, assuming she’s knowing where she’s going.*
Yuriko:  "She died during childbirth."
*Yuriko finally says, seemingly out of nowhere.*
*I turn to her. Can’t say I was expecting to hear THAT.*
You:  "Who?"
Yuriko:  "My mother."
*She answers simply.*
Yuriko:  "With me."
You:  "I’m sorry to hear-"
*I stop myself*
You:  "Oh. Right you were just telling me because you thought I might be interested?"
*Yuriko gives me a small smile.*
Yuriko:  "You catch on quick."
*She turns back forward.*
Yuriko:  "In fact, you might say my mother’s side of the family has a curse."
You:  "A curse?"
Yuriko:  "Every single woman on my mother’s side, as far back as our records go, has died during childbirth."
You:  "Oh. Oh geez. Yeah, I can see why you feel the way you do."
*Another silence widens the gap between us.*
You:  "Um..is that a problem? Like…do you WANT kids?"
*A very un-Yuriko like laugh emanates from her.*
You:  "Did I say something funny?"
*Yuriko stops walking.*
Yuriko:  "Tell me…Mister Kind Heart, what do YOU think about children? Off the record of course."
*Huh…that’s a weird question. Whatever, no real reason to lie.*
You:  "I mean….. they’re whatever. I don’t really care about them persay. They’re more like…fixtures I guess? Like a chair. Only…they’re alive. You?"
Yuriko:  "I hate them."
*Another pause fills the air.*
*My gaze turns back towards Yuriko and she’s wearing that same expression of disgust she had at the festival.*
Yuriko:  "I honestly can’t stand them. They’re smelly, noisy, completely unorganized, and a waste of time that could be spent during more productive things."
*Every word coming from her mouth seems more venomous than the last. And yet, for all her vitriol, her voice never changes its octave, it only adding to the strangeness.*
Yuriko:  "Well? Are you going to ask the question? I know you want to. Please. Go right ahead."
You:  "Okay, i'll bite. Why work in a job then that deals with schools?"
Yuriko:  "Ah. There's the question. To put it simply, it's a stepping stone."
You:  "Oh. So you're basically using it as a job to get a better one in the future."
*Yuriko doesn't answer, but merely smirks back at me.*
*As she finishes her statement, we arrive at what appears to be a crossroad.*
*She raises her hand and points to the left.*
Yuriko:  "Your apartment is down that way."
Yuriko:  "If you walk straight for about three minutes you’ll reach it."
You:  "Oh, thanks. Why are you telling me this now though?"
Yuriko:  "Just in case you realized you had to get home."
*I look at her confused.*
You:  "I thought we decided we were going out to lunch?"
*Yuriko looks over to me, and raises an eyebrow.*
Yuriko:  "And you still want to do that?"
You:  "Um..yeah, did something change I don’t know about?"
*Yuriko seems to pause for a moment before shaking her head, her expression replaced with a small smile.*
Yuriko:  "No, my mistake. Come on Mr Kind Heart, the restaurant’s just up ahead."
You:  "R-right"
*What was that about?*
*I shake my head as I continue to walk following Yuriko until eventually I’m walking side by side with her, holding the umbrella.*
*Well, hopefully she's not busy with a big holiday rush or something.*
*As I pick up the phone to dial her number, suddenly, it rings in my hand.*
*Without looking at the caller id, I pick it up.*
You:  "Um…hello?"
Voice:  "<USER>?"
You:  "Yeah?"
Kotone:  "Oh. Hey. It’s Kotone."
You:  "Oh, well…that was good timing, I was about to call you."
*There’s a short silence.*
Kotone:  "You were? Why?"
*I shrug despite her not being able to see me.*
You:  "Well I’m not doing anything today. Was just going to ask if you were doing anything. I know it’s an important holiday and all."
*There’s another short silence.*
Kotone:  "Y..yeah it normally is. Aren’t you going to be hanging out with Fumo or Aina?"
You:  "No, they wanted to go to some shrine in the middle of nowhere. I really wasn’t feeling like doing that."
Kotone:  "So what? You’re going to spend New Year’s alone?"
You:  "Well…that’s why I was going to call you."
You:  "Speaking of which, you called ME. What exactly did YOU want?"
Kotone:  "Excuse me for calling to check on the poor lonely guy."
*Nimble dodge there Kotone.*
*This will probably go a lot faster if I just play to her ego.*
You:  "Yeah, you got me Kotone. I thought about hanging out with Fumo and Aina, but I really was kind of hoping you’d be free so we could hang out together today."
*There’s another pause before Kotone comes back with a short laugh*
Kotone:  "I guess you can’t help it. I mean, who WOULDN’T want to call me and ask me to keep them company?"
You:  "You got me figured out."
*Kotone lets out a sigh.*
Kotone:  "All right, all right. How can I say no to that? Fine, I’ll hang out with you today. SOMEONE has to show you how to have a proper Japanese New Year’s anyway. "
Kotone:  "I’ll meet you at the fountain in town in an hour. You know where it is?"
*I nod out of habit.*
You:  "Yeah, that’s fine. I’ll see you there."
Kotone:  "Okay…and you BETTER not be late!"
*I hear the phone hang up in my ear.*
*Well, I probably should get ready.*
*Putting the phone back in my pocket I go to my closet and begin to bundle up.*
*The trip to the center of town doesn’t take too long as I make it to the meeting spot with about five minutes to spare.*
*And there, staring at her watch, is a bundled up Kotone. Seeing her in a normalish looking coat, If I didn’t know her so well, I might not have recognized her.*
*I figure I could try to sneak up on her, but it would probably sour her mood for a while and I’d rather not deal with that.*
*Instead I merely walk up to her and wave congenially.*
You:  "Hey Kotone!"
*Hearing my voice seems to shock her for a moment as she looks up.*
Kotone:  "Oh! <USER>!"
*She looks back at her watch.*
*Looking back to me, she gives me a toothy grin.*
Kotone:  "You actually made it on time. I must say I’m impressed."
*That honestly makes it sound like she expected me to be late.*
You:  "Well, what kind of person would I be if I let such a nice girl like you wait out here in the cold?"
Kotone:  "Exactly! See, not many guys understand that. I’m surprised you do."
You:  "Um..thanks?"
Kotone:  "Ugh. Don’t be confused, that was a compliment."
*She looks around.*
Kotone:  "So where are we going?"
You:  "I’m not quite sure. You’re the Japanese expert. Is there something I should be doing on New year’s?"
Kotone:  "Of COURSE I’m the expert. I was just making sure you were leaving everything to me."
*I gesture over to her in a ‘take it away’ motion.*
*Kotone nods smugly. Soon, she takes a few steps and then stops.*
*She then looks around as if trying to get her bearings. She tries to this subtly, but is failing miserably.*
You:  "Everything okay Kotone?"
Kotone:  "Of course it is! I’m just…thinking which is the best place to go. There’s a lot of good places."
You:  "Are we going to a shrine or something?"
*To my surprise, Kotone turns around and looks at me with a raised eyebrow.*
Kotone:  "Did you WANT to go to a shrine?"
*I find myself scratching my head.*
You:  "I mean…I thought that was kind of what you did on New year’s here. Am I wrong?"
Kotone:  "No, you’re right, but we can do that really anytime."
You:  "Then..where are we going?"
*Kotone pauses for a moment as if thinking. Then she turns back to me.*
Kotone:  "Well, on New year’s, it’s traditional to see a movie at the theater. So we should probably go do that."
You:  "It is? I can’t say I’ve ever heard of that."
*Kotone blushes slightly as she crosses her arms.*
Kotone:  "Who’s the Japanese one here?"
*I put up my hands defensively.*
You:  "Sorry, sorry. If you say it’s a custom, okay, it’s a custom. I could see a movie. Lead on oh Kotone-sensei."
*Seemingly satisfied, Kotone begins to walk ahead, leading me downtown.*
*After a few moments of silence, I finally am able to put my finger on what's off.*
You:  "So, it’s rare to not see Uso with you."
Kotone:  "Yeah…well, I couldn’t exactly bring her with me to walk around town."
Kotone:  "She did throw a fit when I was bundling up to leave without her. I can look forward to her pouting later."
You:  "You really care about her, don’t you?"
*She turns to me as if I were accusing her of something.*
Kotone:  "So what if I do?"
*I glance at her confused.*
You:  "Nothing. I just think it’s cute."
*Surprised at my reaction, she turns her head back, putting her hands in her pockets.*
Kotone:  "O-oh. Well…t.thanks."
You:  "I really wish I had a pet rabbit again. I haven’t had one in so long."
Kotone:  "You used to have one in America?"
*I nod.*
You:  "Yeah, her name was Ms Stuffykins. She was a great rabbit. She was brown and white. I had her… well I had her through some tough times."
*I place my hands back in my pockets.*
*A silence hangs in the air as we walk for a while.*
*Finally, I decide to break it.*
You:  "Heh. Sorry about that. Kinda killed the fun mood there."
Kotone:  "it’s okay"
*She says this softly. Far softer than I’m used to hearing Kotone talk.*
*She stops and turns back to me.*
Kotone:  "You know, you CAN talk to me about serious stuff if you want to. "
Kotone:  "You don’t have to worry that I’m going to break or something if the conversation gets serious."
*She gives me a toothy smile.*
Kotone:  "I’m a big girl <USER>. I can handle adult conversations."
*I feel slightly embarrassed at her accurate chastising.*
You:  "Hah. Right. Right. Sorry."
*Luckily though for me, at that moment, we arrive at at large movie theater.*
*I turn to her.*
You:  "You do realize I won’t know what’s going on if we pick a Japanese movie, right?"
*She waves her hand up and down.*
Kotone:  "It’s fine, it’s fine. I’ll translate for you."
You:  "Right…so. What are we seeing?"
Kotone:  "Well..um..let’s see…"
*She looks over the movie choices.*
*I myself glance over the pictures as I obviously can’t read the words.*
*However, one picture does indeed catch my eye.*
*it’s a picture of what looks to be the anime that Kotone showed me before.*
*I gesture at the picture.*
You:  "Hey, isn’t that the anime you were showing me?"
*Kotone turns to me.*
Kotone:  "Well yeah, but…oh my god. You haven’t seen that yet! Okay, Come on."
*Without even thinking about it, Kotone grabs me by the hand and pulls me towards the ticket window.*
*She says something in Japanese to the ticket vendor while holding out two fingers.*
*The vendor looks between the two of us and gives us a congenial smile before handing Kotone the tickets.*
You:  "Oh, right, let me-"
*But before I can reach for my wallet, she, with her free hand, deftly pays the woman.*
*I never noticed it before, but even through her gloves, I can tell, Kotone’s hands are really warm.*
*My hands are far larger than hers, but she manages to be holding mine in such a way that it would be hard to break out unless I really tried to.*
*The ticket seller then says one last thing to Kotone. I’m unsure what it is, but the moment she does, Kotone’s face goes beet red and she quickly removes her hand from mine.*
You:  "Everything okay?"
Kotone:  "What? Yeah! Of course everything’s fine. Why wouldn’t it be?"
*Before I can respond she hands me a ticket.*
Kotone:  "Here."
*I look at the ticket.*
You:  "You know, I CAN pay for stuff."
*I say giving her a smirk.*
You:  "I’m a big boy."
*Kotone gives me what sounds like a scoff before pushing the ticket into my chest. I have to fumble to grab it so it doesn’t hit the floor.*
Kotone:  "Come on then, ‘big boy’, you can buy us snacks."
*She walks towards the concession stand and looks over the options.*
You:  "Go ahead. Pick out whatever you want."
*Kotone nods and picks out a few items and I do as well.*
*After purchasing our items, we make our way towards the theater.*
*Luckily for us, the house lights are still on and we quickly find our seats.*
*It seems to be relatively crowded for a Wednesday, however, that makes sense considering that it’s a holiday. In fact, most of the people in the seats seem to be young teenagers.*
*Oh geez, young teenagers.*
*I look around. Yep, sure enough, I definitely recognize several students from my school.*
*Kotone notices my fidgeting.*
Kotone:  "Everyone okay?"
You:  "Yeah I just… I just realized I know some of these kids from school."
*Almost as if she’s relieved, Kotone gives me a toothy grin.*
Kotone:  "Well then, you’re just going to be the cool teacher, now aren’t you?"
*I look at her with a bit of confusion as she says this.*
*Sure enough, within about a minute, one of them spots me and whispers something to their friend. They turn around and see me as well, giggling.*
*They wave to me, to which I, beet red embarrassed, give a wave back.*
*They giggle and say a bunch of words back and forth to each other in Japanese.*
*One of the words they say the loudest that I manage to pick up is ‘Kanojo’.*
*Kotone must hear this, as suddenly her face turns bright red too, making sure to turn her head away.*
*Confused, I shuffle myself around to grab my translation book.*
Kotone:  "What are you doing?"
*She asks, seeing me move around.*
You:  "I’m getting my translation book. Those girls said something and I wanted to know what it was."
*Something seems to click in Kotone’s head as her eyes grow larger.*
Kotone:  "it’s fine. Don’t worry about it."
*She says as she gently shoves me back into my seat.*
You:  "But-"
*Kotone turns to me, a serious expression on her face that’s made slightly less intimidating by her blush.*
Kotone:  "Don’t look up that word."
*If dad taught me anything, it’s when to pick your fights.*
*I put up my hands in defeat.*
You:  "All right, fine. I won’t look."
*Relieved, Kotone sits back down.*
*As she does, the lights go down and the movie begins.*
You:  "You gonna tell me what it means?"
*I ask, leaning over to her and whisper.*
Kotone:  "Shh. The movie’s starting."
*Nice deflection Kotone.*
*I decide it’s not worth it and let it go for now.*
*As the movie starts the narrators spouts a long string of Japanese out that I can’t begin to hope to comprehend.*
*Thankfully, Kotone leans over and translates for me.*
*I nod. Right, this sounds familiar as it’s the plot of the show I had seen with her earlier.*
*Thankfully, a lot of the movie is action so there isn’t much to translate.*
*The movie chugs along. I had never really seen myself as an anime fan before, but I have to say that the movie was doing pretty well at drawing me in.*
*About three quarters of the way through though you can tell they’re preparing for the final climactic battle and the main female meets up with the main male.*
*The female says something to him in Japanese, obviously distraught and in tears.*
*However this time, Kotone doesn’t say anything.*
*I lean over to her.*
You:  "Kotone, what did she say?"
*I whisper so she can hear me.*
*Kotone keeps looking at the screen.*
Kotone:  "It’s not important"
*She says, once again her ability to lie terribly shining through.*
You:  "What’s going on seems pretty important."
*I suddenly feel a vibration next to me, as if Kotone is shaking.*
*Quickly though, it subsides.*
*Taking a deep breath, she opens her mouth, still looking away from me.*
Kotone:  "She said… Don’t leave me. I love you."
*She says this extremely quickly and doing her best to hide her embarrassment.*
You:  "Ah. Yeah. Okay I can see that now. But wait a second. I thought that Kenji loved Ruriko."
*Kotone turns to me, seemingly forgetting her embarrassment, it being replaced with a look of shock*
Kotone:  "Where did you get that idea?"
You:  "I don’t know..I just thought that the way Kenji interacted with Ruriko was..more genuine. I mean, Nadi is an okay character but.. I think Kenji would be better with Ruriko."
Kotone:  "Thank you. Right! Oh my god. I thought I was the only person who thought that! Nadi is TOTALLY wrong for Kenji. I don’t know why the writers went with that."
You:  "Yeah it’s kind of…lame."
*Kotone snickers.*
Kotone:  "Yeah, it kind of is."
*I slowly go to move my hand to grab for some popcorn. As I do, it seems that Kotone is doing the same thing. *
*For a moment, our hands accidentally brush against each other’s. We pull away quickly*
You:  "Sorry."
*Kotone blushes as she does her best to be dismissive.*
Kotone:  "Don’t worry about it."
*With that awkwardness aside, we basically sit in silence for the rest of the film, Kotone only leaning over to translate dialogue heavy scenes that remain in the film.*
*As the film ends, we stand up and step out of the theater.*
You:  "Well that was…interesting."
Kotone:  "In this situation, interesting is usually another word for bad."
You:  "Well no, the movie was fine, the whole Kenji thing just bothers me."
*Kotone gives a small laugh.*
Kotone:  "<USER> if you get upset every time your pairing doesn’t work out in anime, you’re going to be disappointed a LOT of the time."
You:  "I mean…I guess. I just didn’t think that those two had any chemistry."
Kotone:  "Look at you Doctor Love."
*I give her a sarcastic look.*
You:  "Hardy har."
You:  "So. Where are we off to now?"
Kotone:  "Well, I suppose we should visit a shrine. Give you the whole Japanese new year’s experience."
You:  "All right. Sounds good. Lead the way."
*Kotone nods as she walks ahead of me, making sure to not go too fast so as to lose me.*
*We keep pretty silent for the whole walk as the streets are pretty crowded and it’s taking nearly all my concentration to keep up with her through the crowd.*
*Eventually, we reach the steps of a shrine, with what appears to be an orange gate at the entrance.*
You:  "All right. So…what do we do here?"
*As we finish climbing the stairs, she gestures to a bucket with some flowing water.*
Kotone:  "First you’re going to want to pour water over your hands with that ladel."
You:  "Why?"
Kotone:  "It’s a spiritual cleansing thing."
You:  "Ah. Okay."
*I move my way over towards the water and, following Kotone’s example, wash my hands. The water is pretty cold, but I expected it to be so.*
*I shake my hands off to dry them.*
You:  "Okay, now what?"
*Kotone takes a look around the shrine. Her eyes eventually settle on a wooden box over to the side.*
Kotone:  "Oh, let’s get a fortune!"
*She says as she unconsciously pulls me by the hand.*
*Within a few moments we’re standing at the box.*
You:  "So what do I do?"
*She then quickly explains to me how it's basically a fortune teller system. You pick a scroll and it tells you your fortune for luck, health, etc.*
You:  "Ah. So no pressure then."
Kotone:  "It’s mainly for fun."
You:  "All right, all right. Here we go."
*I drop 100 yen in the box and pick one at random.*
*I then take a cursory glance at it, then extend it out to Kotone.*
You:  "Well, it’s going to be in Japanese, so please, do the honors."
*Kotone gives me a confident grin as she takes it from my hand, puffing out her chest slightly.*
Kotone:  "Well, the Japanese CAN be pretty complicated on these fortunes. I wouldn’t expect you to be nearly as good as me with two languages."
You:  "Right, right, Kotone is amazing."
*I say this somewhat sarcastically, but Kotone merely keeps smiling as she crosses her arms and nods.*
Kotone:  "Right? Right?"
You:  "All right, what does it say?"
Kotone:  "Okay, so it’s separated into different fortunes…so lets see here."
*She pours over the fortune for a moment.*
Kotone:  "Oh, moderate luck, not bad. Let’s see…finances, unchanged. Not getting worse, not getting better."
You:  "Figures"
Kotone:  "Health…Oh. Very good health."
You:  "Well at least I have that going for me."
*Suddenly though, as she gets to the next part of the fortune, she begins to blush as her eyes go wide.*
You:  "Everything okay?"
Kotone:  "Yeah. Yeah, everything’s fine."
You:  "Does it say something bad?"
Kotone:  "What? No! No. It just says that um… You’re going to make a lot of friends."
You:  "Oh. Well, that’s good."
*Kotone gives me a nervous laugh as she closes the fortune and puts it down.*
Kotone:  "So yeah, I guess your fortune isn’t too bad. You’re going to have a good year."
*I gesture towards the fortunes.*
*As I change the subject, Kotone seems to be back in full Kotone mode. She rolls up her sleeves.*
Kotone:  "Okay..let’s see what we’ve got!"
*Reaching in she pulls out a fortune after dropping 100 yen in the box.*
*She unrolls the fortune.*
You:  "So what did you get?"
Kotone:  "Ugh. Small good fortune."
You:  "Well, that’s better than bad right?"
*Kotone lets out a sigh.*
Kotone:  "Yeah yeah. Let’s see here. Ooh. Good for money this year….watch your health…and for rom-"
*She seems to catch herself before finishing a word.*
Kotone:  "Friendship it says…."
You:  "it says…."
*A look of irritation pops up on Kotone’s face as she closes the scroll.*
You:  "Something..bad?"
Kotone:  "Never mind. It’s fine."
You:  "Well now I’m intrigued."

*Gaikokujin No Sensei*

*The End*

*Gaikokujin No Sensei*

*She closes her eyes, and within a few moments, our lips are touching.*
*Kotone, expecting a quick peck, begins to pull away, but I don’t let her as I push myself into her, kissing her deeply and passionately.*
*At first, she seems shocked at the turn of events, but quickly accepts my passion and returns it in kind.*
*When the kiss finally finishes, Kotone looks up at me, her smug expression now back on her face.*
Kotone:  "You do realize that now you’re stuck with me, right?"
*I shrug*
You:  "Guess so."
Kotone:  "I gave you an out. You didn’t take it. Now you have to pay the consequences."
*Kotone is now fully back in Kotone-mode*
You:  "Oh really, and what would these consequences be?"
*She makes a ‘tsk tsk’ with her finger.*
Kotone:  "THAT would be telling."
You:  "Ooh. Surprises. Well, I’m going to have the rest of my life to find out exactly what they are."
*This causes Kotone to smile widely.*
Kotone:  "I’m going to hold you to that."
*I smile back.*
You:  "You better."
*-Three Years Later-*
You:  "Do I really have to come out like this?"
Kotone:  "Come on! The new male uniforms look really cute."
*I feel like I’m going to die of embarrassment.*
*I let out a drawn out sigh as I place the rabbit ears on my head.*
*It’s been three years since the night when I told Kotone my feelings for her, and well, a lot has changed.*
*I finished that second year with the school but didn't opt for a third. Instead Kotone got me a work visa to be employed at her café as her assistant manager.*
*Of course, as of last year, that turned into a spousal visa.*
*If I remember correctly, when I proposed to Kotone, her exact words were ‘Well of COURSE you’d want to marry me. I’m just surprised it took you so long.’*
*She wasn’t very good at hiding her tears though.*
*Over the past three years, Kotone’s rabbit café took off like a rocket and now, business has been so good, that we’re opening another one in the center of Tokyo.*
*After finishing changing, I step out to show Kotone the finished product.*
*I blush from how silly I feel.*
*The outfit has a white shirt with a vest and black pants. The embarrassing part is the large pocket watch along with the ears and of course, the cottontail I’m wearing on my lower back.*
*Kotone takes a look at my outfit from head to toe and gives a nod approvingly.*
Kotone:  "Yep! Looks perfect, just like I thought it would. Doesn’t it Uso?"
*The rabbit on her shoulder lets out a CHUU in agreement.*
*Et tu brute?*
*Seeing my resistance, she puts her arms on her hips.*
Kotone:  "All right pouty, what’s the problem now?"
You:  "Isn’t this just well…a little embarrassing? I think I’m going to need some motivation."
*Kotone rolls her eyes before giving me a smirk.*
*In one smooth motion, she grabs me by the tie and pulls me to her face, planting a large kiss on me which I return.*
*A moment later, the kiss is over and she lets go of my tie, only to readjust it into my vest.*
Kotone:  "You’re impossible sometimes."
*She lets out a dramatic sigh.*
Kotone:  "You’re just lucky I love you so much."
*I give her a warm smile in return.*
You:  "Yeah, I really am."
*Such a response seems to catch Kotone offguard as she blushes.*
Kotone:  "Come on, that’s enough of that. Time to greet our customers."
You:  "Right, right."
*In synch, the two of us head towards the door leading to the front.*
*I can’t believe this is where my life has lead me. From your everyday office guy in America to part owner of a rabbit café in Tokyo.*
*But, that’s just how life is sometimes. And honestly, I wouldn’t change anything for the world.*
*As I emerge from the back room with my wife, I put on my most cheerful face to the line of customers waiting, as in unison the two of us let out an *
Kotone/You:  "IRRASHAIMASE-CHUU!"
*-The End-*
*The bus ride over to the shrine where I first met them is a quiet one. It’s hard to believe that it was a year ago today I met both Aina and Fumo.*
*It’s also hard to believe that in a few minutes they’ll be gone, and I’ll never see them again.*
*Sooner than I would like, the bus stops, and the three of us step off of the bus.*
*The three of us make our way through the forest. The same woods i walked through a year ago.*
*Eventually, we find ourselves in a clearing.*
*A sense of déjà vu envelopes me.*
*Turning my head around, I notice we’re at the place where I first met Aina. That same strange shrine.*
Fumo:  "Well, we're here."
*Fumo’s words break me out of my stupor.*
You:  "Right, I guess we are."
*Before any of us can say anything further, the doors on the tiny shrine shoot open with a loud crash.*
*All of our attentions turn to it.*
*Fumo looks back at me.*
Fumo:  "Well… it looks like it’s time."
*She raises her gaze to stare at me directly in the eyes.*
Fumo:  "<USER> you really are strange. From the day I met you I couldn’t really understand any of your motivations or what you really wanted."
Fumo:  "But, despite all of that, you welcomed us in and treated me as a friend. I want to thank you for that."
*Fumo bows before me.*
Fumo:  "So thank you."
*I feel a sad smile cross my face.*
You:  "Hey, I just did what anyone would have done."
*She returns at me with a small smirk.*
Fumo:  "No, you didn’t. And that’s why I’m thanking you."
*Turning to gaze at the portal, she looks back to me.*
Fumo:  "Good luck with your life <USER>. I mean that."
*She says, showing me a slight smile. Before I can say anything in response, she turns away and walks into the portal.*
*Fumo is enveloped in a bright light, and just like that, she’s gone.*
*Before I even have a chance to think, Aina hops over to me.*
Aina:  "<USER>. I am…very sorry"
*She says in her best English, each word seeming to be extremely hard for her.*
Aina:  "But, I thank you for everything."
*She takes my hand in hers and smiles.*
Aina:  "You treat Aina like person. You make Aina very happy."
*Despite the craziness she put me through, I can’t help but smile at the heartfelt gratitude.*
You:  "Dou Itashimashite Aina. Anata wa…tomodachi desu. (You're welcome Aina. You're my friend.)"
*Even though the Japanese wasn’t very good at all, I could see Aina’s face burst into a smile, tears beginning to well in her eyes.*
Aina:  "Arigatou…<USER>-sama"
*She wipes her eyes.*
Aina:  "Jya, Sayonara, <USER>-sama"
*And with that, she releases my hand and walks towards the portal. I struggle to look at her, but the light is too bright, and, in a few moments, she’s gone.*
*And, just like that. I'm alone.*
*I glance around one more time, as if one of them would pop out at any moment, but of course, this doesn't happen.*
*So, with that, I place my hands in my pockets, and make my way back towards the bus station.*
*Gaikokujin No Sensei*
*It’s been a few days since Fumo and Aina left, and, I hate to admit it, it’s actually gotten a little bit lonely.*
*Sure, life with them was insane, but at the very least, it was never dull.*
*Now, it’s almost hard to get out of bed. Or Futon..or…whatever.*
*I exhale audibly as I look around my empty apartment.*
*Slowly I stand up, and, gathering my things I head towards the door.*
*A new school year has started and another day of work is finished. After flopping down in my chair, I turn my head upwards.*
*I half expect Aina or Fumo to say something, breaking me out of my relaxed mood.*
*But of course, that doesn’t happen.*
*I’m not sure how long I stare at the ceiling before finally turning my head back down.*
*As I glance back up I see Makiko's usual smiling face.*
*At the very least Makiko's continued presence is a comfort. It doesn't completely fill the void, but, it helps.*
*Noticing that I saw her, she moves a little closer. However, she must catch that something's off as her expression changes.*
Makiko:  "Oh. Hey <USER>. How's it going?"
You:  "I’m…I’m okay. Just thinking."
*Makiko's eyes shift to the side.*
Makiko:  "About Fumo and Aina?"
*I give her a small smirk.*
You:  "It's that obvious, huh?"
Makiko:  "Well, you're pretty easy to read."
Makiko:  "If it's any consulation, I miss them too."
*I raise my lip up into a somewhat larger smile.*
You:  "Thanks. It does."
*There's a short silence.*
Makiko:  "Besides! It's not like they're gone forever! They just moved, right? You can always talk to them again!"
*As soon as she says this, my smile immediately vanishes.*
You:  "...y...yeah."
*What else am I supposed to say? I can't tell her the truth of how they're spirits and they're gone forever. She'd think i'm a lunatic.*
*My face falls again*
*An awkward silence falls between us which of course is broken by Makiko.*
Makiko:  "Well hey! We have another year together! Isn't that great?"
*I almost laugh at Makiko's jovialness. I can tell she's trying so hard to cheer me up. Then again, she's always been like that.*
*Truthfully, she has been my first and really only true friend at this whole school. Most of the staff has either been too busy with their own work,*
*Or my own lack of Japanese frightened them away. But...not Makiko. She's done nothing but welcomed me with open arms from the day I first came here.*
*Even now, she's doing her best to cheer me up despite her own life and work. Even now, i'm just a charity case and a burden.*
*Then why...why does the thought of losing Makiko too hurt so much?*
*I open my mouth to respond.*
You:  "...Is it?"
*I say so quietly, i'm not sure that Makiko can even pick it up.*
*Without even looking up, I speak again.*
You:  "Do I...do I belong here Makiko?"
*Makiko looks away, her expression changing slightly.*
Makiko:  "What are you talking about? Of course you do."
*I continue to look away.*
Makiko:  "Do..do you not like it here?"
*I shake my head.*
You:  "It's fine. I just..."
*I look down at my hands as I close them and open them slowly.*
You:  "Would anybody really care if I left?"
*Makiko mumbles something I can't make out.*
*I turn to her.*
You:  "I'm sorry, I didn't catch that."
*There's a short silence.*
Makiko:  "I said um..could I ask you a favor?"
*Well, I certainly wasn’t expecting that.*
You:  "Oh uh, yeah sure Makiko, go ahead."
*This seems to lighten her spirits a bit.*
Makiko:  "I was wondering… I’d like to take you somewhere after work. Would that be all right?"
*I almost feel like making a ‘let me check my schedule’ joke and pull out a blank schedule, but I think better of it.*
You:  "Yeah, sure. I’m not doing anything."
*She turns slightly pink but it also seems to bring more life back into her face.*
Makiko:  "Great! Okay, let me just get my stuff and I’ll be right back!"
You:  "Sure, yeah. Go ahead, I’ll wait."
*With that, Makiko turns on her heels and heads out of the room.*
*As I watch her leave, a small smile passes over my lips.*
*If anything, thank god I still have Makiko to talk to.*
*For some reason, the thought of that causes my heart rate to increase slightly.*
*I don’t have to wait long as I can hear Makiko’s running footsteps from the hallway.*
*She turns into the office on a mid-run heel turn.*
*I open my eyes wide as I see this.*
Makiko:  "Sorry!"
*She says in a pant*
*I almost let out a laugh*
You:  "You didn’t have to run, Makiko. I was going to just sit here and wait."
*As if this thought never crossed her mind, her eyes open wider.*
Makiko:  "Oh! Right!"
Makiko:  "Sorry about that."
*I snicker.*
*Still as scatterbrained as ever.*
*Standing up, I hitch up my messenger bag.*
You:  "So, where are we going?"
*Makiko just places on a toothy grin on her face.*
Makiko:  "Nope. Not telling. You’ll know when we get there."
*I roll my eyes.*
You:  "All right, fine, surprise it is."
*As we leave the school, I have to say, the walk towards the mysterious location is actually a pretty quiet one.*
*Other than a congenial ‘how was your day’ and a few other general statements, we don’t really say much to each other, which is strange considering how much Makiko likes to chat.*
*We board a train and take it for about fifteen stops before Makiko signals for me to get off.*
*I certainly hope she plans on getting me home, because if she doesn’t…I’m going to be completely lost.*
*Upon stepping off the train, I turn to her.*
You:  "So…you plan on telling me yet?"
*Makiko shakes her head.*
Makiko:  "No need. We’re almost there."
*I force myself to hold back a sigh as we continue walking.*
*Suddenly, a bright light hits me square in the eye.*
*I turn to face it as I notice the sun is setting.*
*The tint of pink, orange, and yellow all meld into each other and become a very pretty sight.*
You:  "Oh, that’s a pretty sunset."
*Makiko barely gives it a glance.*
Makiko:  "I suppose."
*She answers half heartedly.*
*I glance back at her confused.*
You:  "You all right?"
*She looks away for a moment.*
Makiko:  "I’ve…just never liked sunsets."

Makiko:  "It’s silly."

Makiko:  "I just…sunsets always make me think of an ending. It’s like… you know that the end is coming, and you can’t stop it. "
Makiko:  "The end of a great day at the beach, the end of a day at the amusement park. Whenever the sun sets..you know that wonderful memory is almost over."

Makiko:  "Sorry, I told you it was silly."
*I really can’t think of anything to say in response.*
You:  "...Makiko."
*She shakes her head.*
Makiko:  "Hee hee. Got a bit serious there for a second. Come on, we’re almost there."
*Makiko picks up the pace slightly. I continue to walk at my current speed as I see her move past me.*
*She begins to get so far ahead, it’s almost as if she’s pulling away from me.*
*For some reason I start to feel a bit of panic inside of myself and move faster to catch her.*
*Luckily, she seems to come to a stop just as I catch up to her.*

*Makiko nods.*

*I smirk.*
You:  "Course I do. You beaned me in the head with a basketball."

Makiko:  "When I was little, I would always watch the Olympics with my parents. Every four years it became a tradition with us. "
Makiko:  "From the first time I watched it, I became enchanted. Other girls would see princess movies, but to me, the Olympics were my happily ever after."
Makiko:  "I knew one day I was going to make it there. It was never a doubt in my mind."
*Up to this point, her story had grown more and more hopeful, obviously great memories were passing through her mind.*
*Realizing how caught up in the moment she was, she pulled back.*
Makiko:  "Anyway, I thought that if I was going to become a famous Olympian athlete, I better learn some more languages, you know, for tours and marketing and stuff."

*I would say I was shocked, but I totally believe the ‘only’ part.*

You:  "So…what happened?"

*That was honestly not the response I was expecting.*
You:  "What?"
*I ask confused.*
*She turns her head to me, still resting her head in her arms.*
Makiko:  "Do you have a dream?"

Makiko:  "Did you ever have one?"

*She looks away from me without responding.*

*Makiko shrugs noncommittally.*
Makiko:  "I guess…I’ll probably settle down.. start a family."

*She shrugs again.*

You:  "Is that what you want?"

Makiko:  "It’s fine."

You:  "Makiko. You're one of the most amazing people I have ever met. Not only do you take the world in stride but you do it with a smile."

Makiko:  "But, you're right. I...I guess I just needed somebody to tell me that. Thank you."

*She clenches her fists tightly, her usual pumped expression on her face.*

*Makiko fights back a sniff.*

*Makiko freezes for a second, before clenching her fists slightly, her voice turning slightly cold.*
Makiko:  "Please. Please don't joke about my feelings like that."
*There's a slight pause.*
You:  "Wait. You..you think i'm joking?"
*Another pause.*
You:  "Makiko. I'd never joke about something like that."
You:  "The only thing I feel stupid about is that it took me this long to realize it."
*Slowly, tentatively, I walk up behind Makiko and place my hands on her shoulders.*
*Makiko jolts slightly, but doesn't fight my touch.*
You:  "When I first came here, I knew nobody, had nothing, but you welcomed me with open arms like I was part of the family."
You:  "I have to say, I was a little surprised at how friendly and excitable you were."
You:  "You care so much about everyone else's happiness that you never stop and think about your own."
