You:  "But..Makiko..I do. I love you."
*A painfully long silence fills the room.*
*Finally, a sniffle breaks it.*
Makiko:  "You know...I've been dropping really obvious hints for a while now."
Makiko:  "I thought they were so obvious that there was no other explanation that you just didn't think of me that way."
You:  "You've been dropping hints?"
*Makiko turns to face me.*
Makiko:  "Are you serious? The festival, water park, new year's? You didn't catch ANY of it?"
*I stop and think for a moment. And suddenly I feel like the world's biggest idiot.*
You:  "Oh."
*To this simple reaction, Makiko gives me a confused yet bemused face.*
Makiko:  "Yeah. Oh."
*Another moment of silence passes as Makiko leans over and slowly puts her arms around me.*
*I follow her lead and wrap my own arms around her.*
*Makiko sniffs.*
Makiko:  "You're a dork you know that?"
You:  "Yeah, I know that."
*The world freezes as the two of us hold each other for what seems like forever. As if no one else on Earth exists. Just the two of us.*
*Finally, Makiko speaks again.*
Makiko:  "Hey. Do you really love me?"
You:  "Yeah. I do."
Makiko:  "I do too."
*She squeezes me tighter.*
Makiko:  "This means you can't leave, you know?"
*I find myself smiling hearing her soft voice, almost like a child giving a command.*
You:  "Don't worry. I'm not going anywhere. I found my dream."
*And, as cheesy at it sounds, I know in my heart, I truly did.*
*-Three Years Later-*
*The roar of the crowd is overwhelming as I stand on the sidelines, cheering right along with them, my voice lost in the sea of cheers.*
*I turn to see Makiko standing on the 2nd place platform, her face beaming brighter than hardly anytime I’ve ever seen before.*
*The podium is nearly blinding from all of the photographs that are being taken, but Makiko doesn’t seem to even notice it as she keeps drinking in the moment.*
*After a few minutes, the cheering seems to die down and the athletes are allowed to step off the podiums.*
*Before I even have a chance to react, Makiko races over to me and leaps into my arms, embracing me in a huge hug.*
Makiko:  "I did it! I did it!"
You:  "You did! I’m so proud of you Makiko!"
*She pulls back from the hug and looks at me with a sly grin.*
Makiko:  "Now if I remember correctly, you said that if I placed at least silver, I’d get something."
You:  "What? Now?"
Makiko:  "Yep!"
*Smirking, I cup her head with my hands and give her a deep kiss. One she eagerly accepts and returns.*
*After a few moments, we release.*
Makiko:  "Ooh! If that’s the prize, I’m glad I didn’t get bronze!"
*She says, smiling at me.*
You:  "Welp, I guess that’s it then."
*Makiko shakes her head defiantly*
Makiko:  "Oh no no, this isn’t the end."
You:  "Wait, it’s not?"
Makiko:  "Of course it isn’t! Do you think we were taking those Tabogan classes for kicks?"
You:  "Uhhh"
Makiko:  "Come on! We have only two years to get ready for the winter games!"
*She reaches over and grabs my hand.*
*Moving ahead of me, she pulls on my arm.*
*So as not to fall over, I have to start running alongside her.*
*Ever since that day a few years ago, Makiko has been training her butt off to get to where she is today.*
*I ended up staying on at my job with the school. Well, that is, until my job found out that I was in a relationship with one of the teachers. Afterwards my contract was swiftly terminated.*
*We decided there was only one way to deal with that. And that was to get me a spousal visa.*
*I had never seen Makiko more nervous in my life than the day she had to wear a wedding dress. And I still torment her with the pictures.*
*I didn’t end up being a stay at home bum though as shortly after the loss of my job, I ended up finding another one as a ‘cultural liaison’ to an international sports company through one of Makiko’s old connections.*
*We decided that once we got a little too old for the Olympics, we’d simply take off. Go wherever we want,  A lifelong adventure with just the two of us.*
*If you had asked me five years ago if I could imagine myself in this position, I would have told you that you were absolutely crazy. But now, I wouldn’t change anything for the world.*
*I love Makiko, and I always will. She insists that I’m her rock, but honestly, I feel it’s the other way around.*
*Seeing me start to slow down, Makiko  looks back at me with a wry smile.*
Makiko:  "Hey slowpoke, you coming?"
*I look towards my wife and return the smile.*
You:  "Will you carry me?"
*She lets out a soft giggle.*
Makiko:  "If I have to."
*She pauses slightly as she turns back to me with her arm outstretched.*
Makiko:  "Because, no matter what, I'll never leave you behind."
You:  "Well, what a coincidence...because I feel the same...WAY!"
*I say this as i bend over, and in one fluid motion, pick Makiko up bridal style.*
Makiko:  "Ah! <USER>! What are you doing?!"
You:  "I'm simply carrying my princess."
You:  "Where to my lady?"
*Makiko blushes a deep, deep crimson before finally speaking as she points out towards the door.*
Makiko:  "To the next adventure!"
*I smirk as I look towards the door.*
You:  "I couldn't have put it better myself."
*And with that, the silver medal still dangling from my wife's neck, I race towards the door, as the two of us head to our next adventure together.*
* -The End-*
*The bus ride over to the shrine where I first met them is a quiet one. It’s hard to believe that it was a year ago today I met both Aina and Fumo.*
*It’s also hard to believe that in a few minutes they’ll be gone, and I’ll never see them again.*
*Sooner than I would like, the bus stops, and the three of us step off of the bus.*
*The three of us make our way through the forest. The same woods i walked through a year ago.*
*Eventually, we find ourselves in a clearing.*
*A sense of déjà vu envelopes me.*
*Turning my head around, I notice we’re at the place where I first met Aina. That same strange shrine.*
Fumo:  "Well, we're here."
*Fumo’s words break me out of my stupor.*
You:  "Right, I guess we are."
*Before any of us can say anything further, the doors on the tiny shrine shoot open with a loud crash.*
*All of our attentions turn to it.*
*Fumo looks back at me.*
Fumo:  "Well… it looks like it’s time."
*She raises her gaze to stare at me directly in the eyes.*
Fumo:  "<USER> you really are strange. From the day I met you I couldn’t really understand any of your motivations or what you really wanted."
Fumo:  "But, despite all of that, you welcomed us in and treated me as a friend. I want to thank you for that."
*Fumo bows before me.*
Fumo:  "So thank you."
*I feel a sad smile cross my face.*
You:  "Hey, I just did what anyone would have done."
*She returns at me with a small smirk.*
Fumo:  "No, you didn’t. And that’s why I’m thanking you."
*Turning to gaze at the portal, she looks back to me.*
Fumo:  "Good luck with your life <USER>. I mean that."
*She says, showing me a slight smile. Before I can say anything in response, she turns away and walks into the portal.*
*Fumo is enveloped in a bright light, and just like that, she’s gone.*
*Before I even have a chance to think, Aina hops over to me.*
Aina:  "<USER>. I am…very sorry"
*She says in her best English, each word seeming to be extremely hard for her.*
Aina:  "But, I thank you for everything."
*She takes my hand in hers and smiles.*
Aina:  "You treat Aina like person. You make Aina very happy."
*Despite the craziness she put me through, I can’t help but smile at the heartfelt gratitude.*
You:  "Dou Itashimashite Aina. Anata wa…tomodachi desu. (You're welcome Aina. You're my friend.)"
*Even though the Japanese wasn’t very good at all, I could see Aina’s face burst into a smile, tears beginning to well in her eyes.*
Aina:  "Arigatou…<USER>-sama"
*She wipes her eyes.*
Aina:  "Jya, Sayonara, <USER>-sama"
*And with that, she releases my hand and walks towards the portal. I struggle to look at her, but the light is too bright, and, in a few moments, she’s gone.*
*And, just like that. I'm alone.*
*I glance around one more time, as if one of them would pop out at any moment, but of course, this doesn't happen.*
*So, with that, I place my hands in my pockets, and make my way back towards the bus station.*
*Gaikokujin No Sensei*
*I can’t believe how quiet my apartment has become. Fumo and Aina have only been gone for a day and already i'm going stir crazy*
*This is ridiculous. I lived for years on my own. Why is this bothering me just all of a sudden?*
*I lay back on my bed and stare up at the ceiling.*
You:  "What am I even doing here?"
*I ask to no one in particular.*
*My eyes move towards the chair in the corner. Less than a week ago, Fumo was sitting in that same chair, flipping through one of the books she bought.*
*I have to turn my head away as the image becomes too vivid.*
*It wasn’t that I had loved either Aina or Fumo, well, not in that way, it had just become so normal to me. So…expected.*
*It was like living with roommates who eventually became like sisters. Without them here, it really made me realize just how lonely this place could be.*
*I lay back and close my eyes. As I do, my mind wanders. Eventually, for some reason, it ends up settling on the events of New Years Day.*
*Yuriko’s face flashes in my mind before the entire day plays in my head. Meeting her at the cemetery, the incident at the restaurant, and her true feelings on kids.*
*All of it passes in front of my eyes as clearly as the day it happened.*
*The movie in my head is interrupted by my phone ringing loudly, nearly causing me to jump out of my skin in shock.*
*My heart beating like a drum, I pick up the phone and put it to my ear.*
You:  "Uh, hello?"
Yuriko:  "Oh, sorry, did I catch you at a bad time?"
*I instantly recognize the voice as Yuriko.*
You:  "Huh? Oh, no, no Yuriko. I was just laying in bed."
*There’s a soft giggle on the other line.*
Yuriko:  "Bed? It’s two."
You:  "Haha, no I wasn’t waking up. I was just thinking."
Yuriko:  "Ah. ..anything I can help with?"
You:  "Hm? Oh. No. My friends just had to move and I was just thinking about that."
*There’s a slight pause.*
Yuriko:  "I see. I’m sorry to hear that."
You:  "No, it’s all right. What can I do for you Yuriko?"
Yuriko:  "Ah. Right. Well another year just started. I wanted to check in and see how you were doing."
You:  "Oh! Well...thank you."
*Another silence*
Yuriko:  "Is everything all right?"
*That was a good question. WAS I all right? I mean the easy answer would have been yes but..*
You:  "Actually, I-"
*Before I can answer though, Yuriko cuts me off.*
Yuriko:  "Oh no, I’m sorry, but I have another call coming in. But I definitely want to continue this conversation. "
Yuriko:  "Let’s continue this in person though. Is an hour okay over at the park? I’ll send you directions. Thank you <USER>. I’ll talk to you soon."
*Before I can even get a word in edgewise, the call disconnects.*
You:  "Wow…didn’t even give me a CHANCE to decline there."
*I put the phone back in my pocket.*
You:  "Well, it’s not like I’m doing anything anyway."
*Slowly, I gather my clothes and begin the process of getting ready.*
*Gaikokujin No Sensei*
*I glance at my phone to make sure that I’ve reached the correct location. *
*As I look around, I see a large sign which luckily has the word 'park' in English*
You:  "Okay, this seems to be the place."
*I take a few steps into the park and begin to look around.*
*What I see shocks me. All around me I see row after row of cherry blossom trees, and to my surprise, they all seem to be in bloom, their blossoms falling to the ground, some being carried by the wind.*
*I pull myself away from the sight and begin to look around for Yuriko. Not seeing her, I walk further inward.*
*I glance at my phone for the time. Yeah, its been an hour, I’m definitely on time.*
*After meandering around for a while I can’t seem to find any sign of Yuriko.*
*I sigh loudly as I turn around again, thinking I must have missed something.*
*It’s then that I hear rapidly approaching footsteps, the familiar clack of high heels on the pavement give away immediately who it is.*
*I turn back to see Yuriko walking at a brisk pace, finally catching up to me.*
Yuriko:  "Oh, I am so sorry. That phone call went on for longer than expected."
*I put my hands up.*
You:  "Haha, it’s fine, it’s fine. You didn’t have to run here. Especially in high heels."
Yuriko:  "Well I’m the one who called you. It’s very rude of me to arrive late."
*I glance at her shoes.*
You:  "I always found those strange. Does it hurt to walk in those?"
*Surprised at my question, Yuriko looks surprised for a moment before giggling.*
Yuriko:  "I’m used to it."
*She looks to the side.*
Yuriko:  "Besides, you’ve..seen how tall I am without them."
*She of course Is referring to the time I saw her at the pool park. I had nearly forgotten how short she was. Without her heels she barely hit the five foot mark.*
*I shrug*
You:  "It doesn’t really bother me."
Yuriko:  "R-right. Either way, I figured it would be a lot easier to get this done if I came to you. I saw you signed on for another year and I sent some more paperwork, and also wanted to see how you were doing."
*She begins to flip through a folder she’s holding.*
You:  "Actually, i'm glad you're here. I wanted to talk to you about that as well."
*Yuriko continues to look through her papers.*
Yuriko:  "Certainly, what can I help you with?"
You:  "Well, I was wondering. Um... What would happen if I were to say... want to.. leave early?"
*Hearing this, Yuriko stops.*
Yuriko:  "You mean break your new contract?"
*I don't give a response.*
Yuriko:  "Would there be any reason for that? Did something happen?"
*I scratch my head.*
You:  "Well, I mean, not necessarily, it's just.."
*I let myself trail off.*
*Ugh, why is it so hard to get words out?*
You:  "Teaching is interesting and all, but….i’m just not sure if I’m cut out for it."
*Yuriko shakes her head and gestures towards me with her hand.*
Yuriko:  "Don’t be silly. You’re a wonderful teacher. All your reviews have been top marks."
You:  "They have?"
*Yuriko nods emphatically.*
Yuriko:  "Oh yes, you’ve been one of our best teachers this year."
*Huh. Well I’ll be damned.*
You:  "Well, it’s great to hear that but…I don’t know. I just…I’m not sure if it’s for me."
*There’s a few moments of silence as Yuriko slides the paper back into her folder.*
Yuriko:  "Oh… I see. Well, we’re um..we’re sorry to hear that. Of course it’s entirely your choice."
Yuriko:  "We can't FORCE you to stay here obviously, but.. it wouldn't look very good on your end. And you certainly wouldn't be able to work for us again."
*I nod slowly.*
You:  "Y..yeah well that makes sense."
*Despite doing her best to hide it, I can tell that my decision is not what she wanted to hear.*
*This is for the best though, right? Still, If that’s the case, why is disappointing Yuriko leaving more than a little bit of a bad taste in my mouth?*
Yuriko:  "While we’re on topic, can you think of anything we might be able to do to help to get you to stay?"
*I stop and think for a moment.*
You:  "Well, honestly, it’s not so much the job that I’m unsure about. It’s more the..i suppose….it’s hard to describe. I just…don’t see a reason I NEED to be here."
Yuriko:  "I..i see."
*She pauses for a moment, her hand to her chin, and then looks back to me.*
Yuriko:  "So…if there were a reason, you’d stay?"
You:  "I suppose you could say that."
Yuriko:  "Well, you’re an important member of this company. In fact, with your skill you could easily be in line for a promotion in a year or so."
You:  "While I do appreciate your confidence in me, I really do. That isn’t exactly what I mean."
*Yuriko looks at me, as if confused.*
Yuriko:  "Then what DO you mean?"
*I scratch my head*
*That's a good question. what DID I mean? Why was this so difficult? This should be easy. I've quit jobs before. Why was it so difficult to say this to Yuriko?*
You:  "I…I’m not sure. I just….I don’t think it’s going to work."
Yuriko:  "Well, if you’re not sure, then you should give it another year! See if it gets better!"
*Her voice starts to lose its cool edge a bit as it becomes more frantic.*
*Yuriko says this in a much louder voice than I expect, causing me to shirk back a bit.*
*An annoyed look crosses my face.*
*Realizing her own volume, Yuriko’s eyes slightly widen as she then tries to recover herself.*
*Why is she making this so hard? This should be as simple as a 'oh, okay, well sorry it didn’t work out' *
You:  "Why? Why do you care so much?"
*This seems to take Yuriko slightly aback as she takes a moment to readjust herself.*
Yuriko:  "B..it’s because we don’t want to see such a talented member of our staff go."
You:  "I’m sure you’ll find someone just as talented. Probably way moreso to take my place."
Yuriko:  "THAT’S NOT TRUE!"
*While both of us are shocked at the sudden outburst, I think Yuriko's even more shocked than me.*
Yuriko:  "S-sorry."
*She says, regaining her calm demeanor, although I can see the redness of her face clearly.*
Yuriko:  "I..I apologize. I don't know what came over me."
*She goes quiet for a moment, as if unsure what to say next.*
*Why is this so difficult? What am I expecting her to say?*
*What magical words do I think that will come out her mouth that will make everything okay and make me want to stay?*
*Why am I putting off simply telling her 'no'?*
*I take a deep breath.*
You:  "If it's not true, then what is it?"
*This seems to give Yuriko pause, as she seems at a loss for words.*
*After a few moments she composes herself and adjusts her glasses.*
Yuriko:  "What...What I meant was, that’s not true. You are a valuable asset to the company."
*Ah. An asset. Right. Just like I was at my last company. an 'Asset'. We all know how that turned out.*
*I'm not sure why, but the word 'asset' hurts my chest more than it should.*
*It's to the point that I almost say something I regret.*
*Luckily i'm able to hold myself back as I take another deep breath.*
*I feel my face adjust to one of cold indifference as I hold out my hand.*
You:  "I’ll take that paper now."
*I say, in a drastically colder tone. The lack of any warmth surprises even me.*
*This reaction obviously catches Yuriko off guard as she fumbles for a moment.*
Yuriko:  "….Oh. Oh..yes of course"
*Slowly, very slowly she reaches for the paper and hands it to me.*
*As soon as its in hand range I take it from her.*
You:  "Thank you Furikawa-san."
*I say as I give her a slight bow. My words coming out far colder than even I mean to.*
*Slowly I turn around and begin to walk.*
Yuriko:  "Just so you know, you can opt to reconsider up until that paperwork is finalized!"
*I hear her voice behind me as I keep walking.*
*Her sound begins to become more and more distant as I walk further.*
Yuriko:  "You-"
*But at this point, I can no longer hear her as her voice becomes lost to the distance.*
*I don’t bother turn around as I’m not sure if my shaking eyes would be visible*
*Having walked a good hundred feet, I slow down a bit and take a deep breath.*
*I look down at the paper in my hand.*
You:  "Am I making the right decision?"
*Suddenly, behind me, I can hear the rapid sounding approach of feet slapping against the pavement.*
*Ah. Probably a jogger. I’ll just step out of the way.*
*As I walk, I gently move a few feet to the side, allowing them to pass through.*
*Soon I can hear panting. It gets louder and louder, until finally, to my surprise, I feel a pair of arms wrap around me from behind*
*I look down and I recognized the hands and sleeves of the owner immediately.*
*I can feel Yuriko’s heart beat quickly against my back, her chest heaving up and down from her panting.*
You:  "Y..yuriko?"
*I ask, confusion evident in my voice.*
*Yuriko continues to pant, as I can hear her attempt to get words out.*
Yuriko:  "Please…don’t leave."
*I freeze up at this.*
*She grabs onto me tighter. Her hands barely make their way around my torso, but she holds onto me as if I were a life preserver.*
*I wait a few moments for her to say something, but only silence follows as she allows herself to catch her breath.*
Yuriko:  "Not…not yet. Not until I say this."
*I try to move to look at her, but she holds on tight.*
Yuriko:  "No. Don’t turn around. I need to say this, and I don’t think I can say it if you look at me."
*I stop moving.*
Yuriko:  "I know who I am…YOU know who I am. You’re probably the ONLY person to know the real, TRUE me. "
Yuriko:  "The Yuriko the world sees… that’s exactly what I want them to see. But you…you know me for who I am."
Yuriko:  "I love who I am. I don’t care if it’s wrong. I don’t CARE if the world won’t accept me for it and I have to hide it."
Yuriko:  "But when I’m around you, all my shields go down. And I could never explain why."
Yuriko:  "And then you walked away. And…and it hurt so much."
Yuriko:  "I hate this. I hate how you make me feel. Normally I’m so in control. I’m so…sure of myself. But around you.. nothing makes sense."
Yuriko:  "I’m sorry. I don’t want you to stay because you’re an asset to the company. I.. I don’t care about that."
Yuriko:  "I, Yuriko, want you to stay…with me."
*She takes a deep breath.*
Yuriko:  "…<USER>"
Yuriko:  "I love you."
*My whole body freezes in shock as I hear these words.*
*A silence fills the air, which lasts for a good ten seconds, as I’m left completely speechless, unsure of what to say.*
You:  "Yuriko..I…"
Yuriko:  "I’m sorry. I know I’m being selfish. I know I deserve to be alone. I just…I had to tell you…before you left."
*As I begin to speak again, I feel her shake her head on my back.*
Yuriko:  "It’s all right. I know what you’re going to say. And that’s okay."
Yuriko:  "I’ve chosen my path, and I’m happy enough with that. I can’t expect someone else to live the life I’ve chosen."
Yuriko:  "I know you’re going back to America. I understand you’re never coming back. And….i’m just happy I got to meet you. And…that I got to experience what failing in love was like."
*Slowly, I feel Yuriko’s hands slide off of me.*
Yuriko:  "I wish you all the best."
*I no longer can feel Yuriko against me, but her voice is still directly behind me.*
Yuriko:  "I’m sorry but…I can’t look at you now. Please just….walk away if you would?"
*After listening to Yuriko pour her heart out to me, I could almost feel a large part of it break myself. *
*I never in a million years would have imagined she felt that way about me.*
*And at the same time, I felt like smacking myself.*
*THAT was the reason I was looking for. But i didn't even know that was case until right now.*
*It was just that my stupid brain couldn't put it into words.*
*I was in love with Yuriko.*
*And it took her saying it for me realize it.*
*As I turn around, I see Yuriko with her back to me, shaking ever so slightly.*
*Thinking back of all the times I spent with Yuriko, all the conversations we had and all the moments we shared, there’s only one response I could possibly give.*
*Slowly, I wrap my arms around her from behind.*
*I feel her jolt in my grasp*
*As I look down, I notice for the first time that she doesn’t appear to have any shoes on. *
*She must have thrown off her high heels to run after me. My chest aches a little thinking of it as I hold her tighter.*
You:  "Yuriko. If you’re going to say your piece, then I get to too. "
You:  "Without you, I would never have come to Japan. It’s because of you that I’m even here."
You:  "And I’m so glad I met you."
You:  "You were everything I hoped to be. Driven, independent, you had a strong purpose in your life, and you were always climbing higher."
You:  "In fact, I was more than a little intimidated by you."
*I let out a small chuckle.*
You:  "You were this unattainable goal that I could never measure up to, and, considering you’re even younger than me, it was even more demoralizing."
You:  "Then, over time, I started to learn about the real you. The REAL Yuriko. And, it didn’t make me upset or scare me off. In fact, I was relieved."
Yuriko:  "...relieved?"
*She asks confused.*
You:  "Yes. I saw that you weren’t this caricature of perfection, but you were a real living, breathing, person. And…"
*I pause for just a moment.*
You:  "That’s the Yuriko I fell in love with."
*I hear a undistinguishable noise come from Yuriko followed by the sound of what I assume to be her attempting to hold back tears.*
You:  "You wanted to know a reason I’d stay here?"
You:  "You."
*With that, it seems like any remaining piece of Yuriko’s mask shatters as her hand goes to cover her mouth as a sobbing sound begins to emanate from her.*
*Even though her hands try desperately to stop them, I can feel several warm tears fall onto my arms.*
*As Yuriko continues to sob, I say nothing as I merely hold her from behind, squeezing her tight with every sob.*
*{cps=15}And honestly, for as long as she'll have me, I don’t plan on ever letting her go.{/cps}*
*(Two Years Later)*
Yuriko:  "<USER>! Do you have the paperwork?"
You:  "Of course I do. When have I ever NOT had the paperwork?"
*I ask my wife jokingly.*
Yuriko:  "Right, you’re right."
*Yuriko seems to be more than a little frazzled at the upcoming meeting. Not that I can exactly blame her.*
*This meeting is going to determine whether or not she becomes acting vice-president of the entirety of Engu-step.*
*And honestly, if it were my meeting, I’d be more than a little nervous too. But of course, I can’t let her know that.*
*After that day two years ago, things have been moving very fast. A few months after that, she had gotten a promotion that now has her in the running for the VP spot.*
*As for me, it turns out she wasn’t just blowing smoke. I actually WAS good enough to get a promotion within another year. Which, with a bit of a push from Yuriko, I took.*
*Funnily enough, Yuriko had actually beaten me to a proposal. *
*About a year ago i was planning on popping the question, but before I could, she asked me if 'I wished to make our partnership permanent.'*
*I of course said yes, and well, here we are.*
*Once we became a couple, I saw a lot more of the Yuriko that she never lets people see, and honestly, she’s even more adorable.*
*She has nerves just like everyone else, and she’s actually a little messy. But that really only adds to her charm. As it is right now, she has a large errant hair out of place.*
You:  "Yuriko."
*I say to my nervous wife as I grab her by the shoulders.*
*I lean in and give her a kiss. While shocked at first, she soon accepts it, as she reaches up and puts her hands on my face*
*Our kiss soon ends and, backing my head up, I look down at her.*
*I move the swatch of hair behind her ear.*
You:  "You’re going to do great. You always do great. Don’t worry so much."
Yuriko:  "You’re right. Of course you’re right."
*Carefully, she puts on her high heels, and upon standing back up bumps up several inches.*
*Grabbing her folder in one hand she adjusts her glasses with the other. The sign that she’s fully back in mask mode.*
Yuriko:  "All right. Are you ready?"
You:  "I’ve been ready for thirty minutes."
Yuriko:  "Yes, well, good preparation takes time."
*She adjusts her glasses again.*
You:  "You know you’re really cute when you fidget."
*As if realizing what she’s doing, she moves her hand away from her glasses.*
*As I look at my wife, I realize just what sort of a crazy ride this has been. *
*I traveled across the world, met all sorts of strange people, including a pair of spirits, but there, from the very beginning, was Yuriko.*
*She had been the one constant in my life from when my life was at its lowest point, and, although she didn’t know it at the time, she pulled me from the depths of despair.*
*I don’t think she’ll ever realize just how much she means to me for doing that. And I don’t think she’ll ever realize just how much I love her.*
*I know that for the rest of my life there will be no rest. I have no delusions of eventual retirement, spending my final years on a beach somewhere.*
*Yuriko will always be looking for the next step up. The next obstacle to overcome, and I will always be there for her. Her partner, her husband, but most importantly, her friend.*
*The friend that truly understands and accepts her for who she is.*
*As the door opens in front of me and the bright light from outside momentarily blinds me, Yuriko cradles her folder in one hand and outstretches out to me with the other.*
*As I look over to her, I see a small smile cross her face*
Yuriko:  "Come husband, we have a world to take on."
*I smirk back as I grab her hand in mine.*
You:  "We certainly do."
*Carefully, Yuriko slips her delicate fingers into mine.*
*As she does, I latch onto them tightly, knowing that no matter what happens, I’m never letting her go.*
*The End*
*The bus ride over to the shrine where I first met them is a quiet one. It’s hard to believe that it was a year ago today I met both Aina and Fumo.*
*It’s also hard to believe that in a few minutes they’ll be gone, and I’ll never see them again.*
*Sooner than I would like, the bus stops, and the three of us step off of the bus.*
*The three of us make our way through the forest. The same woods i walked through a year ago.*
*Eventually, we find ourselves in a clearing.*
*A sense of déjà vu envelopes me.*
*Turning my head around, I notice we’re at the place where I first met Aina. That same strange shrine.*
Fumo:  "Well, we're here."
*Fumo’s words break me out of my stupor.*
You:  "Right, I guess we are."
*Before any of us can say anything further, the doors on the tiny shrine shoot open with a loud crash.*
*All of our attentions turn to it.*
*Fumo looks back at me.*
Fumo:  "Well… it looks like it’s time."
*She raises her gaze to stare at me directly in the eyes.*
Fumo:  "<USER> you really are strange. From the day I met you I couldn’t really understand any of your motivations or what you really wanted."
Fumo:  "But, despite all of that, you welcomed us in and treated me as a friend. I want to thank you for that."
*Fumo bows before me.*
Fumo:  "So thank you."
*I feel a sad smile cross my face.*
You:  "Hey, I just did what anyone would have done."
*She returns at me with a small smirk.*
Fumo:  "No, you didn’t. And that’s why I’m thanking you."
*Turning to gaze at the portal, she looks back to me.*
Fumo:  "Good luck with your life <USER>. I mean that."
*She says, showing me a slight smile. Before I can say anything in response, she turns away and walks into the portal.*
*Fumo is enveloped in a bright light, and just like that, she’s gone.*
*Before I even have a chance to think, Aina hops over to me.*
Aina:  "<USER>. I am…very sorry"
*She says in her best English, each word seeming to be extremely hard for her.*
Aina:  "But, I thank you for everything."
*She takes my hand in hers and smiles.*
Aina:  "You treat Aina like person. You make Aina very happy."
*Despite the craziness she put me through, I can’t help but smile at the heartfelt gratitude.*
You:  "Dou Itashimashite Aina. Anata wa…tomodachi desu. (You're welcome Aina. You're my friend.)"
*Even though the Japanese wasn’t very good at all, I could see Aina’s face burst into a smile, tears beginning to well in her eyes.*
Aina:  "Arigatou…<USER>-sama"
*She wipes her eyes.*
Aina:  "Jya, Sayonara, <USER>-sama"
*And with that, she releases my hand and walks towards the portal. I struggle to look at her, but the light is too bright, and, in a few moments, she’s gone.*
*And, just like that. I'm alone.*
*I glance around one more time, as if one of them would pop out at any moment, but of course, this doesn't happen.*
*So, with that, I place my hands in my pockets, and make my way back towards the bus station.*
*Gaikokujin No Sensei*
*And so, here I am.*
*Fumo and Aina have left, and yet, my world has moved on.*
*Since I had already signed on for another year, I decided to stay.*
*And honestly, I'm glad I did.*
*Nothing as fantastical as before occured, but then again, I didn't expect it to.*
*And in its own way, that's okay.*
*I'm still here, not knowing what I want to do with my life.*
*But that's okay too.*
*Because i'm not worrying about tomorrow.*
*I'm living for today.*
*And in the end, isn't that all any of us can really do?*
*The End*
